```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- § IT THE TABLES, THEIR CONSTRAINTS, GRANTS AND POLICIES (as postgres)
-- ===========================================================================
select inv.val('IT1 the four invoice tables exist', $$select count(*)::text from pg_class where relnamespace = 'public'::regnamespace and relkind = 'r' and relname in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends')$$, '4');
select inv.val('IT2 RLS is enabled AND forced on all four', $$select string_agg(relname || '=' || relrowsecurity || '/' || relforcerowsecurity, ',' order by relname) from pg_class where relnamespace = 'public'::regnamespace and relname in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends')$$, 'invoice_lines=true/true,invoice_sends=true/true,invoice_settings=true/true,invoices=true/true');
select inv.val('IT3 authenticated holds NO write privilege on any of the four (24 checks, 0 held)', $$select count(*)::text || ' checks, ' || count(*) filter (where has_table_privilege('authenticated', t, p))::text || ' held' from unnest(array['public.invoice_settings','public.invoices','public.invoice_lines','public.invoice_sends']) t, unnest(array['INSERT','UPDATE','DELETE','TRUNCATE','REFERENCES','TRIGGER']) p$$, '24 checks, 0 held');
select inv.val('IT4 anon and service_role hold NOTHING on any of the four, table- or column-level (decision 13: the freeze is proved as postgres)', $$select (count(*) filter (where has_table_privilege(r, t, 'SELECT,INSERT,UPDATE,DELETE,TRUNCATE,REFERENCES,TRIGGER')))::text || '/' || (select count(*) from information_schema.column_privileges where table_schema = 'public' and table_name in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends') and grantee in ('anon','service_role','PUBLIC'))::text from unnest(array['anon','service_role']) r, unnest(array['public.invoice_settings','public.invoices','public.invoice_lines','public.invoice_sends']) t$$, '0/0');
select inv.val('IT5 POSITIVE CONTROL: authenticated holds SELECT on all four (the read path § IR exercises)', $$select count(*) filter (where has_table_privilege('authenticated', t, 'SELECT'))::text from unnest(array['public.invoice_settings','public.invoices','public.invoice_lines','public.invoice_sends']) t$$, '4');
select inv.val('IT6 each table carries exactly one policy, a SELECT', $$select string_agg(c.relname || '=' || p.polcmd, ',' order by c.relname) from pg_policy p join pg_class c on c.oid = p.polrelid where c.relnamespace = 'public'::regnamespace and c.relname in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends')$$, 'invoice_lines=r,invoice_sends=r,invoice_settings=r,invoices=r');
select inv.val('IT7 a status outside the four is refused', format($$insert into public.invoices (organisation_id, created_by, status) values (%L, %L, 'sent') returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'fin_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT8 a draft naming BOTH a company and a contact is refused', format($$insert into public.invoices (organisation_id, created_by, recipient_company_id, recipient_contact_id) values (%L, %L, %L, %L) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'fin_a', :'com_a', :'con_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT9 a currency other than GBP is refused', format($$insert into public.invoices (organisation_id, created_by, currency) values (%L, %L, 'EUR') returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'fin_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT10 an invoice naming ANOTHER workspace''s company is refused by the composite FK', format($$insert into public.invoices (organisation_id, created_by, recipient_company_id) values (%L, %L, %L) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'fin_a', :'com_b'), 'denied(23503)');
select inv.val('IT11 a line on ANOTHER workspace''s invoice is refused by the composite FK', format($$insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values (%L, %L, 9, 'x', 1, 1, 'none', 1, 0) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_bd'), 'denied(23503)');
select inv.val('IT12 a line with quantity 0 is refused', format($$insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values (%L, %L, 9, 'x', 0, 1, 'none', 0, 0) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ad'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT13 a line with a VAT rate outside the five is refused', format($$insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values (%L, %L, 9, 'x', 1, 1, 'standard', 1, 0) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ad'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT14 an empty line description is refused', format($$insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values (%L, %L, 9, '', 1, 1, 'none', 1, 0) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ad'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT15 gross must equal net plus VAT', format($$update public.invoices set gross_minor = gross_minor + 1 where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT16 the number is constrained to the shape a filename can carry', $$select (pg_get_constraintdef(oid) like '%[A-Z0-9-]{0,12}[0-9]{4,10}%')::text from pg_constraint where conname = 'invoices_number_check'$$, 'true');
select inv.val('IT17 two invoices in one workspace cannot share a number', $$select pg_get_constraintdef(oid) from pg_constraint where conname = 'invoices_number_unique'$$, 'UNIQUE (organisation_id, number)');
select inv.val('IT18 a sort code that is not six digits is refused', format($$update public.invoice_settings set bank_sort_code = '12-34-56' where organisation_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT19 a number prefix outside A-Z, 0-9 and hyphen is refused', format($$update public.invoice_settings set number_prefix = 'inv/' where organisation_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT20 bank details are all three or none', format($$update public.invoice_settings set bank_sort_code = null where organisation_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT21 a VAT number outside the UK formats is refused', format($$update public.invoice_settings set vat_number = 'FR12345678901' where organisation_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT22 a send to something that is not an email address is refused', format($$insert into public.invoice_sends (organisation_id, invoice_id, to_email, sent_by) values (%L, %L, 'not-an-email', %L) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ai', :'fin_a'), 'denied(23514)');
select inv.val('IT23 an issued row without a recipient address is refused by the issued shape', format($$update public.invoices set recipient_address = null where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IA THE ARITHMETIC AND THE NUMBERING (as postgres)
-- ===========================================================================
create table inv.cases (qty text, unit bigint, rate text, net bigint, vat bigint);
-- ARITHMETIC CASES BEGIN (bound to tests/unit/invoices/arithmetic-cases.ts by arithmetic-cases-match-sql.test.ts)
insert into inv.cases values
  ('1.00', 10000, 'standard_20', 10000, 2000),
  ('2.50', 333, 'standard_20', 833, 167),
  ('0.01', 1, 'standard_20', 0, 0),
  ('0.50', 1, 'standard_20', 1, 0),
  ('3.00', 1999, 'reduced_5', 5997, 300),
  ('1.00', 250, 'standard_20', 250, 50),
  ('1.00', 2, 'reduced_5', 2, 0),
  ('1.00', 10, 'reduced_5', 10, 1),
  ('7.25', 100, 'zero', 725, 0),
  ('1.00', 12345, 'exempt', 12345, 0),
  ('1.00', 12345, 'none', 12345, 0),
  ('12.34', 5678, 'standard_20', 70067, 14013);
-- ARITHMETIC CASES END
select inv.val('IA1 private.invoice_line_amounts matches every row of the shared case table', $$select count(*)::text || ' cases, ' || count(*) filter (where (a.net_minor, a.vat_minor) is distinct from (c.net, c.vat))::text || ' wrong' from inv.cases c cross join lateral private.invoice_line_amounts(c.qty::numeric(12,2), c.unit, c.rate) a$$, '12 cases, 0 wrong');
select inv.val('IA2 numbers pad to four digits and never truncate (decision 11)', $$select private.format_invoice_number('INV-', 1) || ',' || private.format_invoice_number('', 42) || ',' || private.format_invoice_number('INV-', 9999) || ',' || private.format_invoice_number('INV-', 12345)$$, 'INV-0001,0042,INV-9999,INV-12345');
select inv.val('IA3 the snapshot of the issued fixture carries seller VAT, bank, recipient, one line and its totals', format($$select concat_ws('|', snapshot ->> 'number', snapshot -> 'seller' ->> 'vat_number', snapshot -> 'seller' -> 'bank' ->> 'sort_code', snapshot -> 'recipient' ->> 'kind', snapshot -> 'recipient' ->> 'name', jsonb_array_length(snapshot -> 'lines'), snapshot -> 'lines' -> 0 ->> 'quantity', snapshot -> 'totals' ->> 'gross_minor') from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'inv_ai'), 'FIX-0001|GB123456789|123456|company|Alpha Customer Ltd|1|1.00|144000');
select inv.val('IA4 no client role can execute the three private helpers', $$select count(*) filter (where has_function_privilege(r, f, 'EXECUTE'))::text from unnest(array['anon','authenticated','service_role']) r, unnest(array['private.invoice_line_amounts(numeric,bigint,text)','private.format_invoice_number(text,integer)','private.build_invoice_snapshot(uuid,text,date,date,date)']) f$$, '0');

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IR WHO READS WHAT. One string per persona: invoices, lines, settings and
-- sends, each as A/B/S counts.
-- ===========================================================================
select format($$select 'inv:' || (select count(*) from public.invoices where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoices where organisation_id = %2$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoices where organisation_id = %3$L) || ' lines:' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_lines where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_lines where organisation_id = %2$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_lines where organisation_id = %3$L) || ' set:' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %2$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %3$L) || ' sends:' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_sends where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_sends where organisation_id = %2$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_sends where organisation_id = %3$L)$$, :'org_a', :'org_b', :'org_s') as vis \gset
select inv.val('IR0 FIXTURE: as postgres every planted row is there', :'vis', 'inv:2/1/1 lines:2/1/1 set:1/1/1 sends:1/0/0');
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'own_a_c';
select inv.val('IR1 A''s Owner reads A''s invoices, lines, settings and sends, and nothing of B or S', :'vis', 'inv:2/0/0 lines:2/0/0 set:1/0/0 sends:1/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'adm_a_c';
select inv.val('IR2 A''s Admin reads the same', :'vis', 'inv:2/0/0 lines:2/0/0 set:1/0/0 sends:1/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';
select inv.val('IR3 A''s Finance user reads the same', :'vis', 'inv:2/0/0 lines:2/0/0 set:1/0/0 sends:1/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'mgr_a_c';
select inv.val('IR4 A''s Manager (no view_finance) reads nothing', :'vis', 'inv:0/0/0 lines:0/0/0 set:0/0/0 sends:0/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'ro_a_c';
select inv.val('IR5 A''s Read Only reads nothing', :'vis', 'inv:0/0/0 lines:0/0/0 set:0/0/0 sends:0/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c1';
select inv.val('IR6 MFA: A''s Finance user at aal1 reads nothing (view_finance is aal2-gated)', :'vis', 'inv:0/0/0 lines:0/0/0 set:0/0/0 sends:0/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_b_c';
select inv.val('IR7 B''s Finance user reads B and never A', :'vis', 'inv:0/1/0 lines:0/1/0 set:0/1/0 sends:0/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_s_c';
select inv.val('IR8 DOWNGRADE HIDES NOTHING: a Starter workspace''s Finance user still reads its invoices', :'vis', 'inv:0/0/1 lines:0/0/1 set:0/0/1 sends:0/0/0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_f_c';
select inv.val('IR9 a Free workspace''s Finance user reads nothing of anybody else''s', :'vis', 'inv:0/0/0 lines:0/0/0 set:0/0/0 sends:0/0/0');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IB BREAKOUT: A's Finance user (aal2, the strongest writer there is)
-- forges a write on every table.
-- ===========================================================================
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';
select inv.val('IB1 a forged INSERT on invoices is refused by privilege', format($$insert into public.invoices (organisation_id, created_by) values (%L, %L) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'fin_a'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB2 a forged UPDATE on a DRAFT invoice is refused by privilege', format($$update public.invoices set notes = 'forged' where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB3 a forged DELETE on invoices is refused by privilege', format($$delete from public.invoices where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB4 a forged INSERT on invoice_lines is refused by privilege', format($$insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values (%L, %L, 9, 'x', 1, 1, 'none', 1, 0) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB5 a forged UPDATE on invoice_lines is refused by privilege', format($$update public.invoice_lines set unit_price_minor = 1 where invoice_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB6 a forged UPDATE of next_number is refused by privilege', format($$update public.invoice_settings set next_number = 1 where organisation_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB7 a forged INSERT on invoice_sends is refused by privilege', format($$insert into public.invoice_sends (organisation_id, invoice_id, to_email, sent_by) values (%L, %L, 'x@example.test', %L) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ai', :'fin_a'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
select inv.val('IB8 a forged DELETE on invoice_sends is refused by privilege', format($$delete from public.invoice_sends where invoice_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:missing_grant)');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IF THE FREEZE, as postgres -- the table owner, past RLS (decision 13).
-- ===========================================================================
select inv.val('IF1 an issued invoice''s totals cannot change', format($$update public.invoices set net_minor = net_minor + 1, gross_minor = gross_minor + 1 where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF2 ...nor its snapshot', format($$update public.invoices set snapshot = '{"version":1}'::jsonb where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF3 ...nor its number', format($$update public.invoices set number = 'FIX-0002' where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF4 ...nor its recipient', format($$update public.invoices set recipient_company_id = null, recipient_contact_id = %L where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'con_a', :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF5 an issued invoice cannot go back to draft', format($$update public.invoices set status = 'draft', number = null, snapshot = null, issue_date = null, due_date = null where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_transition_not_allowed)');
select inv.val('IF6 a draft cannot jump straight to paid', format($$update public.invoices set status = 'paid' where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:invoice_transition_not_allowed)');
select inv.val('IF7 a line of an issued invoice cannot change', format($$update public.invoice_lines set unit_price_minor = 1 where invoice_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF8 ...nor be added', format($$insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values (%L, %L, 2, 'x', 1, 1, 'none', 1, 0) returning 'written'$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF9 ...nor be deleted directly', format($$delete from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF10 ...nor be moved onto a draft', format($$update public.invoice_lines set invoice_id = %L where invoice_id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad', :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF11 an issued invoice cannot be deleted directly', format($$delete from public.invoices where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF12 its creator cannot be re-pointed at another person', format($$update public.invoices set created_by = %L where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'own_a', :'inv_ai'), 'denied(42501:invoice_is_frozen)');
select inv.val('IF13 POSITIVE CONTROL: a DRAFT is still editable by its owner role', format($$update public.invoices set notes = 'edited' where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_ad'), 'written');
select inv.val('IF14 POSITIVE CONTROL: the erasure re-point (creator -> tombstone) is admitted on an issued invoice', format($$update public.invoices set created_by = private.erased_user_id() where id = %L returning 'written'$$, :'inv_pi'), 'written');
select inv.val('IF15 both freeze triggers are enabled', $$select string_agg(tgname || '=' || tgenabled, ',' order by tgname) from pg_trigger where tgname in ('invoices_freeze','invoice_lines_freeze') and not tgisinternal$$, 'invoice_lines_freeze=O,invoices_freeze=O');
select inv.val('IF16 THE PURGE STILL WORKS: erasing a workspace holding an issued, sent invoice cascades through the freeze', format($$select 'ok' from (select private.erase_organisation(%L, null, false)) s$$, :'org_p'), 'ok');
select inv.val('IF17 ...and leaves none of its invoice rows', format($$select (select count(*) from public.invoices where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_lines where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_sends where organisation_id = %1$L) || '/' || (select count(*) from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %1$L)$$, :'org_p'), '0/0/0/0');

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IN THE NETWORK VIEWS NEVER READ AN INVOICE TABLE (the function closure
-- half is network_leakage_phase6.sql IN10)
-- ===========================================================================
select inv.val('IN1 neither masking view depends on an invoice table directly; the four exist', $$select (select count(*) from pg_class v join pg_rewrite rw on rw.ev_class = v.oid join pg_depend d on d.objid = rw.oid and d.classid = 'pg_rewrite'::regclass and d.refclassid = 'pg_class'::regclass join pg_class t on t.oid = d.refobjid where v.relname in ('network_directory','network_connected_profiles') and t.relname in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends'))::text || '/' || (select count(*) from pg_class where relnamespace = 'public'::regnamespace and relname in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends'))::text$$, '0/4');

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IC THE CATALOGUES
-- ===========================================================================
select inv.val('IC1 the retention roll classifies all four (decision 8)', $$select string_agg(table_name || '=' || treatment, ',' order by table_name) from private.retention_subjects() where table_name in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends')$$, 'invoice_lines=cascades,invoice_sends=cascades,invoice_settings=cascades,invoices=cascades');
select inv.val('IC2 private.assert_retention_roll() passes on the live schema', $$select 'ok' from (select private.assert_retention_roll()) s$$, 'ok');
select inv.val('IC3 the workspace export carries all four; the account export excuses the two that name a person', $$select string_agg(export_kind || ':' || table_name || '=' || disposition, ',' order by export_kind, table_name) from private.export_catalogue() where table_name in ('invoice_settings','invoices','invoice_lines','invoice_sends')$$, 'account:invoice_sends=excused,account:invoices=excused,workspace:invoice_lines=exported,workspace:invoice_sends=exported,workspace:invoice_settings=exported,workspace:invoices=exported');
select inv.val('IC4 the export catalogue is still sound', $$select private.export_catalogue_is_sound()::text$$, 'sound');
select inv.val('IC5 the audit catalogue names the seven invoice actions', $$select count(*)::text from private.audit_action_catalogue() a where a in ('invoice.issued','invoice.sent','invoice.marked_paid','invoice.voided','invoice.pdf_downloaded','invoice_settings.changed','invoice_settings.bank_details_changed')$$, '7');
select inv.val('IC6 no uuid column of an invoice table is an unclassified identifier', $$select count(*)::text from private.unclassified_fkless_identifiers() where table_name like 'public.invoice%'$$, '0');

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IZ HOUSEKEEPING (later tasks insert their sections ABOVE this line)
-- ===========================================================================
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
-- ORIGIN mode, so the cascades run (and the freeze triggers admit them).
delete from public.organisations where id in (:'org_a', :'org_b', :'org_s', :'org_f', :'org_p');
set session_replication_role = replica;
delete from public.audit_events
 where organisation_id in (:'org_a', :'org_b', :'org_s', :'org_f', :'org_p')
    or actor_user_id in (:'own_a', :'adm_a', :'fin_a', :'mgr_a', :'ro_a', :'fin_b', :'fin_s', :'fin_f', :'fin_p');
set session_replication_role = origin;
delete from auth.users where id in (:'own_a', :'adm_a', :'fin_a', :'mgr_a', :'ro_a', :'fin_b', :'fin_s', :'fin_f', :'fin_p');
select inv.val('IZ1 housekeeping left no invoice row behind', format($$select ((select count(*) from public.invoices where organisation_id in (%1$L, %2$L, %3$L, %4$L, %5$L)) + (select count(*) from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id in (%1$L, %2$L, %3$L, %4$L, %5$L)))::text$$, :'org_a', :'org_b', :'org_s', :'org_f', :'org_p'), '0');

\echo
\echo ==================== INVOICES ====================
select seq, label, expected, actual, pass from inv.results order by seq;
select count(*) filter (where pass) as passed, count(*) filter (where not pass) as failed from inv.results;
```
Note IT23: the row is issued, so the freeze trigger answers before the CHECK does; the assertion pins that order.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the suite to see it fail**

Claim the stack (Global Constraints), then:
```bash
npx supabase db reset --local
docker cp supabase/tests/rls_invoices.sql supabase_db_getsetpaid:/tmp/rls_invoices.sql
MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker exec supabase_db_getsetpaid psql -U postgres -d postgres -q --pset=footer=off -f /tmp/rls_invoices.sql 2>&1 | tee "$TEMP/inv.out" | tail -5
```
Expected: an ERROR at the first fixture insert (`relation "public.invoices" does not exist`) — the tables are not there yet.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261106000000_invoice_tables.sql`:
```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES, PART 1 OF 3 -- TASK 3: THE FOUR TABLES.
-- Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-invoices-design.md (founder 2026-10-20).
--
-- Four tenant-owned tables. RLS ON and FORCED; authenticated holds SELECT
-- only, anon and service_role nothing. EVERY WRITE IS A SECURITY DEFINER RPC
-- (Tasks 4-7, 10, 11) that re-checks manage_finance and the Pro entitlement --
-- stricter than the spec's "UPDATE only on draft rows" (plan decision 1), and
-- the reason none of the four is in the CRM audit-coverage census (it counts
-- client-writable tables only).
--
-- THE FREEZE: private.invoices_freeze() and private.invoice_lines_freeze()
-- refuse any change to an issued invoice's content from every role, the
-- table owner included. A hard delete is admitted only from a cascade
-- (pg_trigger_depth() > 1): the workspace purge.
--
-- Person references (created_by, paid_by, voided_by, sent_by) are NO ACTION
-- foreign keys to auth.users, so private.erase_account()'s derived re-point
-- walk (step 7) moves them to the tombstone; the freeze admits exactly that
-- re-point and nothing else.
--
-- Also here, because the catalogues refuse a table they do not name: the
-- seven audit actions the RPCs will write, the retention roll (all four
-- `cascades`, plan decision 8 / Q250), and the export catalogue.
-- ===========================================================================

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. invoice_settings -- one row per workspace.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table public.invoice_settings (
  organisation_id uuid primary key references public.organisations (id) on delete cascade,
  business_name text check (business_name is null or char_length(business_name) between 1 and 200),
  business_address text check (business_address is null or char_length(business_address) between 1 and 500),
  -- Null = not VAT registered: every line is forced to 'none' and the PDF
  -- shows no VAT column (a non-registered business must not charge VAT).
  vat_number text check (vat_number is null
                         or vat_number ~ '^(GB|XI)([0-9]{9}|[0-9]{12}|GD[0-9]{3}|HA[0-9]{3})$'),
  bank_account_name text check (bank_account_name is null or char_length(bank_account_name) between 1 and 140),
  bank_sort_code text check (bank_sort_code is null or bank_sort_code ~ '^[0-9]{6}$'),
  bank_account_number text check (bank_account_number is null or bank_account_number ~ '^[0-9]{8}$'),
  number_prefix text not null default 'INV-' check (number_prefix ~ '^[A-Z0-9-]{0,12}$'),
  -- Advanced ONLY by public.issue_invoice(); no client role can write it.
  next_number integer not null default 1 check (next_number between 1 and 2147483646),
  payment_terms_days integer not null default 30 check (payment_terms_days between 0 and 120),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint invoice_settings_bank_all_or_none
    check (num_nulls(bank_account_name, bank_sort_code, bank_account_number) in (0, 3))
);

comment on table public.invoice_settings is
  'One row per workspace: the seller block, bank details and numbering for its invoices (Invoices part 1). Read by view_finance; written only by public.save_invoice_settings() (step-up in the app; every bank change audited with the last four digits only and emailed to the Owners and Admins) and, for next_number, by public.issue_invoice().';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. invoices
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table public.invoices (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  organisation_id uuid not null references public.organisations (id) on delete cascade,
  status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'issued', 'paid', 'void')),
  -- Null while draft; prefix || sequence once issued. The shape is what lets
  -- the PDF route put it in a filename with no user text reaching it.
  number text constraint invoices_number_check
    check (number is null or number ~ '^[A-Z0-9-]{0,12}[0-9]{4,10}$'),
  recipient_company_id uuid,
  recipient_contact_id uuid,
  -- No CRM record has an address column (plan decision 2); HMRC requires one.
  recipient_address text check (recipient_address is null or char_length(recipient_address) between 1 and 500),
  event_id uuid,
  issue_date date,
  due_date date,
  supply_date date,
  currency text not null default 'GBP' check (currency = 'GBP'),
  net_minor bigint not null default 0 check (net_minor >= 0),
  vat_minor bigint not null default 0 check (vat_minor >= 0),
  gross_minor bigint not null default 0,
  snapshot jsonb check (snapshot is null or jsonb_typeof(snapshot) = 'object'),
  notes text check (notes is null or char_length(notes) <= 2000),
  paid_on date,
  paid_by uuid references auth.users (id),
  paid_entry_id uuid,
  voided_at timestamptz,
  voided_by uuid references auth.users (id),
  void_reason text check (void_reason is null or char_length(void_reason) between 1 and 500),
  created_by uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  constraint invoices_gross_is_net_plus_vat check (gross_minor = net_minor + vat_minor),
  -- A draft may have no recipient yet (plan decision 3); never two.
  constraint invoices_at_most_one_recipient
    check (num_nonnulls(recipient_company_id, recipient_contact_id) <= 1),
  constraint invoices_issued_shape check (
    (status = 'draft') = (number is null)
    and (status = 'draft') = (snapshot is null)
    and (status = 'draft') = (issue_date is null)
    and (status = 'draft') = (due_date is null)
    and (status = 'draft'
         or (supply_date is not null
             and recipient_address is not null
             and num_nonnulls(recipient_company_id, recipient_contact_id) = 1
             and deleted_at is null))),
  constraint invoices_paid_shape check (
    (paid_on is null) = (paid_by is null)
    and (status <> 'paid' or paid_on is not null)
    and (status in ('paid', 'void') or (paid_on is null and paid_entry_id is null))),
  constraint invoices_void_shape check (
    (status = 'void') = (voided_at is not null)
    and (status = 'void') = (voided_by is not null)
    and (status = 'void') = (void_reason is not null)),
  constraint invoices_id_org_unique unique (id, organisation_id),
  constraint invoices_number_unique unique (organisation_id, number)
);

-- Tenant-scoped composite FKs, NO ACTION: none of these parents is ever hard
-- deleted except with the whole workspace, inside the same statement.
alter table public.invoices
  add constraint invoices_recipient_company_fkey
    foreign key (recipient_company_id, organisation_id) references public.companies (id, organisation_id),
  add constraint invoices_recipient_contact_fkey
    foreign key (recipient_contact_id, organisation_id) references public.contacts (id, organisation_id),
  add constraint invoices_event_fkey
    foreign key (event_id, organisation_id) references public.events (id, organisation_id),
  add constraint invoices_paid_entry_fkey
    foreign key (paid_entry_id, organisation_id) references public.financial_entries (id, organisation_id);

comment on table public.invoices is
  'An invoice (Invoices part 1): draft -> issued -> paid, and issued/paid -> void. Tenant-private, never read by a network view. Read by view_finance (not tier-gated: a downgrade leaves invoices readable); written only by the SECURITY DEFINER RPCs, which require manage_finance AND the Pro entitlement. Once issued its content is frozen for every role by private.invoices_freeze(); the PDF is rendered only from snapshot.';
comment on column public.invoices.paid_entry_id is
  'The actual income financial_entries row public.mark_invoice_paid() created for the lines with no financial_entry_id (net of VAT), so public.void_invoice() can soft-delete exactly that row.';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. invoice_lines
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table public.invoice_lines (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  organisation_id uuid not null references public.organisations (id) on delete cascade,
  invoice_id uuid not null,
  position integer not null check (position between 1 and 100),
  description text not null check (char_length(description) between 1 and 500),
  quantity numeric(12,2) not null check (quantity > 0),
  unit_price_minor bigint not null check (unit_price_minor >= 0),
  vat_rate text not null check (vat_rate in ('standard_20', 'reduced_5', 'zero', 'exempt', 'none')),
  net_minor bigint not null check (net_minor >= 0),
  vat_minor bigint not null check (vat_minor >= 0),
  financial_entry_id uuid,
  -- Set by public.mark_invoice_paid() when it flipped this line's entry from
  -- budgeted to actual, so public.void_invoice() reverses exactly those.
  entry_flipped boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint invoice_lines_position_unique unique (invoice_id, position),
  constraint invoice_lines_flip_needs_entry check (not entry_flipped or financial_entry_id is not null),
  constraint invoice_lines_invoice_fkey
    foreign key (invoice_id, organisation_id) references public.invoices (id, organisation_id) on delete cascade,
  constraint invoice_lines_entry_fkey
    foreign key (financial_entry_id, organisation_id) references public.financial_entries (id, organisation_id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. invoice_sends -- append-only: no client grant of any write, no UPDATE path.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table public.invoice_sends (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  organisation_id uuid not null references public.organisations (id) on delete cascade,
  invoice_id uuid not null,
  to_email text not null check (char_length(to_email) between 3 and 254 and to_email ~ '^[^\s@]+@[^\s@.]+\.[^\s@]+$'),
  sent_by uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  sent_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  provider_message_id text check (provider_message_id is null or char_length(provider_message_id) <= 200),
  constraint invoice_sends_invoice_fkey
    foreign key (invoice_id, organisation_id) references public.invoices (id, organisation_id) on delete cascade
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. Indexes: every FK's leading column (schema_invariants D3) and the list.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create index invoices_organisation_id_idx          on public.invoices (organisation_id);
create index invoices_recipient_company_id_org_idx on public.invoices (recipient_company_id, organisation_id);
create index invoices_recipient_contact_id_org_idx on public.invoices (recipient_contact_id, organisation_id);
create index invoices_event_id_org_idx             on public.invoices (event_id, organisation_id);
create index invoices_paid_entry_id_org_idx        on public.invoices (paid_entry_id, organisation_id);
create index invoices_paid_by_idx                  on public.invoices (paid_by);
create index invoices_voided_by_idx                on public.invoices (voided_by);
create index invoices_created_by_idx               on public.invoices (created_by);
create index invoices_org_created_id_idx
  on public.invoices (organisation_id, created_at, id) include (status) where deleted_at is null;
comment on index public.invoices_org_created_id_idx is
  'Covers the invoices list''s default sort (created_at desc) and keyset range, live rows only. lib/invoices/list.ts''s INVOICE_LIST.backedBy names this index.';
create index invoice_lines_organisation_id_idx     on public.invoice_lines (organisation_id);
create index invoice_lines_entry_id_org_idx        on public.invoice_lines (financial_entry_id, organisation_id);
create index invoice_sends_organisation_id_idx     on public.invoice_sends (organisation_id);
create index invoice_sends_invoice_id_org_idx      on public.invoice_sends (invoice_id, organisation_id);
create index invoice_sends_sent_by_idx             on public.invoice_sends (sent_by);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 6. updated_at, by hand, named set_updated_at (rls_phase2_crm.sql L5/L6).
--    invoice_lines and invoice_sends have no updated_at: a line is replaced,
--    never edited; a send is written once.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create trigger set_updated_at before update on public.invoice_settings
  for each row when (old.* is distinct from new.*) execute function private.set_updated_at();
create trigger set_updated_at before update on public.invoices
  for each row when (old.* is distinct from new.*) execute function private.set_updated_at();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 7. THE FREEZE.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function private.invoices_freeze()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  -- The columns a non-draft row may still change, each only in its own
  -- transition (checked below). Everything else is frozen at issue.
  v_moving constant text[] := array['status', 'paid_on', 'paid_by', 'paid_entry_id',
                                    'voided_at', 'voided_by', 'void_reason',
                                    'created_by', 'updated_at'];
  v_tomb uuid := private.erased_user_id();
begin
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
    -- A draft is soft-deleted and an issued invoice voided; a hard delete is
    -- only ever the workspace purge's cascade (depth > 1).
    if old.status <> 'draft' and pg_catalog.pg_trigger_depth() <= 1 then
      raise exception 'invoices: an issued invoice is never deleted'
        using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
    end if;
    return old;
  end if;

  if old.status = 'draft' then
    if new.status not in ('draft', 'issued') then
      raise exception 'invoices: a draft can only be issued'
        using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_transition_not_allowed';
    end if;
    return new;
  end if;

  if (pg_catalog.to_jsonb(new) - v_moving) is distinct from (pg_catalog.to_jsonb(old) - v_moving) then
    raise exception 'invoices: an issued invoice''s content is frozen'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
  end if;

  -- A recorded actor may only ever be re-pointed at the erasure tombstone.
  if (new.created_by is distinct from old.created_by and new.created_by is distinct from v_tomb)
     or (old.paid_by is not null and new.paid_by is distinct from old.paid_by
         and new.paid_by is distinct from v_tomb)
     or (old.voided_by is not null and new.voided_by is distinct from old.voided_by
         and new.voided_by is distinct from v_tomb) then
    raise exception 'invoices: an issued invoice''s actors are frozen'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
  end if;

  if new.status = old.status then
    if (new.paid_on, new.paid_entry_id, new.voided_at, new.void_reason)
         is distinct from (old.paid_on, old.paid_entry_id, old.voided_at, old.void_reason)
       or (old.paid_by is null and new.paid_by is not null)
       or (old.voided_by is null and new.voided_by is not null) then
      raise exception 'invoices: an issued invoice''s content is frozen'
        using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
    end if;
  elsif old.status = 'issued' and new.status = 'paid' then
    null;  -- the paid columns are set here; invoices_paid_shape holds their shape
  elsif old.status in ('issued', 'paid') and new.status = 'void' then
    if (new.paid_on, new.paid_by, new.paid_entry_id)
         is distinct from (old.paid_on, old.paid_by, old.paid_entry_id) then
      raise exception 'invoices: voiding keeps the payment record as it was'
        using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
    end if;
  else
    raise exception 'invoices: % -> % is not a transition', old.status, new.status
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_transition_not_allowed';
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;
revoke all on function private.invoices_freeze() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

create trigger invoices_freeze before update or delete on public.invoices
  for each row execute function private.invoices_freeze();

create or replace function private.invoice_lines_freeze()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_status text;
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.invoice_id is distinct from old.invoice_id then
    raise exception 'invoice_lines: a line never moves between invoices'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
  end if;

  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    select i.status into v_status from public.invoices i where i.id = new.invoice_id;
  else
    select i.status into v_status from public.invoices i where i.id = old.invoice_id;
  end if;

  if v_status is null or v_status = 'draft' then
    if tg_op = 'DELETE' then return old; end if;
    return new;
  end if;
  -- The workspace purge's cascade.
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' and pg_catalog.pg_trigger_depth() > 1 then
    return old;
  end if;
  -- public.mark_invoice_paid() records which entries it flipped, and nothing else.
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE'
     and (pg_catalog.to_jsonb(new) - 'entry_flipped') = (pg_catalog.to_jsonb(old) - 'entry_flipped') then
    return new;
  end if;
  raise exception 'invoice_lines: the invoice is % and its lines are frozen', v_status
    using errcode = '42501', hint = 'invoice_is_frozen';
end;
$$;
revoke all on function private.invoice_lines_freeze() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

create trigger invoice_lines_freeze before insert or update or delete on public.invoice_lines
  for each row execute function private.invoice_lines_freeze();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 8. THE ARITHMETIC (spec § Arithmetic), THE NUMBER, THE SNAPSHOT.
--    lib/invoices/arithmetic.ts is the TypeScript port; one case table binds
--    them (rls_invoices.sql § IA, tests/unit/invoices/arithmetic-cases.ts).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function private.invoice_line_amounts(
  p_quantity numeric, p_unit_price_minor bigint, p_vat_rate text)
returns table (net_minor bigint, vat_minor bigint)
language sql
immutable
set search_path = ''
as $$
  -- round(numeric) is half away from zero; every value here is >= 0, so half up.
  select s.net,
         pg_catalog.round(s.net * case p_vat_rate when 'standard_20' then 0.20
                                                  when 'reduced_5'   then 0.05
                                                  else 0 end)::bigint
    from (select pg_catalog.round(p_quantity * p_unit_price_minor)::bigint as net) s
$$;
revoke all on function private.invoice_line_amounts(numeric, bigint, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

create or replace function private.format_invoice_number(p_prefix text, p_n integer)
returns text
language sql
immutable
set search_path = ''
as $$
  -- lpad() TRUNCATES past its width (plan decision 11): pad below 10 000 only.
  select p_prefix || case when p_n < 10000 then pg_catalog.lpad(p_n::text, 4, '0') else p_n::text end
$$;
revoke all on function private.format_invoice_number(text, integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- The frozen copy (spec § Data model, snapshot): seller block from
-- invoice_settings, recipient block from the CRM record plus the typed
-- address, the lines and the totals. lib/invoices/snapshot.ts parses it.
create or replace function private.build_invoice_snapshot(
  p_invoice_id uuid, p_number text, p_issue date, p_supply date, p_due date)
returns jsonb
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
  select pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
           'version', 1,
           'number', p_number,
           'issue_date', p_issue,
           'supply_date', p_supply,
           'due_date', p_due,
           'currency', i.currency,
           'seller', pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
             'name', s.business_name,
             'address', s.business_address,
             'vat_number', s.vat_number,
             'bank', case when s.bank_account_number is null then null
                          else pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                                 'account_name', s.bank_account_name,
                                 'sort_code', s.bank_sort_code,
                                 'account_number', s.bank_account_number) end),
           'recipient', pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
             'kind', case when i.recipient_company_id is not null then 'company' else 'contact' end,
             'name', coalesce(co.name, ct.name),
             'address', i.recipient_address,
             'email', ct.email),
           'lines', coalesce((select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                                       'position', l.position,
                                       'description', l.description,
                                       'quantity', l.quantity::text,
                                       'unit_price_minor', l.unit_price_minor,
                                       'vat_rate', l.vat_rate,
                                       'net_minor', l.net_minor,
                                       'vat_minor', l.vat_minor) order by l.position)
                                from public.invoice_lines l where l.invoice_id = i.id), '[]'::jsonb),
           'totals', pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
             'net_minor', t.net, 'vat_minor', t.vat, 'gross_minor', t.net + t.vat),
           'notes', i.notes)
    from public.invoices i
    join public.invoice_settings s on s.organisation_id = i.organisation_id
    left join public.companies co
      on co.id = i.recipient_company_id and co.organisation_id = i.organisation_id
    left join public.contacts ct
      on ct.id = i.recipient_contact_id and ct.organisation_id = i.organisation_id
    cross join lateral (
      select coalesce(sum(l.net_minor), 0)::bigint as net, coalesce(sum(l.vat_minor), 0)::bigint as vat
        from public.invoice_lines l where l.invoice_id = i.id) t
   where i.id = p_invoice_id
$$;
revoke all on function private.build_invoice_snapshot(uuid, text, date, date, date) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 9. RLS, grants, policies. Reads are NOT tier-gated (a downgrade leaves the
--    invoices readable, spec § Permissions); view_finance folds in aal2,
--    suspension and the closure-backup read through permitted_organisations().
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
alter table public.invoice_settings enable row level security;
alter table public.invoice_settings force row level security;
alter table public.invoices         enable row level security;
alter table public.invoices         force row level security;
alter table public.invoice_lines    enable row level security;
alter table public.invoice_lines    force row level security;
alter table public.invoice_sends    enable row level security;
alter table public.invoice_sends    force row level security;

revoke all on public.invoice_settings from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke all on public.invoices         from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke all on public.invoice_lines    from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke all on public.invoice_sends    from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select on public.invoice_settings, public.invoices, public.invoice_lines, public.invoice_sends to authenticated;

create policy invoice_settings_select_view_finance on public.invoice_settings
  for select to authenticated
  using (organisation_id in (select private.permitted_organisations('view_finance')));
create policy invoices_select_view_finance on public.invoices
  for select to authenticated
  using (organisation_id in (select private.permitted_organisations('view_finance')));
create policy invoice_lines_select_view_finance on public.invoice_lines
  for select to authenticated
  using (organisation_id in (select private.permitted_organisations('view_finance')));
create policy invoice_sends_select_view_finance on public.invoice_sends
  for select to authenticated
  using (organisation_id in (select private.permitted_organisations('view_finance')));
```

```bash
# ============================================================================
# INVOICES, PART 1 OF 3 (20261106000000 onwards; founder 2026-10-20). Four
# tenant tables no client role writes, the freeze triggers, the arithmetic
# and numbering, and (Tasks 4-11) the draft/issue/paid/void/send RPCs, the

# re-runnable (it removes its own fixtures).
# ============================================================================
run_suite "INVOICES" rls_invoices.sql inv -
inv_fails=$SUITE_FAILS
if [ "$inv_fails" != "0" ]; then
  echo "INVOICES SUITE FAILED: $inv_fails failing assertion(s)" >&2
  echo "  IT/IB red: an invoice table lost RLS, a constraint, or a client role" >&2
  echo "  gained a write grant. IR red: AN INVOICE IS READABLE OUTSIDE ITS" >&2
  echo "  WORKSPACE, or without view_finance. IF red: AN ISSUED INVOICE CAN BE" >&2
  echo "  CHANGED, or the purge no longer passes the freeze. IA red: the" >&2
  echo "  arithmetic or the numbering moved. IN red: a network view reads an" >&2
  echo "  invoice table. IC red: a catalogue lost an invoice table or action." >&2
  echo "  ID/IS/IP/IV/IE/IX/IQ/ID2/IY red: an RPC lost a refusal, its order, or" >&2
  echo "  its finance link (each section's header says which)." >&2
  psql_status=1
else
  echo "Invoices suite: all assertions passed."
fi
```

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261106001000_invoice_draft_rpcs.sql`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's tables and `private.invoice_line_amounts`, `public.has_permission(uuid, text)`, `private.finance_module_organisations()`.
- Produces:
  - `public.save_invoice_draft(p_org uuid, p_invoice_id uuid, p_recipient_company_id uuid, p_recipient_contact_id uuid, p_event_id uuid, p_recipient_address text, p_supply_date date, p_notes text, p_lines jsonb) returns uuid` — `p_invoice_id` null creates a draft; otherwise replaces that draft's fields and lines. `p_lines` is a JSON array (≤ 100) of `{"description": text, "quantity": "N.NN", "unit_price_minor": int, "vat_rate": text, "financial_entry_id": uuid|null}`. Returns the draft's id.
  - `public.delete_invoice_draft(p_invoice_id uuid) returns void` — soft delete.
  - Refusal hints (SQLSTATE): `not_authenticated` (28000), `forbidden` (42501), `plan_does_not_include_invoicing` (42501), `invoice_lines_invalid`, `invoice_line_invalid`, `invoice_one_recipient`, `invoice_recipient_not_live`, `invoice_event_not_live`, `invoice_entry_not_invoiceable` (all 22023), `invoice_not_an_editable_draft` (22023).

```sql
    jsonb_build_object('description', 'Production', 'quantity', '2.50', 'unit_price_minor', 333, 'vat_rate', 'standard_20', 'financial_entry_id', null))) as id_new \gset
select inv.val('ID1 a Finance user saves a new draft; the database computes the totals', format($$select status || '|' || net_minor || '|' || vat_minor || '|' || gross_minor from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'id_new'), 'draft|300833|60167|361000');
select inv.val('ID2 the lines keep their order, their amounts and the pulled entry', format($$select string_agg(position || ':' || (financial_entry_id is not null)::text || ':' || net_minor || ':' || vat_minor, ',' order by position) from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = %L$$, :'id_new'), '1:true:300000:60000,2:false:833:167');
select inv.val('ID3 created_by is the caller, stamped by the database', format($$select (created_by = %L)::text from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'fin_a', :'id_new'), 'true');
select inv.val('ID4 saving the draft again replaces its fields and lines', format($$select (public.save_invoice_draft(%L, %L, %L, null, null, '8 Customer Road, London', null, null, '[{"description":"Hire","quantity":"1.00","unit_price_minor":10000,"vat_rate":"reduced_5","financial_entry_id":null}]'::jsonb) = %L)::text$$, :'org_a', :'id_new', :'com_a', :'id_new'), 'true');
select inv.val('ID5 ...one line now, totals recomputed, recipient switched to the company', format($$select (select count(*) from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = %1$L) || '|' || gross_minor || '|' || (recipient_company_id is not null and recipient_contact_id is null)::text from public.invoices where id = %1$L$$, :'id_new'), '1|10500|true');
select inv.val('ID6 POSITIVE CONTROL: the RPCs are executable by authenticated, and by nobody else', $$select count(*) filter (where has_function_privilege('authenticated', f, 'EXECUTE')) || '/' || count(*) filter (where has_function_privilege('anon', f, 'EXECUTE') or has_function_privilege('service_role', f, 'EXECUTE')) from unnest(array['public.save_invoice_draft(uuid,uuid,uuid,uuid,uuid,text,date,text,jsonb)','public.delete_invoice_draft(uuid)']) f$$, '2/0');
select inv.val('ID7 a line pulled from a COST entry is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, %L, null, null, null, jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('description','x','quantity','1.00','unit_price_minor',1000,'vat_rate','none','financial_entry_id',%L)))::text$$, :'org_a', :'ev_a', :'fe_ac'), 'denied(22023:invoice_entry_not_invoiceable)');
select inv.val('ID8 a pulled line on a draft with no event is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('description','x','quantity','1.00','unit_price_minor',1000,'vat_rate','none','financial_entry_id',%L)))::text$$, :'org_a', :'fe_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_entry_not_invoiceable)');
select inv.val('ID9 two recipients are refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, %L, %L, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a', :'com_a', :'con_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_one_recipient)');
select inv.val('ID10 ANOTHER workspace''s contact as recipient is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, %L, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a', :'con_b'), 'denied(22023:invoice_recipient_not_live)');
select inv.val('ID11 more than 100 lines are refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, (select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('description','x','quantity','1.00','unit_price_minor',1,'vat_rate','none','financial_entry_id',null)) from generate_series(1, 101)))::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_lines_invalid)');
select inv.val('ID12 a three-decimal quantity is refused, not rounded', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[{"description":"x","quantity":"1.005","unit_price_minor":1,"vat_rate":"none","financial_entry_id":null}]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_line_invalid)');
select inv.val('ID13 an unknown VAT rate is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[{"description":"x","quantity":"1.00","unit_price_minor":1,"vat_rate":"standard","financial_entry_id":null}]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_line_invalid)');
select inv.val('ID14 an empty description is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[{"description":"","quantity":"1.00","unit_price_minor":1,"vat_rate":"none","financial_entry_id":null}]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_line_invalid)');
select inv.val('ID15 the ISSUED invoice cannot be saved over', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, %L, %L, null, null, 'x', null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a', :'inv_ai', :'com_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_not_an_editable_draft)');
select inv.val('ID16 B''s draft cannot be saved over by naming A', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, %L, null, null, null, 'x', null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a', :'inv_bd'), 'denied(22023:invoice_not_an_editable_draft)');
select inv.val('ID17 nor by naming B, where the caller holds nothing', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, %L, null, null, null, 'x', null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_b', :'inv_bd'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
select inv.val('ID18 B''s event is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, (select id from public.events where organisation_id = %L limit 1), null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a', :'org_b'), 'denied(22023:invoice_event_not_live)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c1';
select inv.val('ID19 MFA: the same Finance user at aal1 is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'mgr_a_c';
select inv.val('ID20 a Manager (no manage_finance) is refused', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
select inv.val('ID21 ...and cannot delete a draft either', format($$select 'ok' from (select public.delete_invoice_draft(%L)) s$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_s_c';
select inv.val('ID22 a STARTER workspace''s Finance user is refused by the plan', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_s'), 'denied(42501:plan_does_not_include_invoicing)');
select inv.val('ID23 ...and cannot delete its own draft (read-only after a downgrade)', format($$select 'ok' from (select public.delete_invoice_draft(%L)) s$$, :'inv_sd'), 'denied(42501:plan_does_not_include_invoicing)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_f_c';
select inv.val('ID24 a FREE workspace''s Finance user is refused by the plan', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_f'), 'denied(42501:plan_does_not_include_invoicing)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_b_c';
select public.save_invoice_draft(:'org_b', null, null, :'con_b', null, '7 Buyer Street', null, null,
  '[{"description":"Hire","quantity":"1.00","unit_price_minor":10000,"vat_rate":"standard_20","financial_entry_id":null}]'::jsonb) as id_b \gset
select inv.val('ID25 NOT VAT REGISTERED: B''s 20% line is stored as no VAT, with no VAT charged', format($$select vat_rate || '|' || vat_minor from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = %L$$, :'id_b'), 'none|0');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';
select inv.val('ID26 the Finance user soft-deletes the draft', format($$select 'ok' from (select public.delete_invoice_draft(%L)) s$$, :'id_new'), 'ok');
select inv.val('ID27 ...it is soft-deleted, not gone', format($$select (deleted_at is not null)::text from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'id_new'), 'true');
select inv.val('ID28 a soft-deleted draft cannot be saved over', format($$select public.save_invoice_draft(%L, %L, null, null, null, null, null, null, '[]'::jsonb)::text$$, :'org_a', :'id_new'), 'denied(22023:invoice_not_an_editable_draft)');
select inv.val('ID29 an issued invoice cannot be deleted as a draft', format($$select 'ok' from (select public.delete_invoice_draft(%L)) s$$, :'inv_ai'), 'denied(22023:invoice_not_an_editable_draft)');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261106001000_invoice_draft_rpcs.sql`:
```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES PART 1 -- TASK 4: DRAFTS.
-- public.save_invoice_draft() creates or replaces a draft and its lines in
-- one transaction, with the line amounts computed by
-- private.invoice_line_amounts() (the one source of the numbers);
-- public.delete_invoice_draft() soft-deletes one. Both SECURITY DEFINER (no
-- client role can write the tables), both re-check, in this order, a session,
-- manage_finance (which folds in aal2 and suspension), and the Pro
-- entitlement. Not audited: drafts are working copies (plan decision 10).
-- A workspace that is not VAT registered gets every line as 'none'.
-- ===========================================================================

create or replace function public.save_invoice_draft(
  p_org                  uuid,
  p_invoice_id           uuid,
  p_recipient_company_id uuid,
  p_recipient_contact_id uuid,
  p_event_id             uuid,
  p_recipient_address    text,
  p_supply_date          date,
  p_notes                text,
  p_lines                jsonb
)
returns uuid
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_id  uuid := p_invoice_id;
  v_vat text;
  v_bad integer;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  if p_org is null or not public.has_permission(p_org, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: the caller may not manage finance in this workspace'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = p_org) then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;

  if p_lines is null or pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(p_lines) <> 'array'
     or pg_catalog.jsonb_array_length(p_lines) > 100 then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: lines must be an array of at most 100'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_lines_invalid';
  end if;
  -- Quantity text is checked by shape, never silently rounded by the cast.
  select pg_catalog.count(*) into v_bad
    from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(p_lines) e
   where pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(e) <> 'object'
      or coalesce(e ->> 'quantity', '') !~ '^[0-9]{1,10}(\.[0-9]{1,2})?$';
  if v_bad > 0 then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: a line is invalid'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_line_invalid';
  end if;

  if p_recipient_company_id is not null and p_recipient_contact_id is not null then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: an invoice has one recipient'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_one_recipient';
  end if;
  if (p_recipient_company_id is not null
      and not exists (select 1 from public.companies c
                       where c.id = p_recipient_company_id and c.organisation_id = p_org
                         and c.deleted_at is null))
     or (p_recipient_contact_id is not null
      and not exists (select 1 from public.contacts c
                       where c.id = p_recipient_contact_id and c.organisation_id = p_org
                         and c.deleted_at is null)) then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: the recipient is not a live record of this workspace'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_recipient_not_live';
  end if;
  if p_event_id is not null
     and not exists (select 1 from public.events e
                      where e.id = p_event_id and e.organisation_id = p_org and e.deleted_at is null) then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: the event is not a live event of this workspace'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_event_not_live';
  end if;
  -- A pulled line names a live INCOME entry of this workspace, on this
  -- invoice's event (so a draft with no event cannot pull a line).
  select pg_catalog.count(*) into v_bad
    from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(p_lines) e
   where e ->> 'financial_entry_id' is not null
     and not exists (
       select 1 from public.financial_entries fe
        where fe.id = (e ->> 'financial_entry_id')::uuid
          and fe.organisation_id = p_org
          and fe.deleted_at is null
          and fe.category = 'income'
          and fe.event_id is not distinct from p_event_id);
  if v_bad > 0 then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: a pulled line no longer names an invoiceable income entry'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_entry_not_invoiceable';
  end if;

  select s.vat_number into v_vat from public.invoice_settings s where s.organisation_id = p_org;

  if v_id is null then
    insert into public.invoices (organisation_id, recipient_company_id, recipient_contact_id, event_id,
                                 recipient_address, supply_date, notes, created_by)
    values (p_org, p_recipient_company_id, p_recipient_contact_id, p_event_id,
            p_recipient_address, p_supply_date, p_notes, v_uid)
    returning id into v_id;
  else
    update public.invoices i
       set recipient_company_id = p_recipient_company_id,
           recipient_contact_id = p_recipient_contact_id,
           event_id = p_event_id,
           recipient_address = p_recipient_address,
           supply_date = p_supply_date,
           notes = p_notes
     where i.id = v_id and i.organisation_id = p_org and i.status = 'draft' and i.deleted_at is null;
    if not found then
      raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: that is not an editable draft of this workspace'
        using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_an_editable_draft';
    end if;
    delete from public.invoice_lines l where l.invoice_id = v_id;
  end if;

  begin
    insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity,
                                      unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor, financial_entry_id)
    select p_org, v_id, e.ord::integer, e.line ->> 'description', (e.line ->> 'quantity')::numeric(12,2),
           (e.line ->> 'unit_price_minor')::bigint,
           case when v_vat is null then 'none' else e.line ->> 'vat_rate' end,
           a.net_minor, a.vat_minor, (e.line ->> 'financial_entry_id')::uuid
      from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(p_lines) with ordinality as e(line, ord)
     cross join lateral private.invoice_line_amounts(
       (e.line ->> 'quantity')::numeric(12,2),
       (e.line ->> 'unit_price_minor')::bigint,
       case when v_vat is null then 'none' else e.line ->> 'vat_rate' end) a;
  exception when check_violation or not_null_violation or invalid_text_representation
                 or numeric_value_out_of_range then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_draft: a line is invalid'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_line_invalid';
  end;

  update public.invoices i
     set (net_minor, vat_minor, gross_minor) =
         (select coalesce(sum(l.net_minor), 0), coalesce(sum(l.vat_minor), 0),
                 coalesce(sum(l.net_minor + l.vat_minor), 0)
            from public.invoice_lines l where l.invoice_id = v_id)
   where i.id = v_id;

  return v_id;
end;
$$;

comment on function public.save_invoice_draft(uuid, uuid, uuid, uuid, uuid, text, date, text, jsonb) is
  'Creates (p_invoice_id null) or replaces a DRAFT invoice and all its lines in one transaction, computing every amount with private.invoice_line_amounts(). Requires a session, manage_finance (aal2, not suspended) and the Pro entitlement. A recipient, event and pulled finance entry must be live records of p_org; a pulled entry must be income on the invoice''s event. An unregistered seller''s lines are stored as vat_rate none. Refuses with the hints listed in 20261106001000.';

revoke all on function public.save_invoice_draft(uuid, uuid, uuid, uuid, uuid, text, date, text, jsonb)
  from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.save_invoice_draft(uuid, uuid, uuid, uuid, uuid, text, date, text, jsonb)
  to authenticated;

create or replace function public.delete_invoice_draft(p_invoice_id uuid)
returns void
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_org uuid;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'delete_invoice_draft: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  select i.organisation_id into v_org from public.invoices i where i.id = p_invoice_id;
  -- One refusal for "not yours" and "not there": no existence oracle.
  if v_org is null or not public.has_permission(v_org, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'delete_invoice_draft: the caller may not manage finance in this workspace'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = v_org) then
    raise exception 'delete_invoice_draft: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;
  update public.invoices i set deleted_at = pg_catalog.now()
   where i.id = p_invoice_id and i.status = 'draft' and i.deleted_at is null;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'delete_invoice_draft: that is not an editable draft'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_an_editable_draft';
  end if;
end;
$$;

comment on function public.delete_invoice_draft(uuid) is
  'Soft-deletes a live DRAFT invoice. Requires a session, manage_finance and the Pro entitlement in the invoice''s own workspace. An issued invoice is voided, never deleted.';

revoke all on function public.delete_invoice_draft(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.delete_invoice_draft(uuid) to authenticated;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Mutants (record, revert)**

1. Drop the `finance_module_organisations` check in `save_invoice_draft` → ID22 and ID24 go red.
2. Change `case when v_vat is null then 'none' else e.line ->> 'vat_rate' end` (both occurrences) to `e.line ->> 'vat_rate'` → ID25 goes red.
3. Drop `and fe.category = 'income'` → ID7 goes red.
4. Drop `and i.status = 'draft'` from the update → ID15 goes red.

```sql
  (:'inv_n3', :'org_n', null,     null,         :'fin_n');
insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor) values
  (:'org_n', :'inv_n1', 1, 'Fee', 1, 10000, 'standard_20', 0, 0),
  (:'org_n', :'inv_n2', 1, 'Fee', 1, 20000, 'standard_20', 0, 0);
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_n_c';
select inv.val('IS1 no settings row: issuing is refused', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n1'), 'denied(22023:invoice_settings_incomplete)');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
insert into public.invoice_settings (organisation_id, business_name, business_address, number_prefix, payment_terms_days)
  values (:'org_n', 'Numbering Ltd', '1 Number Street', 'NUM-', 14);
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_n_c';
select inv.val('IS2 a draft with no recipient is refused', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n3'), 'denied(22023:invoice_needs_a_recipient)');
select inv.val('IS3 THE FIRST ISSUE TAKES NUMBER 1, padded', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n1'), 'NUM-0001');
select inv.val('IS4 ...the counter moved to 2', format($$select next_number::text from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %L$$, :'org_n'), '2');
select inv.val('IS5 ...the row is issued with UK dates, terms applied, VAT recomputed (not VAT registered: none)', format($$select concat_ws('|', status, number, (issue_date = (now() at time zone 'Europe/London')::date)::text, (supply_date = issue_date)::text, (due_date - issue_date)::text, net_minor, vat_minor, gross_minor) from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'inv_n1'), 'issued|NUM-0001|true|true|14|10000|0|10000');
select inv.val('IS6 ...the line was recomputed to no VAT under an unregistered seller', format($$select vat_rate || '|' || net_minor || '|' || vat_minor from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = %L$$, :'inv_n1'), 'none|10000|0');
select inv.val('IS7 ...the snapshot carries the number, no VAT number, the recipient and the totals', format($$select concat_ws('|', snapshot ->> 'number', coalesce(snapshot -> 'seller' ->> 'vat_number', 'null'), snapshot -> 'recipient' ->> 'name', snapshot -> 'recipient' ->> 'email', snapshot -> 'totals' ->> 'gross_minor', snapshot ->> 'due_date' = due_date::text) from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'inv_n1'), 'NUM-0001|null|Nina Number|nina@example.test|10000|true');
select inv.val('IS8 ...and one invoice.issued audit row, actor the caller, payload {number, status} and no amount (read as postgres: audit_events is superuser-read)', format($$select count(distinct a.id) || '|' || bool_and(a.actor_user_id = %L)::text || '|' || max(a.after ->> 'number') || '|' || string_agg(k, ',' order by k) from public.audit_events a cross join lateral jsonb_object_keys(a.after) k where a.action = 'invoice.issued' and a.target_id = %L$$, :'fin_n', :'inv_n1'), '1|true|NUM-0001|number,status');
select inv.val('IS9 issuing it again is refused', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n1'), 'denied(22023:invoice_not_a_draft)');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
-- A ROLLED-BACK ISSUE CONSUMES NO NUMBER: issue inside a transaction, roll it back.
begin;
set local role authenticated;
select pg_catalog.set_config('request.jwt.claims', :'fin_n_c', true) \gset
select public.issue_invoice(:'inv_n2') as rolled_back_number \gset
rollback;
select inv.val('IS10 a rolled-back issue took a number inside its transaction', format($$select %L$$, :'rolled_back_number'), 'NUM-0002');
select inv.val('IS11 ...and after the rollback the counter is still 2 and the draft still a draft', format($$select (select next_number from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %L) || '|' || (select status from public.invoices where id = %L)$$, :'org_n', :'inv_n2'), '2|draft');
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_n_c';
select inv.val('IS12 the next real issue takes 2 -- no gap', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n2'), 'NUM-0002');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';
select inv.val('IS13 A''s Finance user cannot issue org_n''s draft (no existence oracle)', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n3'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
select inv.val('IS14 ...nor an id that does not exist, with the same refusal', $$select public.issue_invoice(gen_random_uuid())$$, 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
select inv.val('IS15 VAT REGISTERED: A''s draft issues at A''s counter with its 20% intact', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_ad'), 'INV-0001');
select inv.val('IS16 ...its totals and its snapshot''s seller VAT number', format($$select concat_ws('|', net_minor, vat_minor, gross_minor, snapshot -> 'seller' ->> 'vat_number', snapshot -> 'lines' -> 0 ->> 'vat_rate') from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'inv_ad'), '50000|10000|60000|GB123456789|standard_20');
set request.jwt.claims = :'mgr_a_c';
select inv.val('IS17 a Manager cannot issue', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_ad'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_s_c';
select inv.val('IS18 a STARTER workspace cannot issue', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_sd'), 'denied(42501:plan_does_not_include_invoicing)');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
-- The recipient soft-deleted after drafting (plan decision 7, Q249).
update public.invoices set recipient_contact_id = :'con_n', recipient_address = '1 Nina Way' where id = :'inv_n3';
update public.contacts set deleted_at = now() where id = :'con_n';
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_n_c';
select inv.val('IS19 a draft whose recipient was deleted is refused, not issued', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n3'), 'denied(22023:invoice_recipient_deleted)');
select inv.val('IS20 ...and is still a live draft (not soft-deleted with its recipient, Q249)', format($$select status || '|' || (deleted_at is null)::text from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'inv_n3'), 'draft|true');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
update public.contacts set deleted_at = null where id = :'con_n';
delete from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = :'inv_n3';
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_n_c';
select inv.val('IS21 a draft with no lines is refused', format($$select public.issue_invoice(%L)$$, :'inv_n3'), 'denied(22023:invoice_has_no_lines)');
select inv.val('IS22 only authenticated may execute issue_invoice', $$select has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.issue_invoice(uuid)', 'EXECUTE')::text || '/' || (has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.issue_invoice(uuid)', 'EXECUTE') or has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.issue_invoice(uuid)', 'EXECUTE'))::text$$, 'true/false');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;
```
