- [ ] **Step 2: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261106002000_invoice_issue.sql`:
```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES PART 1 -- TASK 5: ISSUE.
-- public.issue_invoice(): in ONE transaction, lock the draft, re-check
-- membership, manage_finance and the Pro entitlement, refuse an incomplete
-- draft or incomplete settings, lock the settings row FOR UPDATE, recompute
-- every line under the seller's current VAT status, take next_number, write
-- number / dates / totals / snapshot, flip to issued, audit. Two concurrent
-- issues in one workspace serialise on the settings row and get consecutive
-- numbers (invoice_issue_concurrency_probe.sh); a rolled-back issue consumes
-- no number (rls_invoices.sql IS10-IS12).
-- ===========================================================================

create or replace function public.issue_invoice(p_invoice_id uuid)
returns text
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid    uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_inv    public.invoices;
  v_set    public.invoice_settings;
  v_number text;
  v_issue  date;
  v_supply date;
  v_due    date;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;

  select * into v_inv from public.invoices i where i.id = p_invoice_id for update;
  -- One refusal for "not yours" and "not there": no existence oracle.
  if not found or not public.has_permission(v_inv.organisation_id, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: the caller may not issue this invoice'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = v_inv.organisation_id) then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;
  if v_inv.status <> 'draft' or v_inv.deleted_at is not null then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: only a live draft can be issued'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_a_draft';
  end if;
  if pg_catalog.num_nonnulls(v_inv.recipient_company_id, v_inv.recipient_contact_id) <> 1 then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: choose a recipient first'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_needs_a_recipient';
  end if;
  if (v_inv.recipient_company_id is not null
      and not exists (select 1 from public.companies c
                       where c.id = v_inv.recipient_company_id
                         and c.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id and c.deleted_at is null))
     or (v_inv.recipient_contact_id is not null
      and not exists (select 1 from public.contacts c
                       where c.id = v_inv.recipient_contact_id
                         and c.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id and c.deleted_at is null)) then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: the recipient has been deleted'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_recipient_deleted';
  end if;
  if v_inv.recipient_address is null or pg_catalog.btrim(v_inv.recipient_address) = '' then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: a VAT invoice shows the recipient''s address'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_needs_recipient_address';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.invoice_lines l where l.invoice_id = p_invoice_id) then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: an invoice needs at least one line'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_has_no_lines';
  end if;

  select * into v_set from public.invoice_settings s
   where s.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id for update;
  if not found or v_set.business_name is null or v_set.business_address is null then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: the workspace''s invoice settings are incomplete'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_settings_incomplete';
  end if;

  -- Recompute under the seller's CURRENT VAT status (still a draft, so the
  -- line freeze admits it).
  update public.invoice_lines l
     set vat_rate = case when v_set.vat_number is null then 'none' else l.vat_rate end,
         (net_minor, vat_minor) = (
           select a.net_minor, a.vat_minor
             from private.invoice_line_amounts(
                    l.quantity, l.unit_price_minor,
                    case when v_set.vat_number is null then 'none' else l.vat_rate end) a)
   where l.invoice_id = p_invoice_id;

  v_number := private.format_invoice_number(v_set.number_prefix, v_set.next_number);
  update public.invoice_settings s set next_number = s.next_number + 1
   where s.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id;

  v_issue  := (pg_catalog.now() at time zone 'Europe/London')::date;
  v_supply := coalesce(v_inv.supply_date, v_issue);
  v_due    := v_issue + v_set.payment_terms_days;

  begin
    update public.invoices i
       set status = 'issued',
           number = v_number,
           issue_date = v_issue,
           supply_date = v_supply,
           due_date = v_due,
           (net_minor, vat_minor, gross_minor) = (
             select coalesce(sum(l.net_minor), 0), coalesce(sum(l.vat_minor), 0),
                    coalesce(sum(l.net_minor + l.vat_minor), 0)
               from public.invoice_lines l where l.invoice_id = i.id),
           snapshot = private.build_invoice_snapshot(i.id, v_number, v_issue, v_supply, v_due)
     where i.id = p_invoice_id;
  exception when unique_violation then
    raise exception 'issue_invoice: % is already used in this workspace', v_number
      using errcode = '23505', hint = 'invoice_number_taken';
  end;

  perform private.log_audit_event(
    p_action          => 'invoice.issued',
    p_target_type     => 'invoice',
    p_organisation_id => v_inv.organisation_id,
    p_target_id       => p_invoice_id,
    p_before          => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('status', 'draft'),
    p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('status', 'issued', 'number', v_number));
  return v_number;
end;
$$;

comment on function public.issue_invoice(uuid) is
  'Issues a live draft: re-checks manage_finance and the Pro entitlement, refuses an incomplete draft or incomplete settings, locks invoice_settings FOR UPDATE, recomputes the lines under the seller''s current VAT status, takes next_number (consecutive under concurrency; a rollback consumes none), sets the UK-local issue date, supply date and due date, writes the snapshot, flips to issued and audits invoice.issued. Returns the number.';

revoke all on function public.issue_invoice(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.issue_invoice(uuid) to authenticated;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the concurrency probe**

Create `supabase/tests/invoice_issue_concurrency_probe.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ============================================================================
# TWO DRAFTS IN ONE WORKSPACE ISSUED AT ONCE (Invoices part 1, Task 5; spec
# § Lifecycle: "Two concurrent issues get two consecutive numbers").
#
# A single-session suite cannot measure this: an unlocked read of
# next_number passes every assertion in rls_invoices.sql § IS and still lets
# two concurrent sessions take the same number. Session one issues draft 1
# and holds its transaction for two seconds; half a second in, session two
# issues draft 2. EXPECTED: PRB-0001 and PRB-0002, next_number 3, and session
# two's call returned PRB-0002 (it waited on the settings row lock).
#
# Run by run-rls-tests.sh, and by hand after a reset:
#   bash supabase/tests/invoice_issue_concurrency_probe.sh
# It cleans up after itself.
# ============================================================================
set -uo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
CTR="supabase_db_$(grep -E '^project_id' "$ROOT/supabase/config.toml" | cut -d'"' -f2)"

psql_q() { MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker exec -i "$CTR" psql -U postgres -d postgres -Atq -c "$1"; }

FIN=$(psql_q "select gen_random_uuid()")
ORG=$(psql_q "select gen_random_uuid()")
CON=$(psql_q "select gen_random_uuid()")
D1=$(psql_q "select gen_random_uuid()")
D2=$(psql_q "select gen_random_uuid()")
FIN_C="{\"sub\":\"$FIN\",\"role\":\"authenticated\",\"aal\":\"aal2\"}"

psql_q "
insert into auth.users (id, instance_id, aud, role, email)
  values ('$FIN', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'authenticated', 'authenticated', 'inv-race-$FIN@test.local');
insert into public.organisations (id, name, slug) values ('$ORG', 'Invoice Race', 'inv-race-${ORG:0:8}');
insert into public.organisation_subscriptions (organisation_id, plan_id, billing_cycle, seat_count, status, current_period_end)
  values ('$ORG', 'pro', 'monthly', 1, 'active', now() + interval '30 days')
  on conflict (organisation_id) do update set plan_id = 'pro', status = 'active';
insert into public.organisation_memberships (organisation_id, user_id, role, status, joined_at)
  values ('$ORG', '$FIN', 'finance', 'active', now());
insert into public.contacts (id, organisation_id, name) values ('$CON', '$ORG', 'Race Customer');
insert into public.invoice_settings (organisation_id, business_name, business_address, number_prefix)
  values ('$ORG', 'Race Ltd', '1 Race Street', 'PRB-');
insert into public.invoices (id, organisation_id, recipient_contact_id, recipient_address, created_by)
  values ('$D1', '$ORG', '$CON', '2 Race Road', '$FIN'), ('$D2', '$ORG', '$CON', '2 Race Road', '$FIN');
insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor)
  values ('$ORG', '$D1', 1, 'One', 1, 100, 'none', 100, 0), ('$ORG', '$D2', 1, 'Two', 1, 200, 'none', 200, 0);
" >/dev/null

DIR=$(mktemp -d)
MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker exec -i "$CTR" psql -U postgres -d postgres -Atq > "$DIR/one" 2>&1 <<SQL &
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claims = '$FIN_C';
select 'issued:' || public.issue_invoice('$D1');
select pg_sleep(2);
commit;
SQL
P1=$!
sleep 0.5
MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker exec -i "$CTR" psql -U postgres -d postgres -Atq > "$DIR/two" 2>&1 <<SQL &
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claims = '$FIN_C';
select 'issued:' || public.issue_invoice('$D2');
commit;
SQL
P2=$!
wait $P1; wait $P2

NUMBERS=$(psql_q "select string_agg(number, ',' order by number) from public.invoices where organisation_id = '$ORG'")
NEXT=$(psql_q "select next_number from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = '$ORG'")
echo "session one: $(tr '\n' ' ' < "$DIR/one")"
echo "session two: $(tr '\n' ' ' < "$DIR/two")"
echo "after: numbers=$NUMBERS next_number=$NEXT"
STATUS=0
if [ "$NUMBERS" != "PRB-0001,PRB-0002" ] || [ "$NEXT" != "3" ] \
   || ! grep -q 'issued:PRB-0001' "$DIR/one" || ! grep -q 'issued:PRB-0002' "$DIR/two"; then
  echo "FAIL: concurrent issues did not get two consecutive numbers -- the settings row lock is gone."
  STATUS=1
else
  echo "PASS: two concurrent issues took PRB-0001 and PRB-0002, in order, with no gap."
fi

psql_q "delete from public.organisations where id = '$ORG';" >/dev/null
psql_q "
set session_replication_role = replica;
delete from public.audit_events where actor_user_id = '$FIN';
set session_replication_role = origin;
delete from auth.users where id = '$FIN';
" >/dev/null
rm -rf "$DIR"
exit $STATUS
```
In `run-rls-tests.sh`, after the retention probe block (ends line ~1337), add:

```bash
# ============================================================================
# TWO INVOICE ISSUES IN ONE WORKSPACE, UNDER A REAL RACE (Invoices part 1,
# Task 5). public.issue_invoice() locks invoice_settings FOR UPDATE; without
# it two sessions read the same next_number.
# ============================================================================
if bash "$HERE/invoice_issue_concurrency_probe.sh"; then
  :
else
  echo "INVOICE ISSUE CONCURRENCY PROBE FAILED: two concurrent issues did not get" >&2
  echo "  two consecutive numbers (issue_invoice's settings row lock)." >&2
  psql_status=1
fi
```

### Task 6: Mark paid and void, with the finance link; the erasure re-point

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261106003000_invoice_paid_and_void.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/rls_invoices.sql` (§ IP, § IV, § IE above § IZ), `supabase/tests/run-rls-tests.sh` (pin)
- Modify: `tests/integration/security/phase7-permission-matrix.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3–5; `public.financial_entries`; `private.erase_account(uuid, uuid)`.
- Produces:
  - `public.mark_invoice_paid(p_invoice_id uuid, p_paid_on date) returns void` — issued only; `p_paid_on` between the issue date and today (UK). If the invoice has a **live** event: each pulled line's `budgeted` entry flips to `actual` (amount unchanged) and the line's `entry_flipped` is set; the net sum of lines with no `financial_entry_id`, if non-zero, becomes one new `actual` `income` entry dated `p_paid_on`, described `Invoice <number>`, recorded in `paid_entry_id`. Audit `invoice.marked_paid`: before `{status}`, after `{status, number, entries_flipped, income_entry_created}` (name-only: no `paid_on`, no amount, because CLAUDE.md § Non-negotiable rules outranks this plan).
  - `public.void_invoice(p_invoice_id uuid, p_reason text) returns void` — issued or paid; reason 1–500 chars after trim. From paid: flipped entries back to `budgeted`, the created entry soft-deleted. Number kept. Audit `invoice.voided`: after `{status, number, previous_status, entries_reverted, income_entry_removed}`; the reason is never in it.
  - Hints: `forbidden`, `plan_does_not_include_invoicing` (42501); `invoice_not_issued`, `invoice_paid_on_out_of_range`, `invoice_not_voidable`, `invoice_void_reason_required` (22023).

```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- § IP MARK PAID (Task 6, 20261106003000)
-- ===========================================================================
\o /dev/null
select gen_random_uuid()::text as ev_d \gset
select gen_random_uuid()::text as fe_d \gset
\o
insert into public.events (id, organisation_id, name, starts_at)
  values (:'ev_d', :'org_a', 'Alpha Cancelled Night', now() + interval '20 days');
insert into public.financial_entries (id, organisation_id, event_id, category, status, amount_minor, description)

set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';
select public.save_invoice_draft(:'org_a', null, null, :'con_a', :'ev_a', '9 Customer Road, London', null, null,
  jsonb_build_array(

    jsonb_build_object('description', 'Production fee', 'quantity', '2.00', 'unit_price_minor', 15000, 'vat_rate', 'standard_20', 'financial_entry_id', null))) as id_p \gset

select inv.val('IP6 ...it is paid, dated, by the caller', format($$select status || '|' || (paid_on = (now() at time zone 'Europe/London')::date)::text || '|' || (paid_by = %L)::text from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'fin_a', :'id_p'), 'paid|true|true');
select inv.val('IP7 ...the pulled entry flipped budgeted -> actual, its amount left as it was', format($$select status || '|' || amount_minor from public.financial_entries where id = %L$$, :'fe_a'), 'actual|300000');
select inv.val('IP8 ...and its line records the flip', format($$select string_agg(position || ':' || entry_flipped::text, ',' order by position) from public.invoice_lines where invoice_id = %L$$, :'id_p'), '1:true,2:false');
select inv.val('IP9 ...the free line''s NET (not gross) became one actual income entry on the event, named for the invoice', format($$select concat_ws('|', fe.category, fe.status, fe.amount_minor, fe.description, (fe.occurred_on = i.paid_on)::text, (fe.event_id = %L)::text) from public.invoices i join public.financial_entries fe on fe.id = i.paid_entry_id where i.id = %L$$, :'ev_a', :'id_p'), 'income|actual|30000|Invoice INV-0002|true|true');

select inv.val('IP13 ...and touches no finance (plan decision 6): the entry stays budgeted, nothing created', format($$select (select status from public.financial_entries where id = %L) || '|' || coalesce((select paid_entry_id::text from public.invoices where id = %L), 'none')$$, :'fe_d', :'id_pd'), 'budgeted|none');
set request.jwt.claims = :'mgr_a_c';

set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';

select inv.val('IV2 a 501-character reason is refused', format($$select 'ok' from (select public.void_invoice(%L, repeat('x', 501))) s$$, :'id_p'), 'denied(22023:invoice_void_reason_required)');
select inv.val('IV3 THE VOID OF A PAID INVOICE', format($$select 'ok' from (select public.void_invoice(%L, 'Wrong recipient')) s$$, :'id_p'), 'ok');
select inv.val('IV4 ...the flipped entry is budgeted again', format($$select status from public.financial_entries where id = %L$$, :'fe_a'), 'budgeted');

select inv.val('IV6 ...the invoice is void, KEEPS its number and its payment record, with the reason', format($$select concat_ws('|', status, number, (paid_on is not null)::text, void_reason, (voided_by = %L)::text) from public.invoices where id = %L$$, :'fin_a', :'id_p'), 'void|INV-0002|true|Wrong recipient|true');

select inv.val('IV13 only authenticated may execute the two RPCs', $$select count(*) filter (where has_function_privilege('authenticated', f, 'EXECUTE')) || '/' || count(*) filter (where has_function_privilege('anon', f, 'EXECUTE') or has_function_privilege('service_role', f, 'EXECUTE')) from unnest(array['public.mark_invoice_paid(uuid,date)','public.void_invoice(uuid,text)']) f$$, '2/0');
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261106003000_invoice_paid_and_void.sql`:
```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES PART 1 -- TASK 6: MARK PAID, VOID, AND THE FINANCE LINK.
-- Income is recorded NET of VAT: VAT collected is owed to HMRC, not income.
-- "The remaining lines" are those with no financial_entry_id (plan decision
-- 5), so a pulled line whose entry is already actual is never booked twice.
-- An invoice whose event is soft-deleted is marked paid without touching
-- finance (plan decision 6). Void keeps the number and reverses exactly what
-- mark-paid recorded (invoice_lines.entry_flipped, invoices.paid_entry_id).
-- The void reason is free text and stays out of the audit payload.
-- ===========================================================================

create or replace function public.mark_invoice_paid(p_invoice_id uuid, p_paid_on date)
returns void
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid     uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_inv     public.invoices;
  v_live    boolean := false;
  v_flipped uuid[] := '{}';
  v_rest    bigint := 0;
  v_entry   uuid;
  v_today   date := (pg_catalog.now() at time zone 'Europe/London')::date;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'mark_invoice_paid: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  select * into v_inv from public.invoices i where i.id = p_invoice_id for update;
  if not found or not public.has_permission(v_inv.organisation_id, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'mark_invoice_paid: the caller may not change this invoice'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = v_inv.organisation_id) then
    raise exception 'mark_invoice_paid: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;
  if v_inv.status <> 'issued' then
    raise exception 'mark_invoice_paid: only an issued invoice can be marked paid'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_issued';
  end if;
  if p_paid_on is null or p_paid_on > v_today or p_paid_on < v_inv.issue_date then
    raise exception 'mark_invoice_paid: the payment date must be between the issue date and today'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_paid_on_out_of_range';
  end if;

  if v_inv.event_id is not null then
    select e.deleted_at is null into v_live
      from public.events e
     where e.id = v_inv.event_id and e.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id;
  end if;

  if coalesce(v_live, false) then
    with flipped as (
      update public.financial_entries fe
         set status = 'actual'
       where fe.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id
         and fe.status = 'budgeted'
         and fe.deleted_at is null
         and fe.id in (select l.financial_entry_id from public.invoice_lines l
                        where l.invoice_id = p_invoice_id and l.financial_entry_id is not null)
      returning fe.id)
    select coalesce(pg_catalog.array_agg(f.id), '{}') into v_flipped from flipped f;

    -- Still 'issued', so the line freeze admits a change to entry_flipped alone.
    update public.invoice_lines l set entry_flipped = true
     where l.invoice_id = p_invoice_id and l.financial_entry_id = any (v_flipped);

    select coalesce(sum(l.net_minor), 0) into v_rest
      from public.invoice_lines l
     where l.invoice_id = p_invoice_id and l.financial_entry_id is null;

    if v_rest > 0 then
      insert into public.financial_entries (organisation_id, event_id, category, status, amount_minor,
                                            currency, occurred_on, description, created_by)
      values (v_inv.organisation_id, v_inv.event_id, 'income', 'actual', v_rest,
              'GBP', p_paid_on, 'Invoice ' || v_inv.number, v_uid)
      returning id into v_entry;
    end if;
  end if;

  update public.invoices i
     set status = 'paid', paid_on = p_paid_on, paid_by = v_uid, paid_entry_id = v_entry
   where i.id = p_invoice_id;

  perform private.log_audit_event(
    p_action          => 'invoice.marked_paid',
    p_target_type     => 'invoice',
    p_organisation_id => v_inv.organisation_id,
    p_target_id       => p_invoice_id,
    p_before          => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('status', 'issued'),
    p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                           'status', 'paid', 'number', v_inv.number,
                           'entries_flipped', pg_catalog.cardinality(v_flipped),
                           'income_entry_created', v_entry is not null));
end;
$$;

comment on function public.mark_invoice_paid(uuid, date) is
  'Marks an ISSUED invoice paid on p_paid_on (issue date .. UK today). With a live event: flips each pulled budgeted entry to actual (recorded in invoice_lines.entry_flipped) and books the net of the lines with no financial_entry_id as one actual income entry (invoices.paid_entry_id). Requires manage_finance and the Pro entitlement; audits invoice.marked_paid.';
revoke all on function public.mark_invoice_paid(uuid, date) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.mark_invoice_paid(uuid, date) to authenticated;

create or replace function public.void_invoice(p_invoice_id uuid, p_reason text)
returns void
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid      uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_inv      public.invoices;
  v_reason   text := pg_catalog.btrim(p_reason);
  v_reverted integer := 0;
  v_removed  boolean := false;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'void_invoice: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  select * into v_inv from public.invoices i where i.id = p_invoice_id for update;
  if not found or not public.has_permission(v_inv.organisation_id, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'void_invoice: the caller may not change this invoice'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = v_inv.organisation_id) then
    raise exception 'void_invoice: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;
  if v_inv.status not in ('issued', 'paid') then
    raise exception 'void_invoice: only an issued or paid invoice can be voided'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_voidable';
  end if;
  if v_reason is null or v_reason = '' or pg_catalog.char_length(v_reason) > 500 then
    raise exception 'void_invoice: a reason of at most 500 characters is required'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_void_reason_required';
  end if;

  if v_inv.status = 'paid' then
    update public.financial_entries fe
       set status = 'budgeted'
     where fe.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id
       and fe.status = 'actual'
       and fe.id in (select l.financial_entry_id from public.invoice_lines l
                      where l.invoice_id = p_invoice_id and l.entry_flipped);
    get diagnostics v_reverted = row_count;
    if v_inv.paid_entry_id is not null then
      update public.financial_entries fe
         set deleted_at = pg_catalog.now()
       where fe.id = v_inv.paid_entry_id
         and fe.organisation_id = v_inv.organisation_id
         and fe.deleted_at is null;
      v_removed := found;
    end if;
  end if;

  update public.invoices i
     set status = 'void', voided_at = pg_catalog.now(), voided_by = v_uid, void_reason = v_reason
   where i.id = p_invoice_id;

  perform private.log_audit_event(
    p_action          => 'invoice.voided',
    p_target_type     => 'invoice',
    p_organisation_id => v_inv.organisation_id,
    p_target_id       => p_invoice_id,
    p_before          => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('status', v_inv.status),
    p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                           'status', 'void', 'number', v_inv.number, 'previous_status', v_inv.status,
                           'entries_reverted', v_reverted, 'income_entry_removed', v_removed));
end;
$$;

comment on function public.void_invoice(uuid, text) is
  'Voids an issued or paid invoice with a reason (1-500 chars). Keeps the number. From paid, reverses exactly what mark_invoice_paid recorded: flipped entries back to budgeted, the created income entry soft-deleted. Requires manage_finance and the Pro entitlement; audits invoice.voided (the reason is not in the payload).';
revoke all on function public.void_invoice(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.void_invoice(uuid, text) to authenticated;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the suite, see it pass**

Reset, run the invoices suite. Expected: failed `0`. If IE1 is refused by an `erase_account` precondition (it records the hint), read the hint, adjust only the § IE fixture (never the function), and re-run.

- [ ] **Step 4: Mutants (record, revert)**

1. Book the gross instead of the net (`sum(l.net_minor + l.vat_minor)`) → IP9 goes red (36000 ≠ 30000).
2. Change mark-paid's `set entry_flipped = true` to `set entry_flipped = false` → IP8 and IV4 go red (void can no longer find what to reverse).
3. Drop `v_removed`'s soft delete → IV5 goes red.
4. Drop the `coalesce(v_live, false)` guard (always touch finance) → IP13 goes red.

- [ ] **Step 5: Matrix, pin, full run, gates, commit**

Add to `MEMBER_REACHABLE_RPCS`:

```ts
    "issued invoice paid; with a live event it flips the pulled budgeted entries and " +
    "books the net of the free lines as actual income. Asserted in rls_invoices.sql § IP.",
  void_invoice:
    "Invoices part 1 Task 6 -- the same holder voids an issued or paid invoice with a " +

    "rls_invoices.sql § IV.",
```

### Task 7: Settings RPC and the bank-change notice queue (database)

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261106004000_invoice_settings_and_bank_notice.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/rls_invoices.sql` (§ IX, § IQ above § IZ), `supabase/tests/run-rls-tests.sh` (pin)
- Modify: `tests/integration/security/phase7-permission-matrix.test.ts` (`MEMBER_REACHABLE_RPCS`, `SERVER_ONLY_RPCS`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `public.save_invoice_settings(p_org uuid, p_business_name text, p_business_address text, p_vat_number text, p_bank_account_name text, p_bank_sort_code text, p_bank_account_number text, p_number_prefix text, p_payment_terms_days integer) returns boolean` — upserts the row (never touches `next_number`); returns `true` when any bank field changed. Audits `invoice_settings.changed` (`after: {changed: [column names]}`) when anything changed, and `invoice_settings.bank_details_changed` (`before/after: {account_last4}`) when a bank field changed. Hints: `forbidden`, `plan_does_not_include_invoicing` (42501), `invoice_settings_invalid` (22023).
  - Notice `invoice_bank_details_changed` in `private.notification_deliveries`, keyed on the `invoice_settings.bank_details_changed` audit row; `public.mark_notices_delivered` / `public.mark_notices_undeliverable` accept it.
  - `public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(p_limit integer default 30) returns table (audit_event_id uuid, emails text[], organisation_slug text, organisation_name text, account_last4 text, changed_at timestamptz)` — `service_role` only; recipients are the workspace's **current** active Owners and Admins (tombstone excluded); seven-day window.

```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- § IX SETTINGS (Task 7, 20261106004000)
-- ===========================================================================
set role authenticated;
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_a_c';
select inv.val('IX1 changing the bank account returns true (the bank changed)', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'Alpha Promotions Ltd', '1 Alpha Street, London', 'GB123456789', 'Alpha Promotions Ltd', '654321', '87654321', 'INV-', 30)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'true');
select inv.val('IX2 ...stored, and next_number untouched by a save', format($$select bank_sort_code || '|' || bank_account_number || '|' || next_number from public.invoice_settings where organisation_id = %L$$, :'org_a'), '654321|87654321|3');
select inv.val('IX3 ...one invoice_settings.changed row naming the changed columns', format($$select count(*) || '|' || max(after -> 'changed' ->> 0) || '|' || max(jsonb_array_length(after -> 'changed'))::text from public.audit_events where action = 'invoice_settings.changed' and organisation_id = %L$$, :'org_a'), '1|bank_account_number|2');
select inv.val('IX4 ...one bank_details_changed row carrying the LAST FOUR digits before and after', format($$select count(*) || '|' || max(before ->> 'account_last4') || '|' || max(after ->> 'account_last4') from public.audit_events where action = 'invoice_settings.bank_details_changed' and organisation_id = %L$$, :'org_a'), '1|5678|4321');
select inv.val('IX5 ...and no audit row anywhere carries the full account number', $$select count(*)::text from public.audit_events where (coalesce(before::text, '') || coalesce(after::text, '')) like '%87654321%'$$, '0');
select inv.val('IX6 saving the business name alone returns false and writes no bank row', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'Alpha Promotions Limited', '1 Alpha Street, London', 'GB123456789', 'Alpha Promotions Ltd', '654321', '87654321', 'INV-', 30)::text || '|' || (select count(*) from public.audit_events where action = 'invoice_settings.bank_details_changed' and organisation_id = %L)$$, :'org_a', :'org_a'), 'false|1');
select inv.val('IX7 saving nothing new writes no audit row at all', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'Alpha Promotions Limited', '1 Alpha Street, London', 'GB123456789', 'Alpha Promotions Ltd', '654321', '87654321', 'INV-', 30)::text || '|' || (select count(*) from public.audit_events where action = 'invoice_settings.changed' and organisation_id = %L)$$, :'org_a', :'org_a'), 'false|2');
select inv.val('IX8 an invalid sort code is refused with the settings hint', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'A', 'B', null, 'N', '12-34', '12345678', 'INV-', 30)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(22023:invoice_settings_invalid)');
select inv.val('IX9 B''s settings cannot be saved by A''s Finance user', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'A', 'B', null, null, null, null, 'INV-', 30)::text$$, :'org_b'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'mgr_a_c';
select inv.val('IX10 a Manager cannot save settings', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'A', 'B', null, null, null, null, 'INV-', 30)::text$$, :'org_a'), 'denied(42501:forbidden)');
set request.jwt.claims = :'fin_s_c';
select inv.val('IX11 a STARTER workspace cannot save settings', format($$select public.save_invoice_settings(%L, 'A', 'B', null, null, null, null, 'INV-', 30)::text$$, :'org_s'), 'denied(42501:plan_does_not_include_invoicing)');
reset role;
reset request.jwt.claims;

-- ===========================================================================
-- § IQ THE BANK-CHANGE NOTICE QUEUE (Task 7). Read and stamped by the sender
-- (service_role, no person on the session), never by a person.
-- ===========================================================================
select id as bank_audit from public.audit_events where action = 'invoice_settings.bank_details_changed' and organisation_id = :'org_a' \gset
select id as issued_audit from public.audit_events where action = 'invoice.issued' and organisation_id = :'org_a' limit 1 \gset
set role service_role;
select inv.val('IQ1 the sender sees the change, addressed to A''s current Owner and Admin only, with the last four', format($$select count(*) || '|' || max(cardinality(emails)) || '|' || bool_and(emails @> array[%L, %L]::text[])::text || '|' || max(account_last4) from public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(30) where audit_event_id = %L$$, 'inv-own-a-' || :'own_a' || '@test.local', 'inv-adm-a-' || :'adm_a' || '@test.local', :'bank_audit'), '1|2|true|4321');
select inv.val('IQ2 a stamp on a row of another action writes nothing', format($$select public.mark_notices_delivered('invoice_bank_details_changed', array[%L]::uuid[])::text$$, :'issued_audit'), '0');
select inv.val('IQ3 the stamp on the bank-change row writes one ledger row', format($$select public.mark_notices_delivered('invoice_bank_details_changed', array[%L]::uuid[])::text$$, :'bank_audit'), '1');
select inv.val('IQ4 ...and the row leaves the queue', format($$select count(*)::text from public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(30) where audit_event_id = %L$$, :'bank_audit'), '0');
select inv.val('IQ5 the undeliverable branch accepts the notice too (idempotent on the same row)', format($$select public.mark_notices_undeliverable('invoice_bank_details_changed', array[%L]::uuid[], 'no_recipient')::text$$, :'bank_audit'), '0');
select inv.val('IQ6 REGRESSION: the existing notices are still accepted', $$select public.mark_notices_delivered('workspace_closed', array[gen_random_uuid()]::uuid[])::text$$, '0');
reset role;
-- As postgres (which may execute it) but WITH a person on the session: the
-- body's own guard must refuse. (authenticated holds no EXECUTE at all, IQ8.)
set request.jwt.claims = :'own_a_c';
select inv.val('IQ7 a person''s session cannot read the queue, not even the Owner''s', $$select count(*)::text from public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(30)$$, 'denied(42501:notification_stamp_is_server_only)');
reset request.jwt.claims;
select inv.val('IQ8 the resolver is service_role-only, the settings RPC authenticated-only', $$select concat_ws('|', has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(integer)', 'EXECUTE')::text, has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(integer)', 'EXECUTE')::text, has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.save_invoice_settings(uuid,text,text,text,text,text,text,text,integer)', 'EXECUTE')::text, has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.save_invoice_settings(uuid,text,text,text,text,text,text,text,integer)', 'EXECUTE')::text)$$, 'true|false|true|false');
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261106004000_invoice_settings_and_bank_notice.sql`:
```sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES PART 1 -- TASK 7: SETTINGS, AND THE BANK-CHANGE NOTICE.
--
-- public.save_invoice_settings(): the only writer of invoice_settings apart
-- from issue_invoice's next_number. Step-up re-authentication is the app's
-- (lib/invoices/settings-actions.ts, checkStepUp); manage_finance here is
-- already aal2-gated. BANK-DETAIL FRAUD GUARD (spec § Security 2): a change to
-- any bank field writes invoice_settings.bank_details_changed carrying the
-- last four digits of the account number only, and that audit row is the
-- subject of an email to every active Owner and Admin, delivered on the
-- existing tick through the notice ledger (plan decision 9) -- the
-- 20261022001000 pattern: ledger notice, rebuilt mark_notices_* branches,
-- a service_role resolver.
-- ===========================================================================

create or replace function public.save_invoice_settings(
  p_org                 uuid,
  p_business_name       text,
  p_business_address    text,
  p_vat_number          text,
  p_bank_account_name   text,
  p_bank_sort_code      text,
  p_bank_account_number text,
  p_number_prefix       text,
  p_payment_terms_days  integer
)
returns boolean
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid     uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_old     public.invoice_settings;
  v_new     public.invoice_settings;
  v_changed text[];
  v_bank    boolean;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_settings: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  if p_org is null or not public.has_permission(p_org, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_settings: the caller may not manage finance in this workspace'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = p_org) then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_settings: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;

  select * into v_old from public.invoice_settings s where s.organisation_id = p_org for update;

  begin
    insert into public.invoice_settings (organisation_id, business_name, business_address, vat_number,
                                         bank_account_name, bank_sort_code, bank_account_number,
                                         number_prefix, payment_terms_days)
    values (p_org, p_business_name, p_business_address, p_vat_number,
            p_bank_account_name, p_bank_sort_code, p_bank_account_number,
            p_number_prefix, p_payment_terms_days)
    on conflict (organisation_id) do update
      set business_name       = excluded.business_name,
          business_address    = excluded.business_address,
          vat_number          = excluded.vat_number,
          bank_account_name   = excluded.bank_account_name,
          bank_sort_code      = excluded.bank_sort_code,
          bank_account_number = excluded.bank_account_number,
          number_prefix       = excluded.number_prefix,
          payment_terms_days  = excluded.payment_terms_days
    returning * into v_new;
  exception when check_violation or not_null_violation then
    raise exception 'save_invoice_settings: a setting is invalid'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_settings_invalid';
  end;

  select coalesce(pg_catalog.array_agg(k order by k), '{}') into v_changed
    from pg_catalog.unnest(array['bank_account_name', 'bank_account_number', 'bank_sort_code',
                                 'business_address', 'business_name', 'number_prefix',
                                 'payment_terms_days', 'vat_number']) k
   where (pg_catalog.to_jsonb(v_new) -> k) is distinct from (pg_catalog.to_jsonb(v_old) -> k);
  v_bank := v_changed && array['bank_account_name', 'bank_account_number', 'bank_sort_code'];

  if pg_catalog.cardinality(v_changed) > 0 then
    -- Column NAMES only, never values.
    perform private.log_audit_event(
      p_action          => 'invoice_settings.changed',
      p_target_type     => 'invoice_settings',
      p_organisation_id => p_org,
      p_target_id       => p_org,
      p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('changed', pg_catalog.to_jsonb(v_changed)));
  end if;
  if v_bank then
    perform private.log_audit_event(
      p_action          => 'invoice_settings.bank_details_changed',
      p_target_type     => 'invoice_settings',
      p_organisation_id => p_org,
      p_target_id       => p_org,
      p_before          => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('account_last4', pg_catalog.right(v_old.bank_account_number, 4)),
      p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('account_last4', pg_catalog.right(v_new.bank_account_number, 4)));
  end if;
  return v_bank;
end;
$$;

comment on function public.save_invoice_settings(uuid, text, text, text, text, text, text, text, integer) is
  'Upserts a workspace''s invoice settings (never next_number). Requires manage_finance and the Pro entitlement; step-up is enforced by the calling Server Action. Audits invoice_settings.changed (column names only) and, on any bank change, invoice_settings.bank_details_changed with the last four digits only -- the subject of the Owner/Admin email. Returns whether a bank field changed.';
revoke all on function public.save_invoice_settings(uuid, text, text, text, text, text, text, text, integer)
  from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.save_invoice_settings(uuid, text, text, text, text, text, text, text, integer)
  to authenticated;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- THE LEDGER LEARNS ONE NOTICE, keyed on its audit row (20261022001000's
-- audit_event_id branch).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
alter table private.notification_deliveries
  drop constraint notification_deliveries_notice_check,
  add constraint notification_deliveries_notice_check
    check (notice in ('connection_received', 'connection_accepted', 'connection_declined',
                      'task_reminder',
                      'workspace_closed', 'workspace_reassigned', 'workspace_deleted',
                      'invoice_bank_details_changed')),
  drop constraint notification_deliveries_one_subject,
  add constraint notification_deliveries_one_subject check (
    case when notice = 'task_reminder'
           then task_id is not null and request_id is null and audit_event_id is null
         when notice in ('workspace_closed', 'workspace_reassigned', 'workspace_deleted',
                         'invoice_bank_details_changed')
           then audit_event_id is not null and request_id is null and task_id is null
         else request_id is not null and task_id is null and audit_event_id is null end);

-- The notice -> audit action map, one more arm.
create or replace function private.closure_notice_action(p_notice text)
returns text
language sql
immutable
set search_path = ''
as $$
  select case p_notice
           when 'workspace_closed'             then 'organisation.closed_by_owner'
           when 'workspace_reassigned'         then 'organisation.closure_reassigned'
           when 'workspace_deleted'            then 'organisation.closure_deleted'
           when 'invoice_bank_details_changed' then 'invoice_settings.bank_details_changed'
         end
$$;
revoke all on function private.closure_notice_action(text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
```
Then append `public.mark_notices_delivered(text, uuid[])` and `public.mark_notices_undeliverable(text, uuid[], text)`, rebuilt from their live `pg_get_functiondef` (latest defining migration `20261022001000_superuser_decides_a_closed_workspace.sql`, lines 78–187; diff first). In **each**, change exactly one line —
```sql
  elsif p_notice in ('workspace_closed', 'workspace_reassigned', 'workspace_deleted') then
```
to
```sql
  elsif p_notice in ('workspace_closed', 'workspace_reassigned', 'workspace_deleted',
                     'invoice_bank_details_changed') then
```
— and nothing else (the branch already filters `a.action = private.closure_notice_action(p_notice)`). Keep their existing grants (re-state them after each function exactly as the live ACL shows: `select proacl from pg_proc where oid = 'public.mark_notices_delivered(text,uuid[])'::regprocedure`).

Then the resolver:
```sql
create or replace function public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(p_limit integer default 30)
returns table (audit_event_id uuid, emails text[], organisation_slug text, organisation_name text,
               account_last4 text, changed_at timestamptz)
language plpgsql
stable
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if (select auth.uid()) is not null
     or coalesce(pg_catalog.current_setting('role', true), 'none') not in ('postgres', 'service_role', 'none') then
    raise exception 'pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications: read by the sender, never from a person''s session'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'notification_stamp_is_server_only';
  end if;
  return query
    select a.id,
           (select pg_catalog.array_agg(u.email::text order by u.email)
              from public.organisation_memberships m
              join auth.users u on u.id = m.user_id
             where m.organisation_id = a.organisation_id
               and m.status = 'active'
               and m.role in ('owner', 'admin')
               and u.id <> private.erased_user_id()
               and u.email is not null),
           o.slug, o.name, a.after ->> 'account_last4', a.created_at
      from public.audit_events a
      join public.organisations o on o.id = a.organisation_id
     where a.action = 'invoice_settings.bank_details_changed'
       and a.created_at > pg_catalog.now() - interval '7 days'
       and not exists (select 1 from private.notification_deliveries d
                        where d.audit_event_id = a.id and d.notice = 'invoice_bank_details_changed')
     order by a.created_at, a.id
     limit greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_limit, 30), 30));
end;
$$;
comment on function public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(integer) is
  'service_role only: invoice_settings.bank_details_changed audit rows from the last seven days not yet in the notice ledger, each with the CURRENT active Owners'' and Admins'' addresses (tombstone excluded), the workspace and the last four digits. lib/email/invoice-notifications.ts sends one email per row on the delivery tick.';
revoke all on function public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(integer) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications(integer) to service_role;
```

```ts
// tests/unit/invoices/settings-form.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readSettingsForm } from "@/lib/invoices/settings-form";

function form(values: Record<string, string>): FormData {
  const data = new FormData();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(values)) data.set(k, v);
  return data;
}
const good = {
  businessName: "Alpha Promotions Ltd",
  businessAddress: "1 Alpha Street\nLondon",
  vatNumber: "gb 123 4567 89",
  bankAccountName: "Alpha Promotions Ltd",
  bankSortCode: "12-34-56",
  bankAccountNumber: "1234 5678",
  numberPrefix: "inv-",
  paymentTermsDays: "30",
};

describe("readSettingsForm", () => {
  it("normalises what a person types: VAT upper-cased without spaces, sort code digits only, prefix upper-cased", () => {
    const r = readSettingsForm(form(good));
    expect(r).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      values: {
        businessName: "Alpha Promotions Ltd",
        businessAddress: "1 Alpha Street\nLondon",
        vatNumber: "GB123456789",
        bankAccountName: "Alpha Promotions Ltd",
        bankSortCode: "123456",
        bankAccountNumber: "12345678",
        numberPrefix: "INV-",
        paymentTermsDays: 30,
      },
    });
  });

  it("treats a blank VAT number and blank bank details as not set", () => {
    const r = readSettingsForm(form({ ...good, vatNumber: "", bankAccountName: "", bankSortCode: "", bankAccountNumber: "" }));
    expect(r.ok && r.values).toMatchObject({ vatNumber: null, bankAccountName: null, bankSortCode: null, bankAccountNumber: null });
  });

  it("refuses each bad field with its own sentence", () => {
    const r = readSettingsForm(form({
      ...good,
      businessName: "",
      vatNumber: "FR123",
      bankSortCode: "123",
      bankAccountNumber: "",
      numberPrefix: "inv/",
      paymentTermsDays: "121",
    }));
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (r.ok) return;
    expect(Object.keys(r.fieldErrors).sort()).toEqual(
      ["bankAccountNumber", "bankSortCode", "businessName", "numberPrefix", "paymentTermsDays", "vatNumber"].sort(),
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `lib/invoices/settings-form.ts`**

```ts
// The invoice settings form, read and validated. PURE (no I/O) so it is
// unit-tested; the database CHECKs on public.invoice_settings are the
// enforcement, this is the sentence a person can act on.
import { field } from "@/lib/forms";

export interface SettingsInput {
  businessName: string;
  businessAddress: string;
  vatNumber: string | null;
  bankAccountName: string | null;
  bankSortCode: string | null;
  bankAccountNumber: string | null;
  numberPrefix: string;
  paymentTermsDays: number;
}

const VAT = /^(GB|XI)([0-9]{9}|[0-9]{12}|GD[0-9]{3}|HA[0-9]{3})$/;

/** The raw textarea value, trimmed at the ends but keeping its line breaks. */
function multiline(data: FormData, name: string): string | null {
  const raw = data.get(name);
  if (typeof raw !== "string") return null;
  const trimmed = raw.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").trim();
  return trimmed === "" ? null : trimmed;
}

export function readSettingsForm(
  formData: FormData,
): { ok: true; values: SettingsInput } | { ok: false; fieldErrors: Record<string, string> } {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};

  const businessName = field(formData, "businessName");
  if (!businessName) errors.businessName = "Enter the business name that goes on your invoices.";
  else if (businessName.length > 200) errors.businessName = "That name is too long — 200 characters at most.";

  const businessAddress = multiline(formData, "businessAddress");
  if (!businessAddress) errors.businessAddress = "Enter the business address that goes on your invoices.";
  else if (businessAddress.length > 500) errors.businessAddress = "That address is too long — 500 characters at most.";

  const vatRaw = field(formData, "vatNumber");
  const vatNumber = vatRaw ? vatRaw.replace(/\s+/g, "").toUpperCase() : null;
  if (vatNumber && !VAT.test(vatNumber)) {
    errors.vatNumber = "Enter a UK VAT number, for example GB123456789 — or leave it blank if you are not VAT registered.";
  }

  const bankAccountName = field(formData, "bankAccountName");
  const sortRaw = field(formData, "bankSortCode");
  const bankSortCode = sortRaw ? sortRaw.replace(/[\s-]+/g, "") : null;
  const accountRaw = field(formData, "bankAccountNumber");
  const bankAccountNumber = accountRaw ? accountRaw.replace(/\s+/g, "") : null;
  const anyBank = bankAccountName !== null || bankSortCode !== null || bankAccountNumber !== null;
  if (anyBank) {
    if (!bankAccountName) errors.bankAccountName = "Enter the account name, or clear all three bank fields.";
    else if (bankAccountName.length > 140) errors.bankAccountName = "That account name is too long — 140 characters at most.";
    if (!bankSortCode || !/^[0-9]{6}$/.test(bankSortCode)) errors.bankSortCode = "Enter a six-digit sort code.";
    if (!bankAccountNumber || !/^[0-9]{8}$/.test(bankAccountNumber)) errors.bankAccountNumber = "Enter an eight-digit account number.";
  }

  const numberPrefix = (field(formData, "numberPrefix") ?? "").toUpperCase();
  if (!/^[A-Z0-9-]{0,12}$/.test(numberPrefix)) {
    errors.numberPrefix = "Use up to 12 letters, digits or hyphens.";
  }

  const termsRaw = field(formData, "paymentTermsDays") ?? "";
  const paymentTermsDays = /^\d{1,3}$/.test(termsRaw) ? Number(termsRaw) : NaN;
  if (!Number.isInteger(paymentTermsDays) || paymentTermsDays < 0 || paymentTermsDays > 120) {
    errors.paymentTermsDays = "Enter a number of days from 0 to 120.";
  }

  if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return { ok: false, fieldErrors: errors };
  return {
    ok: true,
    values: {
      businessName: businessName as string,
      businessAddress: businessAddress as string,
      vatNumber,
      bankAccountName: anyBank ? bankAccountName : null,
      bankSortCode: anyBank ? bankSortCode : null,
      bankAccountNumber: anyBank ? bankAccountNumber : null,
      numberPrefix,
      paymentTermsDays,
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
// tests/unit/invoices/refusals.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { INVOICE_REFUSAL_SENTENCES, invoiceRefusal } from "@/lib/invoices/refusals";
import { FAILED_MESSAGE, REFUSED_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/crm/write";

describe("invoiceRefusal", () => {
  it("maps every RPC hint the migrations raise to its sentence", () => {
    for (const hint of [
      "plan_does_not_include_invoicing", "invoice_not_a_draft", "invoice_not_an_editable_draft",
      "invoice_needs_a_recipient", "invoice_recipient_deleted", "invoice_recipient_not_live",
      "invoice_needs_recipient_address", "invoice_has_no_lines", "invoice_settings_incomplete",
      "invoice_event_not_live", "invoice_entry_not_invoiceable", "invoice_line_invalid",
      "invoice_lines_invalid", "invoice_one_recipient", "invoice_not_issued",
      "invoice_paid_on_out_of_range", "invoice_not_voidable", "invoice_void_reason_required",
      "invoice_not_sendable", "invoice_send_email_invalid", "invoice_number_taken",
      "invoice_settings_invalid",
    ]) {
      expect(INVOICE_REFUSAL_SENTENCES[hint], hint).toBeTruthy();
      expect(invoiceRefusal({ code: "22023", hint, message: "x" })).toBe(INVOICE_REFUSAL_SENTENCES[hint]);
    }
  });
  it("an unmapped 42501 (forbidden) is the generic refusal; anything else the generic failure", () => {
    expect(invoiceRefusal({ code: "42501", hint: "forbidden", message: "x" })).toBe(REFUSED_MESSAGE);
    expect(invoiceRefusal({ code: "XX000", message: "boom" })).toBe(FAILED_MESSAGE);
  });
});
```

```ts
// lib/invoices/refusals.ts
import "server-only";
// An invoice RPC's refusal hint -> the sentence a person can act on. The
// hints are raised by the 2026110600x000 migrations; a hint not listed here
// falls back to the generic refusal (42501) or failure (anything else).
import { FAILED_MESSAGE, REFUSED_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/crm/write";

export const INVOICE_REFUSAL_SENTENCES: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  plan_does_not_include_invoicing: "Invoicing is a Pro feature. An Owner can upgrade the workspace to issue invoices.",
  invoice_not_a_draft: "This invoice has already been issued, so it can no longer be changed.",
  invoice_not_an_editable_draft: "This draft can no longer be changed. Reload the page to see where it stands.",
  invoice_needs_a_recipient: "Choose who the invoice is to before issuing it.",
  invoice_recipient_deleted: "The company or contact this invoice is to has been deleted. Choose another recipient, save, then issue.",
  invoice_recipient_not_live: "That company or contact has been deleted. Choose another recipient.",
  invoice_needs_recipient_address: "Add the recipient’s address before issuing — a VAT invoice must show it.",
  invoice_has_no_lines: "Add at least one line before issuing.",
  invoice_settings_incomplete: "Fill in your business name and address in Invoice settings before issuing.",
  invoice_event_not_live: "That event has been deleted. Choose another event, or none.",
  invoice_entry_not_invoiceable: "One of the lines from event finance can no longer be invoiced — it was deleted, moved or is not income. Reload and try again.",
  invoice_line_invalid: "One of the lines is not valid. Check each description, quantity and price.",
  invoice_lines_invalid: "An invoice can have up to 100 lines.",
  invoice_one_recipient: "Choose one recipient: a company or a contact.",
  invoice_not_issued: "Only an issued invoice can be marked as paid. Reload the page to see where it stands.",
  invoice_paid_on_out_of_range: "Choose a payment date between the issue date and today.",
  invoice_not_voidable: "This invoice can’t be voided. Reload the page to see where it stands.",
  invoice_void_reason_required: "Give a reason for voiding — 500 characters at most.",
  invoice_not_sendable: "Only an issued or paid invoice can be sent.",
  invoice_send_email_invalid: "Enter the email address to send the invoice to.",
  invoice_number_taken: "That invoice number is already used. Change the number prefix in Invoice settings, then issue again.",
  invoice_settings_invalid: "One of the settings is not valid. Check each field and save again.",
};

export function invoiceRefusal(error: { code?: string; hint?: string | null; message: string }): string {
  if (error.hint && Object.hasOwn(INVOICE_REFUSAL_SENTENCES, error.hint)) {
    return INVOICE_REFUSAL_SENTENCES[error.hint];
  }
  if (error.code === "42501") return REFUSED_MESSAGE;
  return FAILED_MESSAGE;
}
```

`lib/invoices/queries.ts` (first loader; later tasks append):
```ts
import "server-only";
// Invoice reads, through the caller's own RLS-bound client: view_finance
// decides what comes back (20261106000000), so another workspace's row is
// simply absent.
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export interface InvoiceSettingsRow {
  businessName: string | null;
  businessAddress: string | null;
  vatNumber: string | null;
  bankAccountName: string | null;
  bankSortCode: string | null;
  bankAccountNumber: string | null;
  numberPrefix: string;
  nextNumber: number;
  paymentTermsDays: number;
}

export async function loadInvoiceSettings(
  organisationId: string,
): Promise<{ outcome: "loaded"; settings: InvoiceSettingsRow | null } | { outcome: "unavailable" }> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("invoice_settings")
    .select("business_name, business_address, vat_number, bank_account_name, bank_sort_code, bank_account_number, number_prefix, next_number, payment_terms_days")
    .eq("organisation_id", organisationId)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (error) {
    console.error("[invoices] settings lookup failed:", error.message);
    return { outcome: "unavailable" };
  }
  if (!data) return { outcome: "loaded", settings: null };
  return {
    outcome: "loaded",
    settings: {
      businessName: data.business_name,
      businessAddress: data.business_address,
      vatNumber: data.vat_number,
      bankAccountName: data.bank_account_name,
      bankSortCode: data.bank_sort_code,
      bankAccountNumber: data.bank_account_number,
      numberPrefix: String(data.number_prefix),
      nextNumber: Number(data.next_number),
      paymentTermsDays: Number(data.payment_terms_days),
    },
  };
}
```

`lib/invoices/settings-actions.ts`:
```ts
"use server";
// Save the workspace's invoice settings. Four gates, in order: the workspace
// (membership), finance WRITE access (manage_finance + Pro + not suspended --
// the same sentence the disabled form shows), STEP-UP re-authentication
// (spec § Permissions: it changes where money is paid), then the form.
// public.save_invoice_settings() re-checks the first two in the database and
// writes the audit rows; a bank change is emailed to the Owners and Admins by
// the delivery tick (lib/email/invoice-notifications.ts).
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { checkStepUp } from "@/lib/auth/step-up-gate";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { echoedValues, field, type FormState } from "@/lib/forms";
import { FAILED_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/crm/write";
import { getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { readSettingsForm } from "@/lib/invoices/settings-form";
import { invoiceRefusal } from "@/lib/invoices/refusals";

const NEEDS_STEP_UP =
  "Confirm it is you before changing invoice settings. Use the prompt on this page, then save again.";

export async function saveInvoiceSettingsAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const values = echoedValues(formData);
  const orgSlug = field(formData, "orgSlug");
  const org = orgSlug ? await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug) : null;
  if (!org) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values };

  const access = await getFinanceWriteAccess(org);
  if (!access.canWrite) return { error: access.explanation, values };
  if (!(await checkStepUp())) return { error: NEEDS_STEP_UP, values };

  const parsed = readSettingsForm(formData);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { fieldErrors: parsed.fieldErrors, values };
  const v = parsed.values;

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("save_invoice_settings", {
    p_org: org.id,
    p_business_name: v.businessName,
    p_business_address: v.businessAddress,
    p_vat_number: v.vatNumber,
    p_bank_account_name: v.bankAccountName,
    p_bank_sort_code: v.bankSortCode,
    p_bank_account_number: v.bankAccountNumber,
    p_number_prefix: v.numberPrefix,
    p_payment_terms_days: v.paymentTermsDays,
  });
  if (error) {
    if (!error.hint) console.error("[invoices] save settings failed:", error.message);
    return { error: invoiceRefusal(error), values };
  }
  revalidatePath(`/${org.slug}/settings/invoicing`);
  return {
    message:
      data === true
        ? "Saved. The bank details changed, so this workspace’s Owners and Admins are being emailed about it."
        : "Saved.",
  };
}
```

`components/invoices/invoice-settings-form.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { saveInvoiceSettingsAction } from "@/lib/invoices/settings-actions";
import { EMPTY_FORM_STATE } from "@/lib/forms";
import type { InvoiceSettingsRow } from "@/lib/invoices/queries";
import { Field, TextAreaField } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";

const NOTE_ID = "invoice-settings-blocked";

export function InvoiceSettingsForm({
  orgSlug,
  settings,
  disabledReason,
}: {
  orgSlug: string;
  settings: InvoiceSettingsRow | null;
  disabledReason: string | null;
}) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(saveInvoiceSettingsAction, EMPTY_FORM_STATE);
  const v = (name: string, stored: string | number | null | undefined) =>
    state.values?.[name] ?? (stored === null || stored === undefined ? "" : String(stored));
  const e = (name: string) => state.fieldErrors?.[name];
  const disabled = disabledReason !== null;

  return (
    <form action={action} className="flex max-w-prose flex-col gap-6" aria-describedby={disabled ? NOTE_ID : undefined}>
      <input type="hidden" name="orgSlug" value={orgSlug} />
      {disabled ? (
        <p id={NOTE_ID} className="border border-rule bg-surface px-3 py-3 text-sm text-ink-muted">
          <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Read-only.</span> {disabledReason}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {state.error ? <Alert tone="error">{state.error}</Alert> : null}
      {state.message ? <Alert tone="success">{state.message}</Alert> : null}

      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        <legend className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">Your business</legend>
        <Field label="Business name" name="businessName" required defaultValue={v("businessName", settings?.businessName)} error={e("businessName")} maxLength={200} />
        <TextAreaField label="Business address" name="businessAddress" required rows={4} defaultValue={v("businessAddress", settings?.businessAddress)} error={e("businessAddress")} maxLength={500} />
        <Field label="VAT number" name="vatNumber" defaultValue={v("vatNumber", settings?.vatNumber)} error={e("vatNumber")} hint="Leave blank if you are not VAT registered: your invoices then charge no VAT and show no VAT column." autoComplete="off" />
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="flex flex-col gap-5 border-t border-rule pt-6">
        <legend className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">Where you are paid</legend>
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">
          Printed on every invoice. Changing them emails every Owner and Admin of this workspace, so a change nobody expected is noticed.
        </p>
        <Field label="Account name" name="bankAccountName" defaultValue={v("bankAccountName", settings?.bankAccountName)} error={e("bankAccountName")} autoComplete="off" />
        <Field label="Sort code" name="bankSortCode" inputMode="numeric" defaultValue={v("bankSortCode", settings?.bankSortCode)} error={e("bankSortCode")} autoComplete="off" className="tabular-nums" />
        <Field label="Account number" name="bankAccountNumber" inputMode="numeric" defaultValue={v("bankAccountNumber", settings?.bankAccountNumber)} error={e("bankAccountNumber")} autoComplete="off" className="tabular-nums" />
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="flex flex-col gap-5 border-t border-rule pt-6">
        <legend className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">Numbering and terms</legend>
        <Field label="Number prefix" name="numberPrefix" required defaultValue={v("numberPrefix", settings?.numberPrefix ?? "INV-")} error={e("numberPrefix")} hint={`The next invoice is number ${settings?.nextNumber ?? 1}. Numbers never repeat and never skip.`} />
        <Field label="Payment terms (days)" name="paymentTermsDays" required inputMode="numeric" defaultValue={v("paymentTermsDays", settings?.paymentTermsDays ?? 30)} error={e("paymentTermsDays")} className="tabular-nums" />
      </fieldset>

      <div>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={disabled} pending={pending} pendingLabel="Saving…" aria-describedby={disabled ? NOTE_ID : undefined}>
          Save invoice settings
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`app/(app)/[orgSlug]/settings/invoicing/page.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { getFinanceReadAccess, getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { loadInvoiceSettings } from "@/lib/invoices/queries";
import { StepUpGate } from "@/components/auth/step-up-gate";
import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { InvoiceSettingsForm } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-settings-form";

// Settings › Invoicing (Invoices part 1). Rendered for view_finance holders
// only (Owner, Admin, Finance); anyone else gets the same not-found as a page
// that does not exist. Saving needs finance WRITE access AND a fresh
// credential (step-up): the gate renders the re-auth prompt when the
// session's last credential is over five minutes old, and the action
// re-checks it server-side either way.
export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/settings/invoicing">): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  return { title: org ? `Invoicing · Settings · ${org.name} · GetSetPaid` : "Not found · GetSetPaid" };
}

export default async function InvoiceSettingsPage({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/settings/invoicing">) {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  if (!org) notFound();
  if (!(await getFinanceReadAccess(org))) notFound();

  const [loaded, access] = await Promise.all([loadInvoiceSettings(org.id), getFinanceWriteAccess(org)]);
  const disabledReason = access.canWrite ? null : access.explanation;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <h1 className="tracking-display text-3xl font-extrabold text-ink">Invoice settings</h1>
        <p className="max-w-prose text-lg text-ink-muted">
          What every invoice from {org.name} says about who it is from and how to pay it.
        </p>
      </div>
      {loaded.outcome === "unavailable" ? (
        <Alert tone="error">We couldn’t load the invoice settings just now. Refresh the page to try again — nothing has changed.</Alert>
      ) : disabledReason === null ? (
        <StepUpGate next={`/${org.slug}/settings/invoicing`}>
          <InvoiceSettingsForm orgSlug={org.slug} settings={loaded.settings} disabledReason={null} />
        </StepUpGate>
      ) : (
        <InvoiceSettingsForm orgSlug={org.slug} settings={loaded.settings} disabledReason={disabledReason} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
// tests/unit/email/invoice-templates.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

  organisationSlug: "neon-nights",
  organisationName: "Neon Nights Ltd",

  changedAt: "2026-10-20T12:00:00.000Z",

};

describe("buildBankDetailsChangedNotice", () => {

    const body = buildBankDetailsChangedNotice(base);

    expect(body.text).toContain("ending 4321");
    expect(body.text).toContain("https://app.example.test/neon-nights/settings/invoicing");
    expect(body.html).not.toMatch(/\b\d{8}\b/);
  });
  it("says the bank details were removed when there is no account any more", () => {
    expect(buildBankDetailsChangedNotice({ ...base, accountLast4: null }).text).toContain("removed");
  });
  it("refuses an input key outside the allowlist, a non-digit last four and a bad slug", () => {
    expect(() => buildBankDetailsChangedNotice({ ...base, extra: "x" } as typeof base)).toThrow();
    expect(() => buildBankDetailsChangedNotice({ ...base, accountLast4: "12ab" })).toThrow();
    expect(() => buildBankDetailsChangedNotice({ ...base, organisationSlug: "../x" })).toThrow();
  });
  it("neutralises a link smuggled into the workspace name", () => {
    const body = buildBankDetailsChangedNotice({ ...base, organisationName: "Evil https://evil.example/login" });
    expect(body.text).not.toContain("https://evil.example");
  });
});
```

```ts
// tests/unit/email/invoice-notifications.test.ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const events = vi.hoisted(() => ({ log: [] as string[] }));
const send = vi.hoisted(() => ({ fn: vi.fn() }));
const db = vi.hoisted(() => ({ rows: [] as unknown[] }));

vi.mock("@/lib/email/send", () => ({
  sendEmail: async (i: { to: { email: string }[]; bcc?: { email: string }[] }) => {
    events.log.push(`send:${[...i.to, ...(i.bcc ?? [])].map((t) => t.email).sort().join("+")}`);
    return send.fn(i);
  },
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/supabase/env", () => ({ siteUrl: () => "https://app.example.test" }));
vi.mock("@/lib/supabase/service", () => ({
  createServiceClient: () => ({
    rpc(name: string, args: { p_notice?: string; p_subject_ids?: string[]; p_reason?: string }) {
      if (name === "mark_notices_delivered") {
        events.log.push(`stamp:${args.p_notice}:${args.p_subject_ids?.join(",")}`);
        return Promise.resolve({ data: 1, error: null });
      }
      if (name === "mark_notices_undeliverable") {
        events.log.push(`refuse:${args.p_notice}:${args.p_subject_ids?.join(",")}:${args.p_reason}`);
        return Promise.resolve({ data: 1, error: null });
      }
      events.log.push(`rpc:${name}`);
      return Promise.resolve({ data: db.rows, error: null });
    },
  }),
}));

const row = (id: string, emails: string[] | null) => ({

});

beforeEach(() => vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {}));

describe("deliverInvoiceBankChangeNotifications", () => {
  it("emails the resolver's Owners and Admins, THEN stamps the one audit row", async () => {

    await expect(deliverInvoiceBankChangeNotifications()).resolves.toEqual({ outcome: "delivered", sent: 1, failed: 0, deferred: 0 });
    expect(events.log).toEqual([

      "send:admin@example.test+owner@example.test",
      "stamp:invoice_bank_details_changed:b1",

  });
  it("a failed send stamps nothing", async () => {
    db.rows = [row("b1", ["owner@example.test"])];
    send.fn.mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, error: "Brevo 503" });

    db.rows = [row("b1", null)];
    await deliverInvoiceBankChangeNotifications();
    expect(events.log).toContain("refuse:invoice_bank_details_changed:b1:no_recipient");
  });
  it("nothing pending is nothing_pending", async () => {
    await expect(deliverInvoiceBankChangeNotifications()).resolves.toEqual({ outcome: "nothing_pending" });
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the template and the deliverer**

`lib/email/invoice-templates.ts`:
```ts
// INVOICE EMAILS, AS PURE BUILDERS WITH AN ALLOWLIST (no I/O; tests/unit/email
// drives it). Task 8: the bank-details-changed notice to a workspace's Owners
// and Admins -- the spec's bank-detail fraud guard. Task 11 adds the invoice
// email itself. Never the full account number: the last four digits only,
// which is what the audit row carries.
import type { EmailBody } from "@/lib/email/templates";
import { NoticeContentError } from "@/lib/email/requester-templates";
import { assertInputAllowlisted, assertOnlyLink, oneLine, origin, render, slugOrRefuse, ukDateTime } from "@/lib/email/notice-render";

export const INVOICE_NOTICE_ALLOWLIST = {
  bankDetailsChanged: ["organisationSlug", "organisationName", "accountLast4", "changedAt", "siteUrl"],
} as const;

export interface BankDetailsChangedInput {
  readonly organisationSlug: string;
  readonly organisationName: string;
  readonly accountLast4: string | null;
  readonly changedAt: string;
  readonly siteUrl: string;
}

export function buildBankDetailsChangedNotice(input: BankDetailsChangedInput): EmailBody {
  assertInputAllowlisted("bankDetailsChanged", INVOICE_NOTICE_ALLOWLIST.bankDetailsChanged, input);
  const slug = slugOrRefuse("bankDetailsChanged", input.organisationSlug);
  if (input.accountLast4 !== null && !/^\d{4}$/.test(input.accountLast4)) {
    throw new NoticeContentError("bankDetailsChanged notice refused: last four is not four digits");
  }
  const name = oneLine(input.organisationName, 120);
  const label = name ? `“${name}” (${slug})` : slug;
  const link = `${origin(input.siteUrl)}/${slug}/settings/invoicing`;
  const what =
    input.accountLast4 === null
      ? "The bank details printed on its invoices were removed."
      : `Its invoices now ask to be paid into the account ending ${input.accountLast4}.`;
  const body = render(
    `[GetSetPaid] The bank details on ${slug}'s invoices were changed`,
    [
      `The bank details on invoices from the workspace ${label} were changed on ${ukDateTime(input.changedAt)}.`,
      what,
      "If you expected this, there is nothing to do. If you did not, open the invoice settings now and check them before another invoice is sent.",
    ],
    link,
    "Open invoice settings",
  );
  assertOnlyLink("bankDetailsChanged", body, link);
  return body;
}
```
`lib/email/invoice-notifications.ts`:
```ts
import "server-only";
// THE BANK-CHANGE NOTICE (Invoices part 1, spec § Security 2) through the ONE
// Brevo sender, on the existing delivery tick. Database half: 20261106004000
// (the resolver, the ledger notice). Same loop, stamps and bounds as
// lib/email/workspace-closure-notifications.ts: one email per audit row, its
// recipients Bcc'd by deliverEach, the stamp written only after ok:true.
import {
  deliverEach,
  REQUESTER_NOTICE_LIMIT,
  type NoticeRefusal,
  type Pending,
  type RequesterNoticeResult,
} from "@/lib/email/requester-notifications";
import { createServiceClient } from "@/lib/supabase/service";
import { siteUrl } from "@/lib/supabase/env";
import { buildBankDetailsChangedNotice } from "@/lib/email/invoice-templates";

const NOTICE = "invoice_bank_details_changed";

interface PendingRow {
  readonly audit_event_id: string;
  readonly emails: string[] | null;
  readonly organisation_slug: string;
  readonly organisation_name: string;
  readonly account_last4: string | null;
  readonly changed_at: string;
}

export async function deliverInvoiceBankChangeNotifications(
  limit: number = REQUESTER_NOTICE_LIMIT,
): Promise<RequesterNoticeResult> {
  const service = createServiceClient();
  const { data, error } = await service.rpc("pending_invoice_bank_change_notifications", {
    p_limit: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, REQUESTER_NOTICE_LIMIT)),
  });
  if (error) {
    console.error("[email] invoice bank-change notices could not be read:", error.message);
    return { outcome: "unavailable", error: error.message };
  }
  const origin = siteUrl();
  const rows = ((data ?? []) as PendingRow[]).map((row): Pending => ({
    id: row.audit_event_id,
    to: row.emails ?? [],
    // FIVE FIELDS, by name -- never a spread of the row.
    build: () =>
      buildBankDetailsChangedNotice({
        organisationSlug: row.organisation_slug,
        organisationName: row.organisation_name,
        accountLast4: row.account_last4,
        changedAt: row.changed_at,
        siteUrl: origin,
      }),
    stamp: async () => {
      const { data: stamped, error: stampError } = await createServiceClient().rpc("mark_notices_delivered", {
        p_notice: NOTICE,
        p_subject_ids: [row.audit_event_id],
      });
      if (stampError) return { error: { message: stampError.message } };
      if (stamped !== 1) return { error: { message: `stamped ${String(stamped)} of 1` } };
      return { error: null };
    },
    refuse: async (reason: NoticeRefusal) => {
      const { error: refuseError } = await createServiceClient().rpc("mark_notices_undeliverable", {
        p_notice: NOTICE,
        p_subject_ids: [row.audit_event_id],
        p_reason: reason,
      });
      return { error: refuseError ? { message: refuseError.message } : null };
    },
  }));
  return deliverEach("invoice bank-change notice", rows);
}
```
