```ts
// tests/unit/invoices/draft-form.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readDraftForm } from "@/lib/invoices/draft-form";

const CONTACT = "11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555";
const ENTRY = "99999999-2222-4333-8444-555555555555";

function form(top: Record<string, string>, lines: Record<string, string>[]): FormData {
  const d = new FormData();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(top)) d.set(k, v);
  for (const l of lines) {
    d.append("line_description", l.description ?? "");
    d.append("line_quantity", l.quantity ?? "");
    d.append("line_unit_price", l.unitPrice ?? "");
    d.append("line_vat_rate", l.vatRate ?? "");
    d.append("line_entry_id", l.entryId ?? "");
  }
  return d;
}

describe("readDraftForm", () => {
  it("reads the recipient, the event and the lines, normalising quantity and price", () => {
    const r = readDraftForm(form(
      { recipient: `contact:${CONTACT}`, eventId: "", recipientAddress: " 1 Road\nLondon ", supplyDate: "2026-10-18", notes: "" },
      [
        { description: "Guarantee", quantity: "1", unitPrice: "3,000", vatRate: "standard_20", entryId: ENTRY },
        { description: "Production", quantity: "2.5", unitPrice: "£150.00", vatRate: "none" },
      ],
    ));
    expect(r).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      draft: {
        recipient: { kind: "contact", id: CONTACT },
        eventId: null,
        recipientAddress: "1 Road\nLondon",
        supplyDate: "2026-10-18",
        notes: null,
        lines: [
          { description: "Guarantee", quantity: "1.00", unitPriceMinor: 300000, vatRate: "standard_20", financialEntryId: ENTRY },
          { description: "Production", quantity: "2.50", unitPriceMinor: 15000, vatRate: "none", financialEntryId: null },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  it("a draft may have no recipient and no lines yet", () => {
    const r = readDraftForm(form({ recipient: "" }, []));
    expect(r.ok && r.draft).toMatchObject({ recipient: null, lines: [] });
  });

  it("keys each line's error by its index and field", () => {
    const r = readDraftForm(form({ recipient: "" }, [
      { description: "", quantity: "0", unitPrice: "-1", vatRate: "standard" },
    ]));
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (r.ok) return;
    expect(Object.keys(r.fieldErrors).sort()).toEqual(
      ["line_0_description", "line_0_quantity", "line_0_unit_price", "line_0_vat_rate"].sort(),
    );
  });

  it("refuses a forged recipient, a forged entry id, an impossible date and 101 lines", () => {
    expect(readDraftForm(form({ recipient: "company:not-a-uuid" }, [])).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(readDraftForm(form({ recipient: "" }, [{ description: "x", quantity: "1", unitPrice: "1", vatRate: "none", entryId: "'; drop" }])).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(readDraftForm(form({ recipient: "", supplyDate: "2026-02-31" }, [])).ok).toBe(false);
    const many = Array.from({ length: 101 }, () => ({ description: "x", quantity: "1", unitPrice: "1", vatRate: "none" }));
    expect(readDraftForm(form({ recipient: "" }, many)).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it("refuses ragged line arrays (a tampered form) as a whole", () => {
    const d = form({ recipient: "" }, [{ description: "x", quantity: "1", unitPrice: "1", vatRate: "none" }]);
    d.append("line_description", "orphan");
    expect(readDraftForm(d).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `lib/invoices/draft-form.ts` and `lib/invoices/list.ts`**

```ts
// lib/invoices/draft-form.ts
// The draft editor's form, read and validated. PURE. The database
// (public.save_invoice_draft) re-checks everything that matters; this is
// the sentence beside the field. Lines arrive as five parallel repeated
// fields, one entry per line in order; ragged arrays mean a tampered form.
import { field } from "@/lib/forms";
import { parseGbpDetailed } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { formatQuantity, isVatRate, lineAmounts, parseQuantity, type VatRate } from "@/lib/invoices/arithmetic";

export const MAX_LINES = 100;
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/;

export interface DraftLine {
  description: string;
  quantity: string;
  unitPriceMinor: number;
  vatRate: VatRate;
  financialEntryId: string | null;
}

export interface DraftInput {
  recipient: { kind: "company" | "contact"; id: string } | null;
  eventId: string | null;
  recipientAddress: string | null;
  supplyDate: string | null;
  notes: string | null;
  lines: DraftLine[];
}

function isCalendarDate(raw: string): boolean {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw)) return false;
  const [y, m, d] = raw.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
  return date.getUTCFullYear() === y && date.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 && date.getUTCDate() === d;
}

function multiline(data: FormData, name: string): string | null {
  const raw = data.get(name);
  if (typeof raw !== "string") return null;
  const trimmed = raw.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").trim();
  return trimmed === "" ? null : trimmed;
}

const strings = (data: FormData, name: string) =>
  data.getAll(name).map((v) => (typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : ""));

export function readDraftForm(
  formData: FormData,
): { ok: true; draft: DraftInput } | { ok: false; fieldErrors: Record<string, string> } {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};

  let recipient: DraftInput["recipient"] = null;
  const recipientRaw = field(formData, "recipient");
  if (recipientRaw) {
    const match = /^(company|contact):(.+)$/.exec(recipientRaw);
    if (match && UUID.test(match[2])) recipient = { kind: match[1] as "company" | "contact", id: match[2] };
    else errors.recipient = "Choose a recipient from the list.";
  }

  const eventId = field(formData, "eventId");
  if (eventId && !UUID.test(eventId)) errors.eventId = "Choose an event from the list.";

  const recipientAddress = multiline(formData, "recipientAddress");
  if (recipientAddress && recipientAddress.length > 500) {
    errors.recipientAddress = "That address is too long — 500 characters at most.";
  }

  const supplyDate = field(formData, "supplyDate");
  if (supplyDate && !isCalendarDate(supplyDate)) errors.supplyDate = "That doesn’t look like a date.";

  const notes = multiline(formData, "notes");
  if (notes && notes.length > 2000) errors.notes = "Those notes are too long — 2,000 characters at most.";

  const descriptions = strings(formData, "line_description");
  const quantities = strings(formData, "line_quantity");
  const prices = strings(formData, "line_unit_price");
  const rates = strings(formData, "line_vat_rate");
  const entries = strings(formData, "line_entry_id");
  const lines: DraftLine[] = [];
  const n = descriptions.length;
  if ([quantities, prices, rates, entries].some((a) => a.length !== n)) {
    errors.lines = "The lines didn’t arrive intact. Reload the page and try again.";
  } else if (n > MAX_LINES) {
    errors.lines = `An invoice can have up to ${MAX_LINES} lines.`;
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const description = descriptions[i];
      if (description === "") errors[`line_${i}_description`] = "Describe this line.";
      else if (description.length > 500) errors[`line_${i}_description`] = "500 characters at most.";
      const q = parseQuantity(quantities[i]);
      if (q === null) errors[`line_${i}_quantity`] = "A quantity above zero, up to two decimal places.";
      const price = parseGbpDetailed(prices[i] === "" ? null : prices[i]);
      if (!price.ok) errors[`line_${i}_unit_price`] = "A price in pounds, up to two decimal places.";
      const rate = rates[i];
      if (!isVatRate(rate)) errors[`line_${i}_vat_rate`] = "Choose a VAT rate from the list.";
      const entry = entries[i] === "" ? null : entries[i];
      if (entry !== null && !UUID.test(entry)) errors.lines = "The lines didn’t arrive intact. Reload the page and try again.";
      if (q !== null && price.ok && isVatRate(rate)) {
        try {
          lineAmounts(q, price.minor, rate);
          lines.push({ description, quantity: formatQuantity(q), unitPriceMinor: price.minor, vatRate: rate, financialEntryId: entry });
        } catch {
          errors[`line_${i}_unit_price`] = "That line’s total is too large.";
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return { ok: false, fieldErrors: errors };
  return { ok: true, draft: { recipient, eventId, recipientAddress, supplyDate, notes, lines } };
}
```
```ts
// lib/invoices/list.ts
// The invoices list. Newest first on invoices_org_created_id_idx (a draft has
// no number, so the number cannot be the sort). No owner filter: invoices
// are read by view_finance holders, who are all full-scope.
import { defineListSpec } from "@/lib/list/spec";
import type { ListParams } from "@/lib/list/params";
import { INVOICE_STATUSES, INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS, type InvoiceStatus } from "@/lib/invoices/status";

/** A literal. The recipient's name comes through the two composite FKs. */
export const INVOICE_SELECT =
  "id, status, number, gross_minor, issue_date, due_date, created_at, company:companies!invoices_recipient_company_fkey(name), contact:contacts!invoices_recipient_contact_fkey(name)";

export interface InvoiceRow {
  id: string;
  status: InvoiceStatus;
  number: string | null;
  grossMinor: number;
  issueDate: string | null;
  dueDate: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  recipientName: string | null;
}

function embeddedName(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return null;
  const name = (value as { name?: unknown }).name;
  return typeof name === "string" ? name : null;
}

export function mapInvoiceRow(row: Record<string, unknown>): InvoiceRow {
  return {
    id: String(row.id),
    status: row.status as InvoiceStatus,
    number: (row.number as string | null) ?? null,
    grossMinor: Number(row.gross_minor),
    issueDate: (row.issue_date as string | null) ?? null,
    dueDate: (row.due_date as string | null) ?? null,
    createdAt: String(row.created_at),
    recipientName: embeddedName(row.company) ?? embeddedName(row.contact),
  };
}

export const INVOICE_LIST = defineListSpec({
  entity: "invoices",
  noun: { one: "invoice", many: "invoices" },
  searchColumn: "number",
  searchLabel: "Search by invoice number",
  sort: {
    created: {
      column: "created_at",
      label: "Created",
      defaultDirection: "desc",
      directionLabels: { asc: "oldest first", desc: "newest first" },
      notNull: true,
      backedBy: "invoices_org_created_id_idx",
    },
  },
  defaultSort: "created",
  filters: {
    status: {
      label: "Status",
      choices: [
        { value: "any", label: "Any status" },
        ...INVOICE_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS[s] })),
      ],
    },
  },
});

export type InvoiceSortKey = keyof (typeof INVOICE_LIST)["sort"];
export type InvoiceFilterKey = keyof (typeof INVOICE_LIST)["filters"];
export type InvoiceParams = ListParams<InvoiceSortKey, InvoiceFilterKey>;
```

Append to `lib/invoices/queries.ts`:
```ts
import type { VatRate } from "@/lib/invoices/arithmetic";
import type { InvoiceStatus } from "@/lib/invoices/status";

export interface InvoiceLineRow {
  position: number;
  description: string;
  quantity: string;
  unitPriceMinor: number;
  vatRate: VatRate;
  financialEntryId: string | null;
  netMinor: number;
  vatMinor: number;
}

export interface InvoiceDetail {
  id: string;
  status: InvoiceStatus;
  number: string | null;
  recipientCompanyId: string | null;
  recipientContactId: string | null;
  recipientAddress: string | null;
  eventId: string | null;
  supplyDate: string | null;
  notes: string | null;
  netMinor: number;
  vatMinor: number;
  grossMinor: number;
  snapshot: unknown;
  paidOn: string | null;
  voidedAt: string | null;
  voidReason: string | null;
  lines: InvoiceLineRow[];
}

const INVOICE_DETAIL_SELECT =
  "id, status, number, recipient_company_id, recipient_contact_id, recipient_address, event_id, supply_date, notes, net_minor, vat_minor, gross_minor, snapshot, paid_on, voided_at, void_reason, deleted_at, invoice_lines(position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, financial_entry_id, net_minor, vat_minor)";

/** One invoice of this workspace through the caller's RLS. A soft-deleted
 *  draft, another tenant's id, an unparseable id and an absent one are all
 *  "missing" -- the page answers each with the same not-found. */
export async function loadInvoice(
  organisationId: string,
  invoiceId: string,
): Promise<{ outcome: "loaded"; invoice: InvoiceDetail } | { outcome: "missing" } | { outcome: "unavailable" }> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("invoices")
    .select(INVOICE_DETAIL_SELECT)
    .eq("organisation_id", organisationId)
    .eq("id", invoiceId)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === "22P02") return { outcome: "missing" };
    console.error("[invoices] invoice lookup failed:", error.message);
    return { outcome: "unavailable" };
  }
  if (!data || data.deleted_at !== null) return { outcome: "missing" };
  const lines = ((data.invoice_lines ?? []) as Record<string, unknown>[])
    .map((l) => ({
      position: Number(l.position),
      description: String(l.description),
      quantity: Number(l.quantity).toFixed(2),
      unitPriceMinor: Number(l.unit_price_minor),
      vatRate: l.vat_rate as VatRate,
      financialEntryId: (l.financial_entry_id as string | null) ?? null,
      netMinor: Number(l.net_minor),
      vatMinor: Number(l.vat_minor),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);
  return {
    outcome: "loaded",
    invoice: {
      id: String(data.id),
      status: data.status as InvoiceStatus,
      number: data.number ?? null,
      recipientCompanyId: data.recipient_company_id ?? null,
      recipientContactId: data.recipient_contact_id ?? null,
      recipientAddress: data.recipient_address ?? null,
      eventId: data.event_id ?? null,
      supplyDate: data.supply_date ?? null,
      notes: data.notes ?? null,
      netMinor: Number(data.net_minor),
      vatMinor: Number(data.vat_minor),
      grossMinor: Number(data.gross_minor),
      snapshot: data.snapshot,
      paidOn: data.paid_on ?? null,
      voidedAt: data.voided_at ?? null,
      voidReason: data.void_reason ?? null,
      lines,
    },
  };
}
```

Create `lib/invoices/actions.ts`:
```ts
"use server";
// Invoice writes. Every one: resolve the workspace from the slug (never a
// client-supplied org id), check finance WRITE access (manage_finance + Pro +
// not suspended -- the same sentence the disabled controls show, so the two
// paths never disagree), then call the SECURITY DEFINER RPC that re-checks
// all of it in the database. Every refusal echoes the submitted values.
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getCurrentOrg, type CurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { echoedValues, field, type FormState } from "@/lib/forms";
import { FAILED_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/crm/write";
import { getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { readDraftForm } from "@/lib/invoices/draft-form";
import { invoiceRefusal } from "@/lib/invoices/refusals";

async function authorise(
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<{ ok: true; org: CurrentOrg } | { ok: false; state: FormState }> {
  const values = echoedValues(formData);
  const orgSlug = field(formData, "orgSlug");
  const org = orgSlug ? await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug) : null;
  if (!org) return { ok: false, state: { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values } };
  const access = await getFinanceWriteAccess(org);
  if (!access.canWrite) return { ok: false, state: { error: access.explanation, values } };
  return { ok: true, org };
}

function refused(error: { code?: string; hint?: string | null; message: string }, context: string, formData: FormData): FormState {
  // A hint is an expected refusal with its own sentence; anything else is a fault.
  if (!error.hint) console.error(`[invoices] ${context} failed:`, error.message);
  return { error: invoiceRefusal(error), values: echoedValues(formData) };
}

export async function saveInvoiceDraftAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const auth = await authorise(formData);
  if (!auth.ok) return auth.state;
  const parsed = readDraftForm(formData);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { fieldErrors: parsed.fieldErrors, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  const d = parsed.draft;
  const invoiceId = field(formData, "invoiceId");

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("save_invoice_draft", {
    p_org: auth.org.id,
    p_invoice_id: invoiceId,
    p_recipient_company_id: d.recipient?.kind === "company" ? d.recipient.id : null,
    p_recipient_contact_id: d.recipient?.kind === "contact" ? d.recipient.id : null,
    p_event_id: d.eventId,
    p_recipient_address: d.recipientAddress,
    p_supply_date: d.supplyDate,
    p_notes: d.notes,
    p_lines: d.lines.map((l) => ({
      description: l.description,
      quantity: l.quantity,
      unit_price_minor: l.unitPriceMinor,
      vat_rate: l.vatRate,
      financial_entry_id: l.financialEntryId,
    })),
  });
  if (error) return refused(error, "save draft", formData);

  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
  if (!invoiceId) redirect(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices/${String(data)}`);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices/${invoiceId}`);
  return { message: "Draft saved." };
}

export async function deleteInvoiceDraftAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const auth = await authorise(formData);
  if (!auth.ok) return auth.state;
  const invoiceId = field(formData, "invoiceId");
  if (!invoiceId) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("delete_invoice_draft", { p_invoice_id: invoiceId });
  if (error) return refused(error, "delete draft", formData);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
  redirect(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
}
```

```tsx
import { INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS, type InvoiceStatus } from "@/lib/invoices/status";

// The label is the signal; the tone only reinforces it (DESIGN.md: never colour alone).
const TONE = { draft: "revoked", issued: "pending", paid: "active", void: "revoked" } as const;
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { EMPTY_FORM_STATE, type FormState } from "@/lib/forms";
import { deleteInvoiceDraftAction } from "@/lib/invoices/actions";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";

/** The one explanation every disabled invoice control points at (rendered by the page). */
export const BLOCKED_NOTE_ID = "invoice-blocked";

type Action = (prev: FormState, formData: FormData) => Promise<FormState>;

function ActionForm({
  action,
  orgSlug,
  invoiceId,
  submitLabel,
  pendingLabel,
  variant = "secondary",
  disabledReason,
  fields,
}: {
  action: Action;
  orgSlug: string;
  invoiceId: string;
  submitLabel: string;
  pendingLabel: string;
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "danger";
  disabledReason: string | null;
  fields?: (state: FormState) => ReactNode;
}) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(action, EMPTY_FORM_STATE);
  const disabled = disabledReason !== null;
  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <input type="hidden" name="orgSlug" value={orgSlug} />
      <input type="hidden" name="invoiceId" value={invoiceId} />
      {fields ? <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="flex flex-col gap-3">{fields(state)}</fieldset> : null}
      {state.error ? <Alert tone="error">{state.error}</Alert> : null}
      {state.message ? <Alert tone="success">{state.message}</Alert> : null}
      <div>
        <Button
          type="submit"
          variant={variant}
          disabled={disabled}
          pending={pending}
          pendingLabel={pendingLabel}
          aria-describedby={disabled ? BLOCKED_NOTE_ID : undefined}
        >
          {submitLabel}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

export function DeleteDraftForm(props: { orgSlug: string; invoiceId: string; disabledReason: string | null }) {
  return <ActionForm {...props} action={deleteInvoiceDraftAction} submitLabel="Delete draft" pendingLabel="Deleting…" variant="danger" />;
}
```

`components/invoices/invoice-editor.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { saveInvoiceDraftAction } from "@/lib/invoices/actions";
import { EMPTY_FORM_STATE } from "@/lib/forms";
import { VAT_RATES, VAT_RATE_LABELS, invoiceTotals, lineAmounts, parseQuantity, type LineAmounts, type VatRate } from "@/lib/invoices/arithmetic";
import { formatGbp, parseGbpToMinor } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { MAX_LINES } from "@/lib/invoices/draft-form";
import { Field, SelectField, TextAreaField } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { BLOCKED_NOTE_ID } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-actions";

// THE DRAFT EDITOR. The line rows are CONTROLLED (state), because adding and
// removing rows and the live totals need JavaScript anyway, and controlled
// rows survive React 19's post-action form reset. The live totals use the
// TypeScript port of the database's arithmetic (lib/invoices/arithmetic.ts);
// the numbers that are stored are the database's.

export interface PickerOption { id: string; name: string }
export interface EditorLine { description: string; quantity: string; unitPrice: string; vatRate: VatRate; entryId: string }
export interface EditorInitial {
  recipient: string;
  eventId: string;
  recipientAddress: string;
  supplyDate: string;
  notes: string;
  lines: EditorLine[];
}

interface Row extends EditorLine { key: number }

const CONTROL =
  "h-11 w-full min-w-0 border bg-surface px-3 text-base text-ink disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-55 ";
const ok = "border-rule hover:border-ink focus:border-accent";
const bad = "border-danger";

export const EMPTY_LINE: EditorLine = { description: "", quantity: "1", unitPrice: "", vatRate: "standard_20", entryId: "" };

export function InvoiceEditor({
  orgSlug,
  invoiceId,
  companies,
  contacts,
  events,
  vatRegistered,
  initial,
  disabledReason,
}: {
  orgSlug: string;
  invoiceId: string | null;
  companies: readonly PickerOption[];
  contacts: readonly PickerOption[];
  events: readonly PickerOption[];
  vatRegistered: boolean;
  initial: EditorInitial;
  disabledReason: string | null;
}) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(saveInvoiceDraftAction, EMPTY_FORM_STATE);
  const nextKey = useRef(initial.lines.length);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>(() => initial.lines.map((l, i) => ({ ...l, key: i })));
  const focusKey = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const disabled = disabledReason !== null;
  const echo = (name: string, fallback: string) => state.values?.[name] ?? fallback;
  const err = (name: string) => state.fieldErrors?.[name];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusKey.current === null) return;
    document.getElementById(`line-${focusKey.current}-description`)?.focus();
    focusKey.current = null;
  }, [rows]);

  const amounts: (LineAmounts | null)[] = useMemo(
    () =>
      rows.map((r) => {
        const q = parseQuantity(r.quantity);
        const p = parseGbpToMinor(r.unitPrice);
        if (q === null || p === null) return null;
        try {
          return lineAmounts(q, p, vatRegistered ? r.vatRate : "none");
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      }),
    [rows, vatRegistered],
  );
  const totals = invoiceTotals(amounts.filter((a): a is LineAmounts => a !== null));

  const update = (key: number, patch: Partial<EditorLine>) =>
    setRows((current) => current.map((r) => (r.key === key ? { ...r, ...patch } : r)));
  const addLine = () => {
    const key = nextKey.current++;
    focusKey.current = key;
    setRows((current) => [...current, { ...EMPTY_LINE, vatRate: vatRegistered ? "standard_20" : "none", key }]);
  };
  const removeLine = (key: number) => setRows((current) => current.filter((r) => r.key !== key));

  const recipientIds = new Set([...companies.map((c) => `company:${c.id}`), ...contacts.map((c) => `contact:${c.id}`)]);
  const eventIds = new Set(events.map((e) => e.id));

  return (
    <form action={action} className="flex flex-col gap-8" aria-describedby={disabled ? BLOCKED_NOTE_ID : undefined}>
      <input type="hidden" name="orgSlug" value={orgSlug} />
      {invoiceId ? <input type="hidden" name="invoiceId" value={invoiceId} /> : null}
      {state.error ? <Alert tone="error">{state.error}</Alert> : null}
      {state.message ? <Alert tone="success">{state.message}</Alert> : null}
      {err("lines") ? <Alert tone="error">{err("lines")}</Alert> : null}

      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <legend className="sr-only">Who and what for</legend>
        <SelectField label="Recipient" name="recipient" defaultValue={echo("recipient", initial.recipient)} error={err("recipient")} key={`r-${echo("recipient", initial.recipient)}`}>
          <option value="">Choose later</option>
          {initial.recipient && !recipientIds.has(initial.recipient) ? (
            <option value={initial.recipient}>Current recipient (no longer in the list)</option>
          ) : null}
          <optgroup label="Companies">
            {companies.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={`company:${c.id}`}>{c.name}</option>)}
          </optgroup>
          <optgroup label="Contacts">
            {contacts.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={`contact:${c.id}`}>{c.name}</option>)}
          </optgroup>
        </SelectField>
        <SelectField label="Event" name="eventId" defaultValue={echo("eventId", initial.eventId)} error={err("eventId")} key={`e-${echo("eventId", initial.eventId)}`}>
          <option value="">No event</option>
          {initial.eventId && !eventIds.has(initial.eventId) ? (
            <option value={initial.eventId}>Current event (no longer in the list)</option>
          ) : null}
          {events.map((e) => <option key={e.id} value={e.id}>{e.name}</option>)}
        </SelectField>
        <TextAreaField label="Recipient address" name="recipientAddress" rows={3} maxLength={500} defaultValue={echo("recipientAddress", initial.recipientAddress)} error={err("recipientAddress")} hint="Required before issuing: a VAT invoice shows the customer’s address." />
        <Field label="Supply date" name="supplyDate" type="date" defaultValue={echo("supplyDate", initial.supplyDate)} error={err("supplyDate")} hint="Leave blank if it is the issue date." />
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-rule pt-6">
        <legend className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">Lines</legend>
        {!vatRegistered ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">No VAT is charged: this workspace has no VAT number in Invoice settings.</p>
        ) : null}
        <ol className="flex flex-col">
          {rows.map((row, index) => {
            const n = index + 1;
            const a = amounts[index];
            return (
              <li key={row.key} className="grid gap-2 border-b border-rule py-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_6rem_8rem_10rem_7rem_auto] sm:items-start">
                <input type="hidden" name="line_entry_id" value={row.entryId} />
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <input id={`line-${row.key}-description`} name="line_description" aria-label={`Line ${n} description`} value={row.description} maxLength={500}
                    onChange={(e) => update(row.key, { description: e.target.value })}
                    aria-invalid={err(`line_${index}_description`) ? true : undefined}
                    className={CONTROL + (err(`line_${index}_description`) ? bad : ok)} />
                  {row.entryId ? <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted">From event finance</span> : null}
                  {err(`line_${index}_description`) ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{err(`line_${index}_description`)}</p> : null}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <input name="line_quantity" aria-label={`Line ${n} quantity`} inputMode="decimal" value={row.quantity}
                    onChange={(e) => update(row.key, { quantity: e.target.value })}
                    aria-invalid={err(`line_${index}_quantity`) ? true : undefined}
                    className={CONTROL + "tabular-nums " + (err(`line_${index}_quantity`) ? bad : ok)} />
                  {err(`line_${index}_quantity`) ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{err(`line_${index}_quantity`)}</p> : null}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <input name="line_unit_price" aria-label={`Line ${n} unit price`} inputMode="decimal" value={row.unitPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => update(row.key, { unitPrice: e.target.value })}
                    aria-invalid={err(`line_${index}_unit_price`) ? true : undefined}
                    className={CONTROL + "tabular-nums " + (err(`line_${index}_unit_price`) ? bad : ok)} />
                  {err(`line_${index}_unit_price`) ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{err(`line_${index}_unit_price`)}</p> : null}
                </div>
                {vatRegistered ? (
                  <select name="line_vat_rate" aria-label={`Line ${n} VAT`} value={row.vatRate}
                    onChange={(e) => update(row.key, { vatRate: e.target.value as VatRate })}
                    className={CONTROL + (err(`line_${index}_vat_rate`) ? bad : ok)}>
                    {VAT_RATES.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r}>{VAT_RATE_LABELS[r]}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : (
                  <input type="hidden" name="line_vat_rate" value="none" />
                )}
                <p className="h-11 content-center text-right text-base text-ink tabular-nums" aria-label={`Line ${n} net`}>
                  {a ? formatGbp(a.netMinor) : "—"}
                </p>
                <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => removeLine(row.key)} aria-label={`Remove line ${n}`}>
                  Remove
                </Button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ol>
        <div>
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={addLine} disabled={rows.length >= MAX_LINES}>
            Add line
          </Button>
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <section aria-label="Totals" aria-live="polite" className="ml-auto w-full max-w-xs border-t border-rule pt-4">
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-y-1 text-base tabular-nums">
          <dt className="text-ink-muted">Net</dt>
          <dd className="text-right text-ink">{formatGbp(totals.netMinor)}</dd>
          {vatRegistered ? (
            <>
              <dt className="text-ink-muted">VAT</dt>
              <dd className="text-right text-ink">{formatGbp(totals.vatMinor)}</dd>
            </>
          ) : null}
          <dt className="font-bold text-ink">Total</dt>
          <dd className="text-right font-bold text-ink">{formatGbp(totals.grossMinor)}</dd>
        </dl>
      </section>

      <fieldset disabled={disabled}>
        <TextAreaField label="Notes" name="notes" rows={3} maxLength={2000} defaultValue={echo("notes", initial.notes)} error={err("notes")} hint="Printed on the invoice." />
      </fieldset>

      <div>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={disabled} pending={pending} pendingLabel="Saving…" aria-describedby={disabled ? BLOCKED_NOTE_ID : undefined}>
          Save draft
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`components/invoices/invoice-view.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { InvoiceSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";
import { VAT_RATE_LABELS } from "@/lib/invoices/arithmetic";
import { formatGbp } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { formatDateOnly } from "@/lib/format/date";

// The issued invoice, read-only, rendered ONLY from its snapshot -- the same
// source as the PDF, so the screen and the paper cannot disagree.
export function InvoiceView({ snapshot: s }: { snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-x-8 gap-y-4 border-t border-rule pt-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <dt className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.04em] text-ink-muted uppercase">From</dt>
          <dd className="whitespace-pre-line text-base text-ink">
            {s.seller.name}{"\n"}{s.seller.address}{s.seller.vatNumber ? `\nVAT ${s.seller.vatNumber}` : ""}
          </dd>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <dt className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.04em] text-ink-muted uppercase">To</dt>
          <dd className="whitespace-pre-line text-base text-ink">{s.recipient.name}{"\n"}{s.recipient.address}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <dt className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.04em] text-ink-muted uppercase">Issued</dt>
          <dd className="text-base text-ink tabular-nums">{formatDateOnly(s.issueDate)}</dd>
        </div>
        {s.supplyDate !== s.issueDate ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <dt className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.04em] text-ink-muted uppercase">Supply date</dt>
            <dd className="text-base text-ink tabular-nums">{formatDateOnly(s.supplyDate)}</dd>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <dt className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.04em] text-ink-muted uppercase">Due</dt>
          <dd className="text-base text-ink tabular-nums">{formatDateOnly(s.dueDate)}</dd>
        </div>
      </dl>

      <div role="region" aria-label="Invoice lines" tabIndex={0} className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full border-collapse text-base">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-rule text-left text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.04em] text-ink-muted uppercase">
              <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-4">Description</th>
              <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-4 text-right">Qty</th>
              <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-4 text-right">Unit price</th>
              {s.vatRegistered ? <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-4">VAT</th> : null}
              <th scope="col" className="py-2 text-right">Net</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {s.lines.map((l) => (
              <tr key={l.position} className="border-b border-rule">
                <td className="py-2 pr-4 text-ink">{l.description}</td>
                <td className="py-2 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{l.quantity}</td>
                <td className="py-2 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{formatGbp(l.unitPriceMinor)}</td>
                {s.vatRegistered ? <td className="py-2 pr-4">{VAT_RATE_LABELS[l.vatRate]}</td> : null}
                <td className="py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{formatGbp(l.netMinor)}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <dl className="ml-auto grid w-full max-w-xs grid-cols-2 gap-y-1 text-base tabular-nums">
        <dt className="text-ink-muted">Net</dt>
        <dd className="text-right">{formatGbp(s.totals.netMinor)}</dd>
        {s.vatRegistered ? (
          <>
            <dt className="text-ink-muted">VAT</dt>
            <dd className="text-right">{formatGbp(s.totals.vatMinor)}</dd>
          </>
        ) : null}
        <dt className="font-bold text-ink">Total</dt>
        <dd className="text-right font-bold text-ink">{formatGbp(s.totals.grossMinor)}</dd>
      </dl>

      <section aria-labelledby="pay-heading" className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-rule pt-6">
        <h2 id="pay-heading" className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">How to pay</h2>
        {s.seller.bank ? (
          <p className="text-base text-ink tabular-nums">
            Bank transfer to {s.seller.bank.accountName}, sort code {s.seller.bank.sortCode}, account {s.seller.bank.accountNumber}, quoting {s.number}.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-base text-ink-muted">No bank details were set when this invoice was issued.</p>
        )}
        {s.notes ? <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-base text-ink-muted">{s.notes}</p> : null}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
function invoiceColumns(orgSlug: string): readonly ListColumn<InvoiceRow, InvoiceSortKey>[] {
  return [
    {
      key: "number",
      header: "Invoice",
      render: (row) => (
        <Link href={`/${orgSlug}/invoices/${row.id}`} className="font-semibold text-primary underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink tabular-nums">
          {row.number ?? "Draft"}
        </Link>
      ),
    },
    { key: "status", header: "Status", render: (row) => <InvoiceStatusChip status={row.status} /> },
    { key: "recipient", header: "To", render: (row) => row.recipientName ?? <Absent label="No recipient yet" /> },
    { key: "total", header: "Total", numeric: true, render: (row) => <span className="tabular-nums">{formatGbp(row.grossMinor)}</span> },
    {
      key: "due",
      header: "Due",
      numeric: true,
      render: (row) => (row.dueDate ? <span className="tabular-nums">{formatDateOnly(row.dueDate)}</span> : <Absent label="Not issued" />),
    },
    {
      key: "created",
      header: "Created",
      sortKey: "created",
      numeric: true,
      secondary: true,
      render: (row) => <time dateTime={row.createdAt} className="text-ink-muted">{formatDateTime(row.createdAt)}</time>,
    },
  ];
}
```

The rest of the page (above `invoiceColumns` in the same file):
```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { getFinanceReadAccess, getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { INVOICE_LIST, INVOICE_SELECT, mapInvoiceRow, type InvoiceFilterKey, type InvoiceRow, type InvoiceSortKey } from "@/lib/invoices/list";
import { isNarrowed, parseListParams, type ListParams, type RawSearchParams } from "@/lib/list/params";
import { fetchListPage, type ListQuery } from "@/lib/list/query";
import type { ListColumn } from "@/lib/list/spec";
import { EntityList } from "@/components/list/entity-list";
import { ListSkeleton } from "@/components/list/list-skeleton";
import { ListToolbar } from "@/components/list/list-toolbar";
import { InvoiceStatusChip } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-status-chip";
import { Absent } from "@/components/ui/absent";
import { formatGbp } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { formatDateOnly, formatDateTime } from "@/lib/format/date";

// The invoices list (Invoices part 1). view_finance holders only; anyone else
// gets the not-found a missing page gets. "New invoice" is offered only to a
// caller who can write finance; otherwise the reason is shown in its place.
export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/invoices">): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  return { title: org ? `Invoices · ${org.name} · GetSetPaid` : "Not found · GetSetPaid" };
}

export default async function InvoicesPage({ params, searchParams }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/invoices">) {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  if (!org) notFound();
  if (!(await getFinanceReadAccess(org))) notFound();

  const raw = (await searchParams) as RawSearchParams;
  const listParams = parseListParams(INVOICE_LIST, raw);
  const basePath = `/${org.slug}/invoices`;
  const narrowed = isNarrowed(INVOICE_LIST, listParams);
  const access = await getFinanceWriteAccess(org);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">
          <Link href={`/${org.slug}`} className="break-words text-primary underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">{org.name}</Link>
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <h1 className="tracking-display text-3xl font-extrabold text-ink">Invoices</h1>
          {access.canWrite ? (
            <Link href={`${basePath}/new`} className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center border border-primary bg-primary px-4 text-base font-semibold text-shell hover:bg-ink hover:border-ink">
              New invoice
            </Link>
          ) : (
            <p className="max-w-sm text-sm text-ink-muted">{access.explanation}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <ListToolbar spec={INVOICE_LIST} params={listParams} basePath={basePath} narrowed={narrowed} />
      <Suspense key={JSON.stringify([listParams.q, listParams.filters, listParams.pageSize])} fallback={<ListSkeleton columns={6} />}>
        <InvoiceResults organisationId={org.id} orgSlug={org.slug} listParams={listParams} basePath={basePath} narrowed={narrowed} />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function InvoiceResults({
  organisationId,
  orgSlug,
  listParams,
  basePath,
  narrowed,
}: {
  organisationId: string;
  orgSlug: string;
  listParams: ListParams<InvoiceSortKey, InvoiceFilterKey>;
  basePath: string;
  narrowed: boolean;
}) {
  const page = await fetchListPage<InvoiceRow, InvoiceSortKey, InvoiceFilterKey>({
    spec: INVOICE_LIST,
    params: listParams,
    organisationId,
    table: "invoices",
    select: INVOICE_SELECT,
    mapRow: mapInvoiceRow,
    applyFilters: (query: ListQuery, filters) => (filters.status !== "any" ? query.eq("status", filters.status) : query),
  });
  return (
    <EntityList
      spec={INVOICE_LIST}
      params={listParams}
      basePath={basePath}
      page={page}
      columns={invoiceColumns(orgSlug)}
      rowKey={(row) => row.id}
      narrowed={narrowed}
      emptyState={
        <div className="border border-rule bg-surface p-5">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">No invoices yet</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-prose text-base text-ink-muted">
            An invoice bills a company or contact for a show. Start one here, or from an event’s finance lines.
          </p>
        </div>
      }
    />
  );
}
```
(The "New invoice" link copies the `primary` button classes from `components/ui/button.tsx` — square, no shadow, tokens only; check the class list there and copy it exactly rather than the approximation above.)

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { getFinanceReadAccess, getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { loadLinkOptions } from "@/lib/crm/queries";
import { loadInvoiceSettings } from "@/lib/invoices/queries";
import { EMPTY_LINE, InvoiceEditor } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-editor";
import { BLOCKED_NOTE_ID } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-actions";

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/invoices/new">): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  return { title: org ? `New invoice · ${org.name} · GetSetPaid` : "Not found · GetSetPaid" };
}

export default async function NewInvoicePage({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/invoices/new">) {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  if (!org) notFound();
  if (!(await getFinanceReadAccess(org))) notFound();
  const [access, companies, contacts, events, settings] = await Promise.all([
    getFinanceWriteAccess(org),
    loadLinkOptions("companies", org.id, []),
    loadLinkOptions("contacts", org.id, []),
    loadLinkOptions("events", org.id, []),
    loadInvoiceSettings(org.id),
  ]);
  const vatRegistered = settings.outcome === "loaded" && Boolean(settings.settings?.vatNumber);
  const disabledReason = access.canWrite ? null : access.explanation;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">
        <Link href={`/${org.slug}/invoices`} className="text-primary underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">Invoices</Link>
      </p>
      <h1 className="tracking-display text-3xl font-extrabold text-ink">New invoice</h1>
      {disabledReason ? (
        <p id={BLOCKED_NOTE_ID} className="max-w-prose border border-rule bg-surface px-3 py-3 text-sm text-ink-muted">
          <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Read-only.</span> {disabledReason}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <InvoiceEditor
        orgSlug={org.slug}
        invoiceId={null}
        companies={companies}
        contacts={contacts}
        events={events}
        vatRegistered={vatRegistered}
        initial={{ recipient: "", eventId: "", recipientAddress: "", supplyDate: "", notes: "", lines: [{ ...EMPTY_LINE, vatRate: vatRegistered ? "standard_20" : "none" }] }}
        disabledReason={disabledReason}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`app/(app)/[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]/page.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { getFinanceReadAccess, getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { loadLinkOptions } from "@/lib/crm/queries";
import { loadInvoice, loadInvoiceSettings, type InvoiceDetail } from "@/lib/invoices/queries";
import { parseSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";
import { minorToPoundsInput } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { InvoiceEditor, type EditorInitial } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-editor";
import { InvoiceView } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-view";
import { InvoiceStatusChip } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-status-chip";
import { BLOCKED_NOTE_ID, DeleteDraftForm } from "@/components/invoices/invoice-actions";

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]">): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { orgSlug } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  return { title: org ? `Invoice · ${org.name} · GetSetPaid` : "Not found · GetSetPaid" };
}

function editorInitial(inv: InvoiceDetail): EditorInitial {
  return {
    recipient: inv.recipientCompanyId ? `company:${inv.recipientCompanyId}` : inv.recipientContactId ? `contact:${inv.recipientContactId}` : "",
    eventId: inv.eventId ?? "",
    recipientAddress: inv.recipientAddress ?? "",
    supplyDate: inv.supplyDate ?? "",
    notes: inv.notes ?? "",
    lines: inv.lines.map((l) => ({
      description: l.description,
      quantity: l.quantity,
      unitPrice: minorToPoundsInput(l.unitPriceMinor),
      vatRate: l.vatRate,
      entryId: l.financialEntryId ?? "",
    })),
  };
}

export default async function InvoicePage({ params }: PageProps<"/[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]">) {
  const { orgSlug, invoiceId } = await params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  if (!org) notFound();
  if (!(await getFinanceReadAccess(org))) notFound();
  const [result, access] = await Promise.all([loadInvoice(org.id, invoiceId), getFinanceWriteAccess(org)]);
  if (result.outcome === "unavailable") {
    return <Alert tone="error">We couldn’t load this invoice just now. Refresh the page to try again — nothing has changed.</Alert>;
  }
  if (result.outcome === "missing") notFound();
  const invoice = result.invoice;
  const disabledReason = access.canWrite ? null : access.explanation;
  const blocked = disabledReason ? (
    <p id={BLOCKED_NOTE_ID} className="max-w-prose border border-rule bg-surface px-3 py-3 text-sm text-ink-muted">
      <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Read-only.</span> {disabledReason}
    </p>
  ) : null;
  const back = (
    <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">
      <Link href={`/${org.slug}/invoices`} className="text-primary underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">Invoices</Link>
    </p>
  );

  if (invoice.status === "draft") {
    const [companies, contacts, events, settings] = await Promise.all([
      loadLinkOptions("companies", org.id, []),
      loadLinkOptions("contacts", org.id, []),
      loadLinkOptions("events", org.id, []),
      loadInvoiceSettings(org.id),
    ]);
    const vatRegistered = settings.outcome === "loaded" && Boolean(settings.settings?.vatNumber);
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
        {back}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <h1 className="tracking-display text-3xl font-extrabold text-ink">Draft invoice</h1>
          <InvoiceStatusChip status="draft" />
        </div>
        {blocked}
        <InvoiceEditor orgSlug={org.slug} invoiceId={invoice.id} companies={companies} contacts={contacts} events={events} vatRegistered={vatRegistered} initial={editorInitial(invoice)} disabledReason={disabledReason} />
        {/* ISSUE (Task 10) renders here. */}
        <section aria-labelledby="delete-heading" className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-rule pt-6">
          <h2 id="delete-heading" className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">Delete this draft</h2>
          <DeleteDraftForm orgSlug={org.slug} invoiceId={invoice.id} disabledReason={disabledReason} />
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const snapshot = parseSnapshot(invoice.snapshot);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      {back}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <h1 className="tracking-display text-3xl font-extrabold text-ink tabular-nums">Invoice {invoice.number}</h1>
        <InvoiceStatusChip status={invoice.status} />
      </div>
      {invoice.status === "void" && invoice.voidReason ? (
        <p className="max-w-prose text-base text-ink-muted">Voided: {invoice.voidReason}</p>
      ) : null}
      {blocked}
      {snapshot ? (
        <InvoiceView snapshot={snapshot} />
      ) : (
        <Alert tone="error">This invoice’s issued copy could not be read. Nothing has been changed; contact support.</Alert>
      )}
      {/* ACTIONS (Tasks 10-12) render here. */}
    </div>
  );
}
```
The two `{/* … */}` comments mark where Tasks 10 and 12 add sections; they are comments in real code, not missing content.

```ts
async function plantInvoiceFixtures(p: {
  proOrgId: string;
  starterOrgId: string;
  otherOrgId: string;
  financeUserId: string;
  otherOwnerId: string;
  proContactId: string;
}) {
  const invoiceEventId = randomUUID();
  const invoiceIncomeEntryId = randomUUID();
  const proDraftInvoiceId = randomUUID();
  const proIssuedInvoiceId = randomUUID();
  const starterDraftInvoiceId = randomUUID();
  const otherInvoiceId = randomUUID();
  const starterContactId = randomUUID();

  await sql(
    `insert into public.invoice_settings (organisation_id, business_name, business_address, vat_number,
                                          bank_account_name, bank_sort_code, bank_account_number)
     values ($1, 'E2E Pro Promotions Ltd', '1 Ledger Street, London', 'GB123456789', 'E2E Pro Promotions Ltd', '123456', '12345678'),
            ($2, 'E2E Starter Shows', '2 Starter Lane, Bristol', null, null, null, null)`,
    [p.proOrgId, p.starterOrgId],
  );
  await sql(
    `insert into public.events (id, organisation_id, owner_user_id, name, starts_at, status)
     values ($1, $2, $3, $4, now() + interval '30 days', 'confirmed')`,
    [invoiceEventId, p.proOrgId, p.financeUserId, INVOICE_EVENT_NAME],

  await sql(
    `insert into public.financial_entries (id, organisation_id, event_id, category, status, amount_minor, description)

    [invoiceIncomeEntryId, p.proOrgId, invoiceEventId, INVOICE_INCOME.amountMinor, INVOICE_INCOME.description],

    starterContactId,
    p.starterOrgId,

  // Drafts first; the issued one is flipped after its line exists (the line freeze).

    `insert into public.invoices (id, organisation_id, recipient_contact_id, recipient_address, created_by)
     values ($1, $2, $3, '1 Supplier Street, London', $4),
            ($5, $2, $3, '1 Supplier Street, London', $4),
            ($6, $7, $8, '3 Customer Close, Bristol', $4),
            ($9, $10, null, null, $11)`,
    [proDraftInvoiceId, p.proOrgId, p.proContactId, p.financeUserId, proIssuedInvoiceId,
     starterDraftInvoiceId, p.starterOrgId, starterContactId, otherInvoiceId, p.otherOrgId, p.otherOwnerId],
  );
  await sql(
    `insert into public.invoice_lines (organisation_id, invoice_id, position, description, quantity, unit_price_minor, vat_rate, net_minor, vat_minor)
     values ($1, $2, 1, $3, 1, 50000, 'standard_20', 50000, 10000),
            ($1, $4, 1, 'Fixture issued line — headline fee', 1, 120000, 'standard_20', 120000, 24000),
            ($5, $6, 1, $7, 1, 50000, 'none', 50000, 0)`,
    [p.proOrgId, proDraftInvoiceId, INVOICE_FIXTURES.draftLine, proIssuedInvoiceId, p.starterOrgId,
     starterDraftInvoiceId, INVOICE_FIXTURES.starterLine],
  );
  await sql(`update public.invoices set net_minor = 50000, vat_minor = 10000, gross_minor = 60000 where id = $1`, [proDraftInvoiceId]);
  await sql(`update public.invoices set net_minor = 50000, gross_minor = 50000 where id = $1`, [starterDraftInvoiceId]);
  await sql(
    `update public.invoices i
        set status = 'issued', number = $2, issue_date = current_date, supply_date = current_date,
            due_date = current_date + 30, net_minor = 120000, vat_minor = 24000, gross_minor = 144000,
            snapshot = private.build_invoice_snapshot(i.id, $2, current_date, current_date, current_date + 30)
      where i.id = $1`,
    [proIssuedInvoiceId, INVOICE_FIXTURES.issuedNumber],
  );
  return { invoiceEventId, invoiceIncomeEntryId, proDraftInvoiceId, proIssuedInvoiceId, starterDraftInvoiceId, otherInvoiceId };
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
import {
  INVOICE_FIXTURES,
  PRO_SUPPLIER_NAME,
  setUpWorld,
  tearDownWorld,
  type E2eWorld,
} from "./support/fixtures";
import { signIn } from "./support/sign-in";

// INVOICES, PART 1 -- walked in a real browser. Tasks 9-13 each add their
// slice; Task 14 adds the golden path (the sixth sibling of golden-path,
// events, operations, finance and network), the Starter refusal and the
// keyboard-only line editor. Serial: the slices share one world.
test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" });

let world: E2eWorld;
test.beforeAll(async () => {
  world = await setUpWorld();
});
test.afterAll(async () => {
  if (world) await tearDownWorld(world);
});

function totals(page: Page) {
  return page.getByRole("region", { name: "Totals" });
}

test("a Finance user drafts an invoice, sees live totals, and saves it", async ({ page }) => {
  await signIn(page, world, "finance");
  await page.goto(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/invoices/new`);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "New invoice", level: 1 })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole("combobox", { name: /^Recipient/ }).selectOption({ label: PRO_SUPPLIER_NAME });
  await page.getByLabel("Recipient address").fill("1 Supplier Street, London");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 1 description").fill("E2E draft line — sound hire");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 1 quantity").fill("2");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 1 unit price").fill("150.00");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 1 VAT").selectOption("standard_20");
  // 2 × £150 = £300 net, £60 VAT, £360 total -- the TS port, before any save.
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£300.00");
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£60.00");
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£360.00");

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save draft" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/invoices/[0-9a-f-]{36}$`));
  // A fresh GET: the totals now come from what the DATABASE stored.
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Line 1 description")).toHaveValue("E2E draft line — sound hire");
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£360.00");

  await page.goto(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/invoices`);
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText("£360.00");
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(INVOICE_FIXTURES.issuedNumber);
});
```

### Task 10: Issue from the page, and the PDF (rendered on demand, audited, never stored)

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261106005000_invoice_download_audit.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/rls_invoices.sql` (§ IY above § IZ), `supabase/tests/run-rls-tests.sh` (pin)
- Create: `lib/invoices/pdf/invoice-document.tsx`, `lib/invoices/pdf/render.tsx`
- Create: `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]/pdf/route.ts`
- Modify: `lib/invoices/actions.ts` (`issueInvoiceAction`), `components/invoices/invoice-actions.tsx` (`IssueInvoiceForm`), `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]/page.tsx`
- Modify: `tests/integration/security/phase7-permission-matrix.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/invoices.spec.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/invoices/pdf-render.test.ts`

(The File Structure's `pdf/render.ts` is `pdf/render.tsx`: it renders JSX.)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `public.issue_invoice` (Task 5); `InvoiceSnapshot`, `parseSnapshot` (Task 2); the fonts (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `public.audit_invoice_download(p_invoice_id uuid) returns void` — `view_finance` in the invoice's own workspace (NOT tier-gated: a downgraded workspace keeps downloading); refuses a draft (`invoice_not_issued`); audits `invoice.pdf_downloaded` with the number.
  - `lib/invoices/pdf/render.tsx`: `export async function renderInvoicePdf(snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot, options: { status: "issued" | "paid" | "void" }): Promise<Buffer>` (a void invoice prints a VOID banner).
  - `issueInvoiceAction(prev, formData)`; `IssueInvoiceForm({ orgSlug, invoiceId, disabledReason })`.
  - `GET /[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]/pdf` → `200 application/pdf` with `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="<number>.pdf"`, or `404` (not yours / not there / a draft), or `500` (unreadable snapshot, audit failure). The audit row is written before any byte is rendered.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the migration**

```sql
-- supabase/migrations/20261106005000_invoice_download_audit.sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES PART 1 -- TASK 10: THE PDF DOWNLOAD IS AUDITED.
-- The PDF route renders from the immutable snapshot on demand and stores
-- nothing; it calls this BEFORE rendering a byte -- no audit, no PDF. Reads
-- are not tier-gated (spec § Permissions: after a downgrade "view and
-- download stay"), so view_finance alone, in the invoice's own workspace.
-- ===========================================================================
create or replace function public.audit_invoice_download(p_invoice_id uuid)
returns void
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid    uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_org    uuid;
  v_status text;
  v_number text;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'audit_invoice_download: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  select i.organisation_id, i.status, i.number into v_org, v_status, v_number
    from public.invoices i where i.id = p_invoice_id;
  if v_org is null or not public.has_permission(v_org, 'view_finance') then
    raise exception 'audit_invoice_download: the caller may not read this invoice'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if v_status = 'draft' then
    raise exception 'audit_invoice_download: a draft has no PDF'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_issued';
  end if;
  perform private.log_audit_event(
    p_action          => 'invoice.pdf_downloaded',
    p_target_type     => 'invoice',
    p_organisation_id => v_org,
    p_target_id       => p_invoice_id,
    p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('number', v_number, 'status', v_status));
end;
$$;
comment on function public.audit_invoice_download(uuid) is
  'Writes invoice.pdf_downloaded for an issued, paid or void invoice the caller may read (view_finance, not tier-gated). Called by the PDF route before it renders anything.';
revoke all on function public.audit_invoice_download(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.audit_invoice_download(uuid) to authenticated;
```
Run the suite — green. Add to `MEMBER_REACHABLE_RPCS`:
```ts
  audit_invoice_download:
    "Invoices part 1 Task 10 -- the PDF route calls it before rendering, so it is " +
    "reachable by whoever may read the invoice (view_finance, not tier-gated). It " +
    "refuses a draft and anything outside the caller's workspace, writes an " +
    "identifier-only audit row, and returns no data. Asserted in rls_invoices.sql § IY.",
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the document and the renderer**

`lib/invoices/pdf/invoice-document.tsx`:
```tsx
// THE INVOICE PDF, laid out from @react-pdf primitives -- not HTML, so no
// user text can become markup or script (spec § Security 3). Rendered ONLY
// from the snapshot. Fields per HMRC's full VAT invoice requirements: unique
// sequential number; issue date; supply date when it differs; seller name,
// address and VAT number; recipient name and address; per line the
// description, quantity, unit price ex VAT, VAT rate and net amount; total
// net, total VAT, total gross; due date and bank details. With no VAT number
// every VAT field is omitted. A void invoice carries a VOID banner so a
// printed copy cannot be mistaken for a live one.
import { Document, Page, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "@react-pdf/renderer";
import type { InvoiceSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";
import { VAT_RATE_LABELS } from "@/lib/invoices/arithmetic";
import { formatGbp } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { formatDateOnly } from "@/lib/format/date";

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { fontFamily: "Source Serif 4", fontSize: 10, padding: 40, color: "#111111" },
  h1: { fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 4 },
  void: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, borderWidth: 2, padding: 6, marginBottom: 12, textAlign: "center" },
  row: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 16 },
  block: { width: "48%" },
  label: { fontSize: 8, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", marginBottom: 2 },
  th: { flexDirection: "row", borderBottomWidth: 1, paddingBottom: 4, fontWeight: 700 },
  tr: { flexDirection: "row", borderBottomWidth: 0.5, paddingVertical: 4 },
  desc: { flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 },
  num: { width: 70, textAlign: "right" },
  rate: { width: 80, paddingLeft: 8 },
  totals: { marginTop: 12, marginLeft: "auto", width: 200 },
  totalRow: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", paddingVertical: 2 },
  strong: { fontWeight: 700 },
  section: { marginTop: 20 },
});

export function InvoiceDocument({ snapshot: x, status }: { snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot; status: "issued" | "paid" | "void" }) {
  return (
    <Document title={`Invoice ${x.number}`} author={x.seller.name}>
      <Page size="A4" style={s.page}>
        {status === "void" ? <Text style={s.void}>VOID — this invoice has been cancelled and is not payable</Text> : null}
        <Text style={s.h1}>Invoice {x.number}</Text>
        <View style={s.row}>
          <View style={s.block}>
            <Text style={s.label}>From</Text>
            <Text style={s.strong}>{x.seller.name}</Text>
            <Text>{x.seller.address}</Text>
            {x.seller.vatNumber ? <Text>VAT number {x.seller.vatNumber}</Text> : null}
          </View>
          <View style={s.block}>
            <Text style={s.label}>To</Text>
            <Text style={s.strong}>{x.recipient.name}</Text>
            <Text>{x.recipient.address}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={s.row}>
          <View style={s.block}>
            <Text>Issue date: {formatDateOnly(x.issueDate)}</Text>
            {x.supplyDate !== x.issueDate ? <Text>Supply date (tax point): {formatDateOnly(x.supplyDate)}</Text> : null}
          </View>
          <View style={s.block}>
            <Text style={s.strong}>Due date: {formatDateOnly(x.dueDate)}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={s.th}>
          <Text style={s.desc}>Description</Text>
          <Text style={s.num}>Quantity</Text>
          <Text style={s.num}>Unit price</Text>
          {x.vatRegistered ? <Text style={s.rate}>VAT rate</Text> : null}
          <Text style={s.num}>Net</Text>
        </View>
        {x.lines.map((l) => (
          <View key={l.position} style={s.tr} wrap={false}>
            <Text style={s.desc}>{l.description}</Text>
            <Text style={s.num}>{l.quantity}</Text>
            <Text style={s.num}>{formatGbp(l.unitPriceMinor)}</Text>
            {x.vatRegistered ? <Text style={s.rate}>{VAT_RATE_LABELS[l.vatRate]}</Text> : null}
            <Text style={s.num}>{formatGbp(l.netMinor)}</Text>
          </View>
        ))}

        <View style={s.totals}>
          <View style={s.totalRow}><Text>Total net</Text><Text>{formatGbp(x.totals.netMinor)}</Text></View>
          {x.vatRegistered ? (
            <View style={s.totalRow}><Text>Total VAT</Text><Text>{formatGbp(x.totals.vatMinor)}</Text></View>
          ) : null}
          <View style={s.totalRow}><Text style={s.strong}>Total due</Text><Text style={s.strong}>{formatGbp(x.totals.grossMinor)}</Text></View>
        </View>

        <View style={s.section}>
          <Text style={s.label}>How to pay</Text>
          {x.seller.bank ? (
            <Text>
              Bank transfer to {x.seller.bank.accountName}, sort code {x.seller.bank.sortCode}, account number{" "}
              {x.seller.bank.accountNumber}. Please quote {x.number}.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <Text>Contact {x.seller.name} for payment details, quoting {x.number}.</Text>
          )}
        </View>
        {x.notes ? (
          <View style={s.section}>
            <Text style={s.label}>Notes</Text>
            <Text>{x.notes}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}
      </Page>
    </Document>
  );
}
```
`lib/invoices/pdf/render.tsx`:
```tsx
import "server-only";
import path from "node:path";
import { Font, renderToBuffer } from "@react-pdf/renderer";
import type { InvoiceSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";
import { InvoiceDocument } from "@/lib/invoices/pdf/invoice-document";

// Source Serif 4, embedded (Task 1's static TTFs, OFL). Registered once per
// server process.
const FONTS = path.join(process.cwd(), "lib", "invoices", "pdf", "fonts");
let registered = false;
function registerFonts(): void {
  if (registered) return;
  Font.register({
    family: "Source Serif 4",
    fonts: [
      { src: path.join(FONTS, "SourceSerif4-Regular.ttf"), fontWeight: 400 },
      { src: path.join(FONTS, "SourceSerif4-Bold.ttf"), fontWeight: 700 },
    ],
  });
  // Never hyphenate a sort code, an account number or an invoice number.
  Font.registerHyphenationCallback((word) => [word]);
  registered = true;
}

export async function renderInvoicePdf(
  snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot,
  options: { status: "issued" | "paid" | "void" },
): Promise<Buffer> {
  registerFonts();
  return renderToBuffer(<InvoiceDocument snapshot={snapshot} status={options.status} />);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The route**

`app/(app)/[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]/pdf/route.ts`:
```ts
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { parseSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";
import { renderInvoicePdf } from "@/lib/invoices/pdf/render";

// GET /[orgSlug]/invoices/[invoiceId]/pdf (spec § PDF). THE ORDER IS THE
// CONTROL: load through the caller's RLS (view_finance) so another tenant's,
// a missing and a draft invoice are the same 404; parse the snapshot; AUDIT;
// only then render. Nothing is stored. The filename is the constrained
// number, so no user text reaches the header.
export const runtime = "nodejs";

const NO_STORE = { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } as const;

export async function GET(
  _request: Request,
  ctx: { params: Promise<{ orgSlug: string; invoiceId: string }> },
): Promise<Response> {
  const { orgSlug, invoiceId } = await ctx.params;
  const org = await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug);
  if (!org) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404, headers: NO_STORE });

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("invoices")
    .select("status, snapshot")
    .eq("organisation_id", org.id)
    .eq("id", invoiceId)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === "22P02") return new Response("Not found", { status: 404, headers: NO_STORE });
    console.error("[invoices] PDF lookup failed:", error.message);
    return new Response("Error", { status: 500, headers: NO_STORE });
  }
  if (!data || data.status === "draft") return new Response("Not found", { status: 404, headers: NO_STORE });

  const snapshot = parseSnapshot(data.snapshot);
  if (!snapshot) {
    console.error(`[invoices] invoice ${invoiceId} has an unreadable snapshot`);
    return new Response("Error", { status: 500, headers: NO_STORE });
  }

  const { error: auditError } = await supabase.rpc("audit_invoice_download", { p_invoice_id: invoiceId });
  if (auditError) {
    console.error("[invoices] PDF download audit failed; refusing the download:", auditError.message);
    return new Response("Error", { status: 500, headers: NO_STORE });
  }

  const pdf = await renderInvoicePdf(snapshot, { status: data.status as "issued" | "paid" | "void" });
  return new Response(new Uint8Array(pdf), {
    headers: {
      ...NO_STORE,
      "Content-Type": "application/pdf",
      "Content-Disposition": `attachment; filename="${snapshot.number}.pdf"`,
    },
  });
}
```

Append to `lib/invoices/actions.ts`:
```ts
export async function issueInvoiceAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const auth = await authorise(formData);
  if (!auth.ok) return auth.state;
  const invoiceId = field(formData, "invoiceId");
  if (!invoiceId) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("issue_invoice", { p_invoice_id: invoiceId });
  if (error) return refused(error, "issue", formData);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices/${invoiceId}`);
  return { message: `Issued as ${String(data)}.` };
}
```

```ts
/** Audit rows of one action about one target (Invoices part 1). As postgres. */
export async function countAuditRows(action: string, targetId: string): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await sql<{ n: string }>(
    `select count(*)::text as n from public.audit_events where action = $1 and target_id = $2`,
    [action, targetId],
  );
  return Number(rows[0]?.n ?? 0);
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - Rate-limit category `invoice_send`: user-scoped, 30 per 3600 s.
  - `public.record_invoice_send(p_invoice_id uuid, p_to_email text, p_provider_message_id text) returns uuid` — issued or paid only (`invoice_not_sendable`), address shape (`invoice_send_email_invalid`), `manage_finance` + Pro; inserts `invoice_sends`, audits `invoice.sent` (number only, never the address).
  - `buildInvoiceEmail(input: { number: string; sellerName: string; recipientName: string; grossMinor: number; dueDate: string; bank: InvoiceSnapshot["seller"]["bank"] }): EmailBody`.
  - `sendInvoiceAction(prev, formData)` — form fields `orgSlug`, `invoiceId`, `toEmail`. Order: authorise → validate address → load invoice (issued/paid) → **consume the budget** → render PDF → Brevo → record. A refused budget sends nothing; a failed send records nothing.
  - `loadInvoiceSends(organisationId, invoiceId): Promise<{ toEmail: string; sentAt: string }[]>` (latest 20).
  - `SendInvoiceForm({ orgSlug, invoiceId, defaultEmail, disabledReason })`.

- [ ] **Step 2: The migration**

```sql
-- supabase/migrations/20261106006000_invoice_send.sql
-- ===========================================================================
-- INVOICES PART 1 -- TASK 11: SEND.
-- The budget is spent by sendInvoiceAction BEFORE the Brevo call (the uploads
-- shape: there is no client INSERT on invoice_sends to meter at, and a
-- trigger here would meter after the email had already gone). The send is
-- recorded AFTER Brevo accepted it, through public.record_invoice_send(),
-- which re-checks manage_finance and the Pro entitlement and audits
-- invoice.sent with the number only -- the recipient's address is personal
-- data and stays in invoice_sends, out of the audit log.
-- ===========================================================================
insert into public.rate_limit_policies (category, subject_scope, max_requests, window_seconds, description)
values ('invoice_send', 'user', 30, 3600,
        'Invoice emails sent per user (Invoices part 1). Consumed by sendInvoiceAction before the Brevo call; outbound mail to an arbitrary address is a spam vector.');

create or replace function public.record_invoice_send(p_invoice_id uuid, p_to_email text, p_provider_message_id text)
returns uuid
language plpgsql
volatile
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_uid    uuid := (select auth.uid());
  v_org    uuid;
  v_status text;
  v_number text;
  v_id     uuid;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'record_invoice_send: no authenticated user'
      using errcode = '28000', hint = 'not_authenticated';
  end if;
  select i.organisation_id, i.status, i.number into v_org, v_status, v_number
    from public.invoices i where i.id = p_invoice_id;
  if v_org is null or not public.has_permission(v_org, 'manage_finance') then
    raise exception 'record_invoice_send: the caller may not send this invoice'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'forbidden';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from private.finance_module_organisations() f where f = v_org) then
    raise exception 'record_invoice_send: this workspace''s plan does not include invoicing'
      using errcode = '42501', hint = 'plan_does_not_include_invoicing';
  end if;
  if v_status not in ('issued', 'paid') then
    raise exception 'record_invoice_send: only an issued or paid invoice is sent'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_not_sendable';
  end if;
  if p_to_email is null or pg_catalog.char_length(p_to_email) > 254
     or p_to_email !~ '^[^\s@]+@[^\s@.]+\.[^\s@]+$' then
    raise exception 'record_invoice_send: that is not an email address'
      using errcode = '22023', hint = 'invoice_send_email_invalid';
  end if;
  insert into public.invoice_sends (organisation_id, invoice_id, to_email, sent_by, provider_message_id)
  values (v_org, p_invoice_id, p_to_email, v_uid, pg_catalog.left(p_provider_message_id, 200))
  returning id into v_id;
  perform private.log_audit_event(
    p_action          => 'invoice.sent',
    p_target_type     => 'invoice',
    p_organisation_id => v_org,
    p_target_id       => p_invoice_id,
    p_after           => pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('number', v_number));
  return v_id;
end;
$$;
comment on function public.record_invoice_send(uuid, text, text) is
  'Records an invoice email Brevo accepted: issued or paid only, manage_finance + Pro. Appends invoice_sends and audits invoice.sent (number only, never the address).';
revoke all on function public.record_invoice_send(uuid, text, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.record_invoice_send(uuid, text, text) to authenticated;
```
Run the suites — green. Move the INVOICES pin (180 + 10 = 190). Add to `MEMBER_REACHABLE_RPCS`:

```ts
import { buildInvoiceEmail } from "@/lib/email/invoice-templates";

describe("buildInvoiceEmail", () => {

    dueDate: "2026-11-19",

  it("neutralises a link smuggled into a name", () => {
    const body = buildInvoiceEmail({ ...input, sellerName: "Pay https://evil.example now" });
    expect(body.text).not.toContain("https://evil.example");
    expect(body.subject).not.toContain("https://");
  });
  it("refuses a number outside the invoice shape", () => {
    expect(() => buildInvoiceEmail({ ...input, number: "../x" })).toThrow();
  });
});
```

Append to `lib/email/invoice-templates.ts`:
```ts
import { escapeHtml } from "@/lib/email/templates";
import { formatGbp } from "@/lib/format/money";
import { formatDateOnly } from "@/lib/format/date";
import type { InvoiceSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";

const INVOICE_NUMBER = /^[A-Z0-9-]{0,12}[0-9]{4,10}$/;

export interface InvoiceEmailInput {
  readonly number: string;
  readonly sellerName: string;
  readonly recipientName: string;
  readonly grossMinor: number;
  readonly dueDate: string;
  readonly bank: InvoiceSnapshot["seller"]["bank"];
}

/** The invoice email to the customer: plain, no link (the PDF is attached),
 *  every person's text one line and neutralised. */
export function buildInvoiceEmail(input: InvoiceEmailInput): EmailBody {
  if (!INVOICE_NUMBER.test(input.number)) throw new NoticeContentError("invoice email refused: bad number");
  const seller = oneLine(input.sellerName, 120);
  const recipient = oneLine(input.recipientName, 120);
  const paragraphs = [
    `Dear ${recipient},`,
    `Please find attached invoice ${input.number} from ${seller} for ${formatGbp(input.grossMinor)}, due on ${formatDateOnly(input.dueDate)}.`,
    input.bank
      ? `Please pay by bank transfer to ${oneLine(input.bank.accountName, 140)}, sort code ${input.bank.sortCode}, account number ${input.bank.accountNumber}, quoting ${input.number}.`
      : `Please contact ${seller} for payment details, quoting ${input.number}.`,
    `Sent on behalf of ${seller} by GetSetPaid.`,
  ];
  return {
    subject: `Invoice ${input.number} from ${seller}`,
    text: paragraphs.join("\n\n"),
    html: paragraphs.map((p) => `<p>${escapeHtml(p)}</p>`).join("\n"),
  };
}
```

```ts
    expect((await sendInvoiceAction({}, form("ap@customer.example"))).error).toBeTruthy();
    expect(h.log).toEqual([]);
```
