- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `lib/invoices/send-action.ts`**

```ts
"use server";
// Send an issued or paid invoice by email with its PDF attached (spec § Send).
// ORDER IS THE CONTROL: authorise; validate the address; load the invoice
// through RLS (issued or paid); SPEND THE BUDGET (invoice_send, 30/h/user,
// SECURITY.md § 5) -- after the cheap refusals, before any work; render the
// PDF from the snapshot; hand it to Brevo; only after Brevo accepted it,
// record the send (public.record_invoice_send audits it). A failed send
// records nothing; a recorded send that the database then refuses is
// reported as sent-but-not-logged, never as a failure to send.
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { getCurrentOrg } from "@/lib/auth";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { echoedValues, field, isValidEmail, type FormState } from "@/lib/forms";
import { FAILED_MESSAGE, REFUSED_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/crm/write";
import { getFinanceWriteAccess } from "@/lib/crm/finance-access";
import { consumeRateLimit } from "@/lib/security/rate-limit";
import { formatRetryAfter } from "@/lib/format/duration";
import { parseSnapshot } from "@/lib/invoices/snapshot";
import { renderInvoicePdf } from "@/lib/invoices/pdf/render";
import { invoiceRefusal } from "@/lib/invoices/refusals";
import { sendEmail } from "@/lib/email/send";
import { buildInvoiceEmail } from "@/lib/email/invoice-templates";

const SEND_FAILED = "We couldn’t send that email just now. Nothing was sent. Try again in a moment.";
const SENT_NOT_RECORDED =
  "The email was sent, but we couldn’t record it in the send history. Don’t send it again — reload the page.";

export async function sendInvoiceAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const values = echoedValues(formData);
  const orgSlug = field(formData, "orgSlug");
  const org = orgSlug ? await getCurrentOrg(orgSlug) : null;
  if (!org) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values };
  const access = await getFinanceWriteAccess(org);
  if (!access.canWrite) return { error: access.explanation, values };

  const invoiceId = field(formData, "invoiceId");
  const toEmail = field(formData, "toEmail");
  if (!invoiceId) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values };
  if (!toEmail || !isValidEmail(toEmail)) {
    return { fieldErrors: { toEmail: "Enter the email address to send the invoice to." }, values };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("invoices")
    .select("status, snapshot")
    .eq("organisation_id", org.id)
    .eq("id", invoiceId)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (error || !data) return { error: REFUSED_MESSAGE, values };
  if (data.status !== "issued" && data.status !== "paid") {
    return { error: invoiceRefusal({ hint: "invoice_not_sendable", message: "" }), values };
  }
  const snapshot = parseSnapshot(data.snapshot);
  if (!snapshot) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values };

  const budget = await consumeRateLimit("invoice_send");
  if (!budget.allowed) {
    return {
      error: `You’ve sent as many invoices as allowed for now — nothing is wrong. Try again in ${formatRetryAfter(budget.retryAfterSeconds)}.`,
      values,
    };
  }

  const pdf = await renderInvoicePdf(snapshot, { status: data.status });
  const body = buildInvoiceEmail({
    number: snapshot.number,
    sellerName: snapshot.seller.name,
    recipientName: snapshot.recipient.name,
    grossMinor: snapshot.totals.grossMinor,
    dueDate: snapshot.dueDate,
    bank: snapshot.seller.bank,
  });
  const sent = await sendEmail({
    to: [{ email: toEmail }],
    subject: body.subject,
    html: body.html,
    text: body.text,
    attachments: [{ name: `${snapshot.number}.pdf`, contentBase64: pdf.toString("base64") }],
  });
  if (!sent.ok) {
    // The invoice id, never the address (a log is read by more people than the database).
    console.error(`[invoices] email of invoice ${invoiceId} failed:`, sent.error);
    return { error: SEND_FAILED, values };
  }

  const { error: recordError } = await supabase.rpc("record_invoice_send", {
    p_invoice_id: invoiceId,
    p_to_email: toEmail,
    p_provider_message_id: sent.messageId || null,
  });
  if (recordError) {
    console.error(`[invoices] invoice ${invoiceId} was emailed but not recorded:`, recordError.message);
    return { error: SENT_NOT_RECORDED, values };
  }
  revalidatePath(`/${org.slug}/invoices/${invoiceId}`);
  return { message: `Sent to ${toEmail}.` };
}
```

```ts
export async function loadInvoiceSends(organisationId: string, invoiceId: string): Promise<{ toEmail: string; sentAt: string }[]> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("invoice_sends")
    .select("to_email, sent_at")
    .eq("organisation_id", organisationId)
    .eq("invoice_id", invoiceId)
    .order("sent_at", { ascending: false })
    .limit(20);

}
```

Append to the § 5 table (after the Document uploads row):
```markdown
| Invoice emails (Invoices part 1) | 30 / hour, per user | **ENFORCED IN THE APP, BEFORE THE SEND** — `sendInvoiceAction` (`lib/invoices/send-action.ts`) calls `consumeRateLimit("invoice_send")` after the cheap refusals (permission, plan, address shape, the invoice is issued or paid) and before the PDF is rendered or Brevo is called (`20261106006000` adds the policy row). Not a trigger: there is no client write on `invoice_sends` to meter, and a trigger at the recording would meter after the email had gone. - [ ] **Step 2: The actions**
```

Append to `lib/invoices/actions.ts`:
```ts
export async function markInvoicePaidAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const auth = await authorise(formData);
  if (!auth.ok) return auth.state;
  const invoiceId = field(formData, "invoiceId");
  const paidOn = field(formData, "paidOn");
  if (!invoiceId) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  if (!paidOn || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(paidOn)) {
    return { fieldErrors: { paidOn: "Choose the date the payment arrived." }, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  }
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("mark_invoice_paid", { p_invoice_id: invoiceId, p_paid_on: paidOn });
  if (error) return refused(error, "mark paid", formData);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices/${invoiceId}`);
  return { message: "Marked as paid. Any income it brings in is now on its event’s finance." };
}

export async function voidInvoiceAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const auth = await authorise(formData);
  if (!auth.ok) return auth.state;
  const invoiceId = field(formData, "invoiceId");
  const reason = field(formData, "reason");
  if (!invoiceId) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  if (!reason || reason.length > 500) {
    return { fieldErrors: { reason: "Give a reason for voiding — 500 characters at most." }, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  }
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("void_invoice", { p_invoice_id: invoiceId, p_reason: reason });
  if (error) return refused(error, "void", formData);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices/${invoiceId}`);
  return { message: "Voided. The number stays used; issue a new invoice to correct it." };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Loader, action, component, page**

Append to `lib/invoices/queries.ts`:
```ts
export async function loadInvoiceableIncomeLines(
  organisationId: string,
  eventId: string,
): Promise<{ id: string; description: string | null; amountMinor: number; status: "budgeted" | "actual" }[]> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("financial_entries")
    .select("id, description, amount_minor, status")
    .eq("organisation_id", organisationId)
    .eq("event_id", eventId)
    .eq("category", "income")
    .is("deleted_at", null)
    .order("occurred_on", { ascending: true })
    .order("id", { ascending: true })
    .limit(50);
  if (error) {
    console.error("[invoices] invoiceable income lookup failed:", error.message);
    return [];
  }
  return (data ?? []).map((r) => ({
    id: String(r.id),
    description: (r.description as string | null) ?? null,
    amountMinor: Number(r.amount_minor),
    status: r.status as "budgeted" | "actual",
  }));
}
```
Append to `lib/invoices/actions.ts` (add `REFUSED_MESSAGE` to the `@/lib/crm/write` import):
```ts
export async function createDraftFromFinanceAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const auth = await authorise(formData);
  if (!auth.ok) return auth.state;
  const eventId = field(formData, "eventId");
  const ids = [...new Set(formData.getAll("entryId").filter((v): v is string => typeof v === "string" && v !== ""))];
  if (!eventId) return { error: FAILED_MESSAGE, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  if (ids.length === 0) return { error: "Tick at least one income line to invoice.", values: echoedValues(formData) };

  // Read through RLS; the RPC re-checks each entry is live income on this event.
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("financial_entries")
    .select("id, description, amount_minor")
    .eq("organisation_id", auth.org.id)
    .eq("event_id", eventId)
    .eq("category", "income")
    .is("deleted_at", null)
    .in("id", ids);
  if (error || !data || data.length !== ids.length) {
    return { error: REFUSED_MESSAGE, values: echoedValues(formData) };
  }
  const byId = new Map(data.map((e) => [String(e.id), e]));
  const lines = ids.map((id) => {
    const e = byId.get(id)!;
    return {
      description: (e.description as string | null) ?? "Income",
      quantity: "1.00",
      unit_price_minor: Number(e.amount_minor),
      vat_rate: "standard_20",
      financial_entry_id: id,
    };
  });
  const { data: invoiceId, error: saveError } = await supabase.rpc("save_invoice_draft", {
    p_org: auth.org.id,
    p_invoice_id: null,
    p_recipient_company_id: null,
    p_recipient_contact_id: null,
    p_event_id: eventId,
    p_recipient_address: null,
    p_supply_date: null,
    p_notes: null,
    p_lines: lines,
  });
  if (saveError) return refused(saveError, "draft from finance", formData);
  revalidatePath(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices`);
  redirect(`/${auth.org.slug}/invoices/${String(invoiceId)}`);
}
```

```tsx

export function InvoiceFromFinanceForm({
  orgSlug,
  eventId,
  lines,
}: {
  orgSlug: string;
  eventId: string;
  lines: readonly { id: string; description: string | null; amountMinor: number; status: "budgeted" | "actual" }[];
}) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(createDraftFromFinanceAction, EMPTY_FORM_STATE);
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="invoice-lines-heading" className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-t border-rule pt-6">
      <h2 id="invoice-lines-heading" className="text-xl font-bold text-ink">Invoice these lines</h2>
      <form action={action} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <input type="hidden" name="orgSlug" value={orgSlug} />
        <input type="hidden" name="eventId" value={eventId} />
        <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <legend className="text-sm text-ink-muted">Tick the income to bill. Each line becomes a line on a new draft invoice.</legend>
          {lines.map((l) => (
            <label key={l.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-base text-ink">
              <input type="checkbox" name="entryId" value={l.id} className="h-5 w-5 accent-[var(--color-primary)]" />
              <span>
                {l.description ?? "Income"} — <span className="tabular-nums">{formatGbp(l.amountMinor)}</span>{" "}
                <span className="text-ink-muted">({l.status === "budgeted" ? "Budgeted" : "Actual"})</span>
              </span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </fieldset>
        {state.error ? <Alert tone="error">{state.error}</Alert> : null}
        <div>
          <Button type="submit" variant="secondary" pending={pending} pendingLabel="Creating…">Create draft invoice</Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}
```
(Check the checkbox accent against how other checkboxes in `components/crm/` are styled — e.g. the company type group — and copy that class rather than inventing one; tokens only.)

```ts
/** One cell of the event's budget-vs-actuals table (finance.spec.ts's helper). */
function summaryCell(page: Page, row: string, column: 0 | 1): Locator {
  return page
    .getByRole("region", { name: "Budget versus actuals", exact: true })
    .locator("tbody tr")
    .filter({ has: page.locator(`th:text-is("${row}")`) })
    .locator("td")
    .nth(column);
}

test("THE GOLDEN PATH: settings behind step-up, a draft from the event's income plus a free line, issue, PDF, paid, income on the event", async ({ page }) => {
  // A real TOTP challenge happens inside signIn (finance is MFA-required),
  // which is also the fresh credential the settings page's step-up asks for.
  await signIn(page, world, "finance");

  // ---- 1. SETTINGS (step-up) ----------------------------------------------
  await page.goto(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/settings/invoicing`);
  await page.getByLabel("Number prefix").fill("E2E-");
  await page.getByLabel("Payment terms (days)").fill("14");
  await page.getByLabel("Account number").fill("87654321");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save invoice settings" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Saved." })).toBeVisible();
  // The fraud guard: a bank change is audited (and queued for the Owners/Admins).
  expect(await countAuditRows("invoice_settings.bank_details_changed", world.proOrgId)).toBe(1);

  // ---- 2. A DRAFT FROM THE EVENT'S BUDGETED INCOME --------------------------
  await page.goto(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/events/${world.invoiceEventId}`);
  await expect(summaryCell(page, "Gross", 0)).toHaveText(INVOICE_INCOME.formatted);
  await expect(summaryCell(page, "Gross", 1)).toHaveText("£0.00");
  const section = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Invoice these lines" });
  await section.getByLabel(new RegExp(INVOICE_INCOME.description)).check();
  await section.getByRole("button", { name: "Create draft invoice" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Draft invoice", level: 1 })).toBeVisible();

  // ---- 3. RECIPIENT, ADDRESS, ONE FREE LINE -------------------------------
  await page.getByRole("combobox", { name: /^Recipient/ }).selectOption({ label: PRO_SUPPLIER_NAME });
  await page.getByLabel("Recipient address").fill("1 Supplier Street, London");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add line" }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Line 2 description").fill("E2E golden line — production");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 2 quantity").fill("2");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 2 unit price").fill("150");
  await page.getByLabel("Line 2 VAT").selectOption("standard_20");
  // £3,000 + £300 net, £600 + £60 VAT = £3,960.
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£3,960.00");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save draft" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Draft saved." })).toBeVisible();

  // ---- 4. ISSUE -----------------------------------------------------------
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Issue invoice" }).click();
  const heading = page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 });
  await expect(heading).toHaveText(/^Invoice E2E-\d{4}$/);
  const number = ((await heading.textContent()) ?? "").replace("Invoice ", "");

  // ---- 5. THE PDF ---------------------------------------------------------
  const downloading = page.waitForEvent("download");
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Download PDF" }).click();
  const download = await downloading;
  expect(download.suggestedFilename()).toBe(`${number}.pdf`);
  expect((await readFile(await download.path())).subarray(0, 5).toString("latin1")).toBe("%PDF-");

  // ---- 6. PAID -------------------------------------------------------------
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Mark as paid" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator("main").getByText("Paid", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  // ---- 7. THE PAYOFF: THE INCOME IS ON THE EVENT, NET OF VAT ---------------
  // The pulled £3,000 flipped budgeted -> actual; the free line's £300 net
  // (not its £360 gross) became a new actual income line.
  await page.goto(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/events/${world.invoiceEventId}`);
  await expect(summaryCell(page, "Gross", 0)).toHaveText("£0.00");
  await expect(summaryCell(page, "Gross", 1)).toHaveText("£3,300.00");
  await expect(page.getByRole("region", { name: "Finance", exact: true })).toContainText(`Invoice ${number}`);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The Starter refusal and the keyboard-only editor**

```ts
/** Issued, paid or void invoices of one workspace (Invoices part 1). As postgres. */
export async function countIssuedInvoices(organisationId: string): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await sql<{ n: string }>(
    `select count(*)::text as n from public.invoices where organisation_id = $1 and status <> 'draft'`,
    [organisationId],
  );
  return Number(rows[0]?.n ?? 0);
}
```
Append to `tests/e2e/invoices.spec.ts`:
```ts
test("a Starter workspace reads its draft, and its disabled Issue control refuses server-side with disabled stripped", async ({ page }) => {
  await signIn(page, world, "finance");
  await page.goto(`/${world.starterOrgSlug}/invoices/${world.starterDraftInvoiceId}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Draft invoice", level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Line 1 description")).toHaveValue(INVOICE_FIXTURES.starterLine);
  await expect(page.locator("#invoice-blocked")).toContainText("Finance is a Pro feature");

  const issue = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Issue invoice" });
  await expect(issue).toBeDisabled();
  // HIDING UI IS NOT ENFORCEMENT: strip the attribute and submit for real.
  await issue.evaluate((button) => button.removeAttribute("disabled"));
  await issue.click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: "Finance is a Pro feature" })).toBeVisible();

  // AND NOTHING WAS ISSUED: the denominator is the database, not the page.
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Draft invoice", level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
  expect(await countIssuedInvoices(world.starterOrgId)).toBe(0);
});

test("the line editor works from the keyboard alone", async ({ page }) => {
  await signIn(page, world, "finance");
  await page.goto(`/${world.proOrgSlug}/invoices/new`);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add line" }).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  // The new row's description takes focus (the editor moves it there).
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Line 2 description")).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.type("Keyboard line");
  await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Line 2 quantity")).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press("Control+A");
  await page.keyboard.type("3");
  await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Line 2 unit price")).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.type("10");
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£36.00"); // 3 × £10 + 20% VAT
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove line 2" }).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Line 2 description")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(totals(page)).toContainText("£0.00");
});
```

```ts
test("another tenant's invoice is unreachable by id under my slug, by its own slug, and as a PDF", async ({ page }) => {
  await signIn(page, world, "finance");
  // RECORD level, under a valid slug: the streamed not-found contract.
  await expectRefused(page, `/${world.proOrgSlug}/invoices/${world.otherInvoiceId}`);
  // WORKSPACE level: a slug the caller cannot see is a literal 404.
  await expectRefusedAtWorkspace(page, `/${world.otherOrgSlug}/invoices/${world.otherInvoiceId}`);
  // The PDF route handler is not a page: a literal 404, never a 403.

});
```

```ts
// ===========================================================================
// INVOICES PART 1: the four new pages (five renders -- a draft and an issued
// invoice are different renders of one route), scanned as the FINANCE role,
// because a Manager holds no view_finance and would scan a not-found page.
// ===========================================================================
const INVOICE_PAGES: [string, (w: E2eWorld) => string][] = [
  ["invoices list", (w) => `/${w.proOrgSlug}/invoices`],
  ["new invoice (the line editor)", (w) => `/${w.proOrgSlug}/invoices/new`],
  ["a draft invoice (editor, issue, delete)", (w) => `/${w.proOrgSlug}/invoices/${w.proDraftInvoiceId}`],
  ["an issued invoice (view, download, send, paid, void)", (w) => `/${w.proOrgSlug}/invoices/${w.proIssuedInvoiceId}`],
  ["invoice settings (fresh credential, so the form, not the prompt)", (w) => `/${w.proOrgSlug}/settings/invoicing`],
];

for (const [label, url] of INVOICE_PAGES) {
  test(`${label} has no automated accessibility violations`, async ({ page }) => {
    await signIn(page, world, "finance");
    await page.goto(url(world));
    await settled(page);
    await scan(page, label);
  });
}
```

### Task 15: The record — SECURITY.md, CLAUDE.md, the decisions queue — and the final gate run

- [ ] **Step 1: The decisions queue — Q248, Q249, Q250**

Append to the decisions queue:
```markdown
## Q248 -- Invoicing enters scope (founder 2026-10-20), part 1 built on `invoicing`

- **Q248 -- Scope change, by founder decision 2026-10-20 (recorded, not a question).** Until now finance was "budget & actuals only, no invoicing" (CLAUDE.md § Scope discipline). The founder reversed that: workspaces generate invoices, and later take card payment through their own linked Stripe account. Three sub-projects, each with its own spec, plan and branch, built in order: (1) **Invoices** -- draft, issue, number, PDF, email, mark paid, void, pull lines from event finance; bank transfer only (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-invoices-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-20-invoices.md`); (2) **Stripe Connect** -- link an existing Standard account (OAuth), a "Pay by card" Checkout link as a direct charge carrying a percentage application fee, a webhook marks the invoice paid; (3) **Network delivery** -- an invoice to a connected network organisation also appears inside that organisation's GetSetPaid. The answers that bind all three:

  | Question | Answer |
  |---|---|
  | Timing | Built in parallel with Phase 10 |
  | Recipient | Any CRM company/contact by email, and (part 3) connected network orgs in-app |
  | Platform fee | A small percentage per Stripe payment (part 2; rate to DECISIONS-QUEUE with a default) |
  | Connect type | Link an existing Stripe account (Standard) |
  | Line items | Free lines, plus "pull from event finance" |
  | Tier | Pro only |
  | Without Stripe | Invoicing works; bank details on the invoice; marked paid by hand |
  | Approach | Our own invoices (A); Stripe only takes the payment |

  Part 1's own defaults where the spec was silent are listed in the plan's "Decisions this plan takes" section (no client write grants; a recipient address column; a draft may have no recipient; `paid_on` as a date; "remaining lines" = lines with no pulled entry; no finance link for a deleted event; the bank-change email on the delivery tick; drafts unaudited; numbers never truncate; UK-local issue date). Task 1 spike result: <the measured outcome from Task 1's report: dev-server render, `serverExternalPackages` needed or not, Brevo attachment status or "owed: no local key">.

## Q249 -- Invoices part 1: a contact's erasure and the drafts that name it, default implemented

- **Q249 -- Should a draft invoice be soft-deleted when its recipient is deleted?** The spec says a data-subject erasure of a contact "soft-deletes drafts that name it". No code path erases a contact: a contact's request is routed to the workspace (Q132), which soft-deletes the record -- reversibly -- and a merge retires a duplicate the same way, so auto-deleting drafts would destroy work on a restore or a merge. **Default (built): drafts are left alone; `issue_invoice` refuses a deleted recipient (`invoice_recipient_deleted`) and the draft stays editable; issued snapshots are never touched (legal-obligation basis).** Measured: `rls_invoices.sql` IS19/IS20. Revisit if a real contact-erasure engine is ever built.

## Q250 -- Invoices part 1: how long an invoice is kept, default implemented

- **Q250 -- Retention class of an invoice.** The spec says an issued, paid or void invoice is a financial record kept 7 years. Today `financial_entries` is `retained_with_workspace` and nothing deletes a financial record on a clock (Q180 is still open). **Default (built): the four invoice tables are classified `cascades` -- they live exactly as long as the workspace, and an issued invoice cannot be deleted by its workspace at all (the freeze trigger).** Classifying `invoices` as `retained_with_workspace` instead would require `/legal/data-retention` to name invoices (rls_phase8_retention.sql RL4 binds that class to the page), which is a new published legal version and waits for counsel. When Q180 is answered, invoices take the same seven-year class as financial entries.
```
Replace the angle-bracket sentence in Q248 with Task 1's actual measured outcome before committing (it is the one value only the executor knows).

- [ ] **Step 2: SECURITY.md**

1. § 2 (Tenancy and RLS), add a subsection:
```markdown
### Invoices (Invoices part 1)

Four tenant tables -- `invoice_settings`, `invoices`, `invoice_lines`, `invoice_sends` -- with RLS enabled and forced, `authenticated` holding SELECT only (rows to `view_finance`: Owner, Admin, Finance, at aal2; not tier-gated, so a downgraded workspace still reads and downloads), and `anon`/`service_role` nothing. **No client role can write any of them.** Every write is a `SECURITY DEFINER` RPC (`save_invoice_draft`, `delete_invoice_draft`, `issue_invoice`, `mark_invoice_paid`, `void_invoice`, `save_invoice_settings`, `record_invoice_send`, `audit_invoice_download`) that derives the workspace from the row or checks the caller's membership, and re-checks `manage_finance` and the Pro entitlement in the database. **An issued invoice is frozen for every role**, the table owner included: `private.invoices_freeze()` and `private.invoice_lines_freeze()` refuse any change to its content and admit only the issued→paid, issued/paid→void transitions, the erasure tombstone re-point, and the workspace purge's cascade (`rls_invoices.sql` § IF). Numbers are gap-free per workspace: `issue_invoice` locks the settings row (`invoice_issue_concurrency_probe.sh`). Nothing in either network view reads an invoice table (`network_leakage_phase6.sql` IN10).
```
2. § 7 (Audit logging), add: "**Invoices** write `invoice.issued`, `invoice.sent` (the number only -- the recipient's address stays in `invoice_sends`), `invoice.marked_paid`, `invoice.voided` (the reason stays out of the payload), `invoice.pdf_downloaded` (written before a byte of the PDF is rendered: no audit, no PDF), `invoice_settings.changed` (column names only) and `invoice_settings.bank_details_changed` (the last four digits of the account number, never the number). Draft saves and deletes are not audited: a draft is a working copy."
3. § 8 (Retention), add: "**Invoices** are classified `cascades` (Q250): they live as long as their workspace and an issued invoice cannot be deleted by it. An account erasure re-points `created_by`/`paid_by`/`voided_by`/`sent_by` at the tombstone and leaves the issued snapshot byte-identical (`rls_invoices.sql` § IE). A contact's soft-delete leaves the drafts naming it; they cannot be issued (Q249)."
4. § 10 (Known risk areas), add three bullets: **bank-detail fraud** (a changed account number redirects payment: step-up to save, an email to every Owner and Admin on the delivery tick, last-four audit); **outbound email to arbitrary addresses** (30/hour/user, the address validated, the send recorded only after Brevo accepts); **the PDF** (laid out from react-pdf primitives, not HTML; the filename is the constrained number; a void invoice prints a VOID banner; rendered on demand from the immutable snapshot and never stored).

2. **The gates table**, `pnpm test:e2e` row: add `invoices.spec.ts` to the list of golden-path specs with a one-sentence description (settings behind step-up → a draft from an event's income plus a free line → issue → PDF → paid → the income on the event's summary, net of VAT; the Starter refusal with `disabled` stripped; the keyboard-only line editor), the two tenant-breakout invoice tests, and **re-count** the axe-scan and total-test numbers from the suite (`npx playwright test --list | tail -1`) rather than adding by hand.
3. Add a section after "Network, as it actually shipped in Phase 6":
```markdown
**Invoices, as they actually shipped (part 1 of 3, founder 2026-10-20)** — draft, issue, PDF, email, mark paid, void, and "invoice these lines" from an event's finance. Things that read as gaps and are deliberate:

- **Bank transfer only.** Card payment through the workspace's own Stripe account is part 2; delivery into a connected network organisation is part 3. Each has its own spec.
- **No client write on any invoice table.** Every write is a `SECURITY DEFINER` RPC that re-checks `manage_finance` and Pro; an issued invoice is frozen for every role by trigger. Corrections are void-and-reissue: there are no credit notes, and a void keeps its number (the sequence never has a gap).
- **Income is booked net of VAT** on mark-paid: pulled `budgeted` lines flip to `actual`, the free lines' net becomes one new `actual` income line; void reverses exactly that. An invoice whose event was deleted is marked paid without touching finance.
- **The bank-change email is not instant.** It is queued on the existing delivery tick like every other notification (`lib/email/invoice-notifications.ts`).
- **A deleted recipient does not delete the drafts that name it** (Q249); the draft cannot be issued until another recipient is chosen.
- **Out of scope**: an invoices panel on company/contact pages, recurring invoices, reminders, multiple currencies, per-sender reply-to, a branded email.
```
4. **Scope discipline**: change "Finance starts at event/booking level and MVP scope is **budget & actuals only** — fee, cost and income lines per event, no invoicing, no full P&L." to "Finance starts at event/booking level: budget & actuals per event, and — since the founder's 2026-10-20 scope change (Q248) — **invoices** (part 1 shipped: bank transfer; part 2 Stripe Connect and part 3 network delivery follow, each with its own spec). Still no full P&L and no accounting-software features." Leave "settlements" in the Not-MVP list.
